# sqlvim v0.2 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 交付 sqlvim v0.2「写 SQL、拿结果」：textarea 控制台（`<Space>c`）+ `<Space>v` 用 nvim 编辑；分句并执行光标处语句、异步执行与 `Ctrl-c` 取消；全部格式的复制与导出；SQL 历史与草稿自动保存；基础保护（`readonly` 生效 + 数据库会话级只读兜底、缺失 WHERE 拦截）。另外补上 v0.1 推迟的四项：断线自动重连、`<Space>n` 新建连接表单、列表内 `/` 搜索、`zh`/`zl` 横向滚动。

**Architecture:** 在 v0.1 之上扩展。`db.Conn` 增加流式读取 `Stream` 和独占会话 `Session`（控制台用它保持会话状态并支持服务端取消：PG 发送 cancel request，MySQL 执行 `KILL QUERY`）。新增纯逻辑包：`sqlx`（词法、分句、语句分类、缺失 WHERE 检测）、`runner`（保护检查 + 依次执行语句）、`export`（tsv/csv/json/md/sql/xlsx 写出器）、`history`（嵌入式 SQLite：SQL 历史与草稿）。UI 新增控制台 tab（`panes/editor` 包装 Bubbles textarea + 结果表格 + 消息日志）和若干浮层（确认、输入、选择、列表选择器、连接表单）。所有 IO 仍在 `tea.Cmd` 中执行。

**Tech Stack:** v0.1 全部依赖，另加 charm.land/bubbles/v2 v2.2.1（textarea）· modernc.org/sqlite v1.60.1 · github.com/xuri/excelize/v2 v2.11.0 · github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn（ctxwatch，已在 pgx 内）

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-sqlvim-design.md`（§5.1、§5.3、§5.4、§5.5、§5.9、§7、§8、§10 v0.2 行）；上一版计划 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-13-sqlvim-v0.1.md`（v0.1 的约定在此继续有效）。

## Global Constraints

- v0.1 计划的 Global Constraints 全部继续有效（模块路径、XDG 目录、密码不落盘、日志不含密码、英文 UI 文案、UI 层不做 IO、Conventional Commits + attribution、集成测试 build tag `integration`）。
- 新增依赖只允许上面 Tech Stack 列出的三个，版本固定为：`charm.land/bubbles/v2 v2.2.1`、`modernc.org/sqlite v1.60.1`、`github.com/xuri/excelize/v2 v2.11.0`。
- `config.toml` 新增键：`max_rows`（默认 `1000`，必须为正）、`editor`（默认空：依次取 `$VISUAL`、`$EDITOR`、`nvim`）、`export_dir`（默认空：`~/Downloads`；支持 `~` 开头）。
- 状态库：`$XDG_STATE_HOME/sqlvim/state.db`（未设置时 `~/.local/state/sqlvim/state.db`），文件权限 `0600`；打开失败时退回内存库并记日志，**不退出**。
- 控制台查询没有超时，只能 `Ctrl-c` 取消；会话 ping 超时 5 秒；取消后最多等 5 秒（`cancelGrace`/`killGrace`），服务器仍不响应就丢弃该连接。
- `Ctrl-c` 不再退出程序：在控制台中取消正在运行的查询，否则在状态栏提示 `:qa to quit`。
- `readonly = true` 的连接：写语句（DML）和 DDL 在应用层被拒绝；另外 PG 连接设置 `default_transaction_read_only = on`，MySQL 连接设置 `transaction_read_only = 1` 兜底。v0.2 没有 `:set noreadonly`（v0.3 才有），解锁方法是改配置。
- UPDATE/DELETE 不带顶层 WHERE：弹确认框，必须输入表名才能继续（任何环境）。
- 自动重连只对只读操作（树加载、表数据分页、结构、整表导出）自动重试一次；控制台语句**从不**自动重试，只在下次执行前 ping，断了就重开会话。
- 导出默认文件名：`<表名或 query>-<YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS>.<扩展名>`，文件名中 `[A-Za-z0-9_.-]` 以外的字符替换为 `_`。
- `connections.toml` 由 `<Space>n` 追加写入：只追加 `[[connection]]` 块，不重写已有内容（保留注释）；新建文件权限 `0600`。

### v0.2 明确不做

VISUAL 模式与表格框选（复制范围在 v0.2 为整个结果集 / 当前单元格 / 当前行）、`env = prod` 确认框与红框、`:set noreadonly`、手动事务模式（v0.3）；错误定位跳转到编辑器行列（v0.4 与补全一起做）；补全、高亮（v0.4）。

## Review Focus

1. **分号出现在字符串、注释、标识符、PG dollar-quote、MySQL `DELIMITER` 块里** → 分句正确，执行光标处语句时跑的就是光标所在那条（Task 2 测试）。
2. **绕过只读的语句写法**：小写、前导注释 `/* x */ delete …`、`WITH d AS (DELETE …) SELECT …`、`EXPLAIN ANALYZE DELETE …`、`SELECT … INTO` → 都被识别为写语句；应用层漏判时数据库会话级只读兜底（Task 3 测试、Task 5 合约测试）。
3. **取消长查询、查询巨大结果集**（`SELECT SLEEP(30)`、上千万行表） → UI 不卡，`max_rows` 限制内存，取消后会话仍可用（临时表还在）（Task 5 合约测试、Task 16、Task 17 测试）。
4. **导出/复制的值含制表符、换行、引号、`|`、反斜杠、NULL 与空串、MySQL 二进制 `0x…`、CJK** → 每种格式都能正确往返；JSON/Markdown/SQL 区分 NULL 与空串（TSV/CSV 中两者都是空字段，这是格式本身的限制）（Task 8、Task 9 测试）。
5. **连接中途断开**（服务器重启、笔记本休眠） → 下一次只读操作自动重连一次；控制台不重复执行写语句，下次执行前自动重开会话；重连失败时树中该连接标红，按 `Enter` 重试（Task 21 测试）。

```go
func TestNewSettingsDefaultsAndValidation(t *testing.T) {
	if got := DefaultSettings().MaxRows; got != 1000 {
		t.Fatalf("MaxRows default = %d", got)
	}
	dir := t.TempDir()
	writeFile(t, dir, "config.toml", "max_rows = 50\neditor = \"code -w\"\nexport_dir = \"~/out\"\n")
	cfg, err := Load(dir)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s := cfg.Settings
	if s.MaxRows != 50 || s.Editor != "code -w" || s.ExportDir != "~/out" {
		t.Fatalf("settings = %+v", s)
	}
	writeFile(t, dir, "config.toml", "max_rows = 0\n")
	if _, err := Load(dir); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "max_rows") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestEditorCommand(t *testing.T) {
	env := map[string]string{}
	getenv := func(k string) string { return env[k] }
	if got := (Settings{}).EditorCommand(getenv); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"nvim"}) {
		t.Fatalf("fallback = %v", got)
	}
	env["EDITOR"] = "vim"
	if got := (Settings{}).EditorCommand(getenv); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"vim"}) {
		t.Fatalf("EDITOR = %v", got)
	}
	env["VISUAL"] = "hx"
	if got := (Settings{}).EditorCommand(getenv); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"hx"}) {
		t.Fatalf("VISUAL wins over EDITOR: %v", got)
	}
	if got := (Settings{Editor: "code -w"}).EditorCommand(getenv); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"code", "-w"}) {
		t.Fatalf("setting wins: %v", got)
	}
}

func TestExportDirectory(t *testing.T) {
	cases := map[string]string{
		"":          "/home/u/Downloads",
		"~":         "/home/u",
		"~/out":     "/home/u/out",
		"/tmp/x":    "/tmp/x",
		"relative":  "relative",
	}
	for in, want := range cases {
		if got := (Settings{ExportDir: in}).ExportDirectory("/home/u"); got != want {
			t.Errorf("ExportDirectory(%q) = %q, want %q", in, got, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestNormalize(t *testing.T) {
	c, err := Normalize(Connection{Name: "x", Driver: "postgres"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if c.Port != 5432 || c.Host != "localhost" || c.Env != EnvDev {
		t.Fatalf("defaults not applied: %+v", c)
	}
	for _, bad := range []Connection{
		{Driver: "mysql"},
		{Name: "x", Driver: "sqlite"},
		{Name: "x", Driver: "mysql", Env: "qa"},
	} {
		if _, err := Normalize(bad); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("Normalize(%+v) succeeded", bad)
		}
	}
}

func TestAppendConnectionKeepsExistingContent(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	writeFile(t, dir, "connections.toml", "# my servers\n[[connection]]\nname = \"a\"\ndriver = \"mysql\"\n")
	got, err := AppendConnection(dir, Connection{Name: "b \"quoted\"", Driver: "postgres", User: "app", Database: "shop", Env: EnvProd})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got.Port != 5432 {
		t.Fatalf("returned connection is normalized: %+v", got)
	}
	data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(dir, "connections.toml"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !strings.HasPrefix(string(data), "# my servers\n") {
		t.Fatalf("existing content rewritten:\n%s", data)
	}
	cfg, err := Load(dir)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("file no longer loads: %v\n%s", err, data)
	}
	if len(cfg.Connections) != 2 || cfg.Connections[1].Name != `b "quoted"` || cfg.Connections[1].Env != EnvProd {
		t.Fatalf("connections = %+v", cfg.Connections)
	}
	if _, err := AppendConnection(dir, Connection{Name: "a", Driver: "mysql"}); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "already exists") {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestAppendConnectionCreatesPrivateFile(t *testing.T) {
	dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "new", "sqlvim")
	if _, err := AppendConnection(dir, Connection{Name: "a", Driver: "mysql"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	info, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(dir, "connections.toml"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if perm := info.Mode().Perm(); perm != 0o600 {
		t.Fatalf("perm = %o", perm)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

修改 `internal/config/config.go`：

```go
// Settings is config.toml.
type Settings struct {
	PageSize       int    `toml:"page_size"`
	MaxColumnWidth int    `toml:"max_column_width"`
	MaxRows        int    `toml:"max_rows"`
	Editor         string `toml:"editor"`
	ExportDir      string `toml:"export_dir"`
}

// DefaultSettings returns the settings used when config.toml omits a key.
func DefaultSettings() Settings {
	return Settings{PageSize: 200, MaxColumnWidth: 40, MaxRows: 1000}
}

// EditorCommand is the command line <space>v runs: the editor setting, then
// $VISUAL, then $EDITOR, then nvim.
func (s Settings) EditorCommand(getenv func(string) string) []string {
	for _, v := range []string{s.Editor, getenv("VISUAL"), getenv("EDITOR")} {
		if f := strings.Fields(v); len(f) > 0 {
			return f
		}
	}
	return []string{"nvim"}
}

// ExportDirectory returns export_dir with a leading ~ expanded, defaulting to ~/Downloads.
func (s Settings) ExportDirectory(home string) string {
	switch d := s.ExportDir; {
	case d == "":
		return filepath.Join(home, "Downloads")
	case d == "~":
		return home
	case strings.HasPrefix(d, "~/"):
		return filepath.Join(home, d[2:])
	default:
		return d
	}
}
```

把 `normalize` 拆成单个连接的 `Normalize`：

```go
// Normalize validates one connection and fills in defaults (host, port, env).
func Normalize(c Connection) (Connection, error) {
	if c.Name == "" {
		return c, errors.New("connection: name is required")
	}
	port, ok := defaultPorts[c.Driver]
	if !ok {
		return c, fmt.Errorf("connection %q: unknown driver %q (want mysql or postgres)", c.Name, c.Driver)
	}
	if c.Port == 0 {
		c.Port = port
	}
	if c.Host == "" {
		c.Host = "localhost"
	}
	switch c.Env {
	case "":
		c.Env = EnvDev
	case EnvDev, EnvStaging, EnvProd:
	default:
		return c, fmt.Errorf("connection %q: unknown env %q (want dev, staging or prod)", c.Name, c.Env)
	}
	return c, nil
}

func normalize(in []Connection) ([]Connection, error) {
	seen := make(map[string]bool, len(in))
	out := make([]Connection, 0, len(in))
	for i, c := range in {
		if c.Name == "" {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("connection #%d: name is required", i+1)
		}
		if seen[c.Name] {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("connection %q: duplicate name", c.Name)
		}
		seen[c.Name] = true
		c, err := Normalize(c)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out = append(out, c)
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

追加连接（新文件内容由 go-toml 生成，保证转义正确）：

```go
// connBlock is what <space>n writes; empty optional keys are left out.
type connBlock struct {
	Name     string `toml:"name"`
	Driver   string `toml:"driver"`
	Host     string `toml:"host"`
	Port     int    `toml:"port"`
	User     string `toml:"user,omitempty"`
	Database string `toml:"database,omitempty"`
	Env      Env    `toml:"env"`
}

// AppendConnection validates c and appends it to dir/connections.toml as a new
// [[connection]] block, leaving existing content (and comments) untouched.
func AppendConnection(dir string, c Connection) (Connection, error) {
	c, err := Normalize(c)
	if err != nil {
		return c, err
	}
	cfg, err := Load(dir)
	if err != nil {
		return c, err
	}
	for _, e := range cfg.Connections {
		if e.Name == c.Name {
			return c, fmt.Errorf("connection %q already exists", c.Name)
		}
	}
	block, err := toml.Marshal(struct {
		Connection []connBlock `toml:"connection"`
	}{[]connBlock{{
		Name: c.Name, Driver: c.Driver, Host: c.Host, Port: c.Port,
		User: c.User, Database: c.Database, Env: c.Env,
	}}})
	if err != nil {
		return c, err
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o700); err != nil {
		return c, err
	}
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "connections.toml")
	f, err := os.OpenFile(path, os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		return c, err
	}
	defer func() { _ = f.Close() }()
	if info, err := f.Stat(); err == nil && info.Size() > 0 {
		block = append([]byte("\n"), block...)
	}
	if _, err := f.Write(block); err != nil {
		return c, err
	}
	return c, f.Close()
}
```

### Task 2: sqlx 词法与分句

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/sqlx/lex.go`, `internal/sqlx/split.go`
- Test: `internal/sqlx/split_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `type Dialect int`，常量 `MySQL`、`Postgres`；`func DialectOf(driver string) Dialect`
  - `type Statement struct { Text string; Start, End int }`（`Text` 不含结尾分隔符，已去掉右侧空白；`Start`/`End` 是 `Text` 在原文中的字节偏移，`End` 不含）
  - `func Split(src string, d Dialect) []Statement`
  - `func At(stmts []Statement, offset int) (Statement, bool)`
  - 包内：`token{kind tokKind; text string; pos, end int}`、`tokWord`/`tokIdent`/`tokString`/`tokPunct`/`tokComment`、`func lex(src string, i int, d Dialect) token`、`func codeTokens(src string, d Dialect) []token`（Task 3 使用）

**词法规则：**

| 结构 | MySQL | PostgreSQL |
|---|---|---|
| `'…'` | 字符串，`''` 与 `\x` 转义 | 字符串，只有 `''` 转义 |
| `E'…'` | — | 字符串，`\x` 转义 |
| `"…"` | 字符串，`\x` 转义 | 标识符，`""` 转义 |
| `` `…` `` | 标识符，``` `` ``` 转义 | — |
| `$tag$…$tag$` | — | 字符串（tag 为空或 `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*`） |
| `-- …` | 注释（`--` 后必须是空白或结尾） | 注释 |
| `# …` | 注释 | — |
| `/* … */` | 注释 | 注释，可嵌套 |
| `DELIMITER x` | 语句开头的整行指令，改变分隔符 | — |

未闭合的字符串/注释一直延伸到文本末尾。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试** `internal/sqlx/split_test.go`

```go
package sqlx

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/go-cmp/cmp"
)

func texts(stmts []Statement) []string {
	out := []string{}
	for _, s := range stmts {
		out = append(out, s.Text)
	}
	return out
}

func TestSplit(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		name string
		d    Dialect
		src  string
		want []string
	}{
		{"basic", MySQL, "select 1; select 2", []string{"select 1", "select 2"}},
		{"trailing delimiter and blanks", Postgres, "select 1;\n\n  ;\n", []string{"select 1"}},
		{"semicolon in string", MySQL, "select ';' as a; select 2;", []string{"select ';' as a", "select 2"}},
		{"mysql backslash escape", MySQL, `select 'it\'s; ok'; select 2`, []string{`select 'it\'s; ok'`, "select 2"}},
		{"pg doubled quote", Postgres, "select 'it''s; ok'; select 2", []string{"select 'it''s; ok'", "select 2"}},
		{"pg backslash is literal", Postgres, `select 'a\'; select 2`, []string{`select 'a\'`, "select 2"}},
		{"pg E string", Postgres, `select E'a\';b'; select 2`, []string{`select E'a\';b'`, "select 2"}},
		{"pg dollar quotes", Postgres, "select $$a;b$$; select $f$x;$f$; select 3",
			[]string{"select $$a;b$$", "select $f$x;$f$", "select 3"}},
		{"pg positional param is not a dollar quote", Postgres, "select $1; select 2", []string{"select $1", "select 2"}},
		{"line comment", Postgres, "select 1 -- ; x\n; select 2", []string{"select 1 -- ; x", "select 2"}},
		{"mysql -- needs a space", MySQL, "select 1--1; select 2", []string{"select 1--1", "select 2"}},
		{"mysql hash comment", MySQL, "select 1 # ; x\n; select 2", []string{"select 1 # ; x", "select 2"}},
		{"block comment", MySQL, "/* a; b */ select 1;", []string{"/* a; b */ select 1"}},
		{"pg nested block comment", Postgres, "/* a /* b; */ c; */ select 1", []string{"/* a /* b; */ c; */ select 1"}},
		{"comment-only statements are dropped", Postgres, "-- note\n;\nselect 1;\n-- trailing", []string{"select 1"}},
		{"backtick identifier", MySQL, "select `a;b` from t; select 2", []string{"select `a;b` from t", "select 2"}},
		{"pg quoted identifier", Postgres, `select "a;b" from t; select 2`, []string{`select "a;b" from t`, "select 2"}},
		{"mysql double-quoted string", MySQL, `select "a;\"b"; select 2`, []string{`select "a;\"b"`, "select 2"}},
		{"unterminated string runs to the end", MySQL, "select 'abc; select 2", []string{"select 'abc; select 2"}},
		{"delimiter blocks", MySQL,
			"DELIMITER //\nCREATE PROCEDURE p() BEGIN SELECT 1; SELECT 2; END//\ndelimiter ;\nselect 3;",
			[]string{"CREATE PROCEDURE p() BEGIN SELECT 1; SELECT 2; END", "select 3"}},
		{"delimiter is only a directive at statement start", MySQL, "select delimiter from t; select 2",
			[]string{"select delimiter from t", "select 2"}},
		{"cjk", Postgres, "select '李雷;'; select 2", []string{"select '李雷;'", "select 2"}},
		{"empty", MySQL, "", []string{}},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			if diff := cmp.Diff(c.want, texts(Split(c.src, c.d))); diff != "" {
				t.Fatalf("(-want +got):\n%s", diff)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestSplitOffsets(t *testing.T) {
	src := "  select 1 ;\n select 2"
	got := Split(src, MySQL)
	want := []Statement{{Text: "select 1", Start: 2, End: 10}, {Text: "select 2", Start: 14, End: 22}}
	if diff := cmp.Diff(want, got); diff != "" {
		t.Fatalf("(-want +got):\n%s", diff)
	}
	for _, s := range got {
		if src[s.Start:s.End] != s.Text {
			t.Errorf("offsets of %q point at %q", s.Text, src[s.Start:s.End])
		}
	}
}

func TestAt(t *testing.T) {
	src := "select 1;\n\nselect 2;\nselect 3"
	stmts := Split(src, MySQL)
	cases := map[int]string{
		0:            "select 1",
		8:            "select 1", // on the ;
		10:           "select 1", // blank line after it
		11:           "select 2",
		len(src):     "select 3",
		len(src) + 5: "select 3",
	}
	for off, want := range cases {
		got, ok := At(stmts, off)
		if !ok || got.Text != want {
			t.Errorf("At(%d) = %q, %v; want %q", off, got.Text, ok, want)
		}
	}
	if _, ok := At(nil, 0); ok {
		t.Error("At on no statements")
	}
}

func TestDialectOf(t *testing.T) {
	if DialectOf("postgres") != Postgres || DialectOf("mysql") != MySQL {
		t.Fatal("DialectOf")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** `internal/sqlx/lex.go`

```go
// Package sqlx holds SQL text utilities: lexing, statement splitting and
// statement classification. It never talks to a database.
package sqlx

import (
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf8"
)

// Dialect selects the lexical rules of one engine.
type Dialect int

const (
	MySQL Dialect = iota
	Postgres
)

// DialectOf maps a driver name ("mysql", "postgres") to its dialect.
func DialectOf(driver string) Dialect {
	if driver == "postgres" {
		return Postgres
	}
	return MySQL
}

type tokKind int

const (
	tokWord    tokKind = iota // keyword or bare identifier
	tokIdent                  // quoted identifier; text is unquoted
	tokString                 // any string literal; text is the raw source
	tokPunct                  // one character
	tokComment                // -- # /* */
)

type token struct {
	kind     tokKind
	text     string
	pos, end int
}

func isSpace(c byte) bool { return c == ' ' || c == '\t' || c == '\n' || c == '\r' || c == '\f' || c == '\v' }

func isWordByte(c byte) bool {
	return c == '_' || c == '$' || c >= 0x80 ||
		c >= 'a' && c <= 'z' || c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z' || c >= '0' && c <= '9'
}

// lex reads the token starting at src[i]; src[i] must not be whitespace.
func lex(src string, i int, d Dialect) token {
	c := src[i]
	switch {
	case c == '-' && strings.HasPrefix(src[i:], "--") && (d == Postgres || i+2 >= len(src) || isSpace(src[i+2])):
		return lineComment(src, i)
	case c == '#' && d == MySQL:
		return lineComment(src, i)
	case c == '/' && strings.HasPrefix(src[i:], "/*"):
		return blockComment(src, i, d == Postgres)
	case c == '\'':
		return quoted(src, i, '\'', tokString, d == MySQL)
	case (c == 'E' || c == 'e') && d == Postgres && i+1 < len(src) && src[i+1] == '\'':
		t := quoted(src, i+1, '\'', tokString, true)
		t.pos = i
		return t
	case c == '"' && d == MySQL:
		return quoted(src, i, '"', tokString, true)
	case c == '"':
		return quoted(src, i, '"', tokIdent, false)
	case c == '`' && d == MySQL:
		return quoted(src, i, '`', tokIdent, false)
	case c == '$' && d == Postgres:
		if t, ok := dollar(src, i); ok {
			return t
		}
		return token{kind: tokPunct, text: "$", pos: i, end: i + 1}
	case isWordByte(c):
		j := i
		for j < len(src) && isWordByte(src[j]) {
			j++
		}
		return token{kind: tokWord, text: src[i:j], pos: i, end: j}
	}
	_, size := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(src[i:])
	return token{kind: tokPunct, text: src[i : i+size], pos: i, end: i + size}
}

func lineComment(src string, i int) token {
	end := strings.IndexByte(src[i:], '\n')
	if end < 0 {
		end = len(src)
	} else {
		end += i
	}
	return token{kind: tokComment, text: src[i:end], pos: i, end: end}
}

func blockComment(src string, i int, nested bool) token {
	depth := 0
	j := i
	for j < len(src) {
		switch {
		case strings.HasPrefix(src[j:], "/*") && (nested || depth == 0):
			depth++
			j += 2
		case strings.HasPrefix(src[j:], "*/"):
			depth--
			j += 2
			if depth == 0 {
				return token{kind: tokComment, text: src[i:j], pos: i, end: j}
			}
		default:
			j++
		}
	}
	return token{kind: tokComment, text: src[i:], pos: i, end: len(src)}
}

// quoted reads a q-delimited token; a doubled q is an escaped q, and with
// backslash a backslash escapes the next byte.
func quoted(src string, i int, q byte, kind tokKind, backslash bool) token {
	var b strings.Builder
	j := i + 1
	for j < len(src) {
		c := src[j]
		switch {
		case backslash && c == '\\' && j+1 < len(src):
			b.WriteByte(c)
			b.WriteByte(src[j+1])
			j += 2
			continue
		case c == q && j+1 < len(src) && src[j+1] == q:
			b.WriteByte(q)
			j += 2
			continue
		case c == q:
			j++
			return finishQuoted(src, i, j, kind, b.String())
		}
		b.WriteByte(c)
		j++
	}
	return finishQuoted(src, i, len(src), kind, b.String())
}

func finishQuoted(src string, i, end int, kind tokKind, inner string) token {
	if kind == tokIdent {
		return token{kind: kind, text: inner, pos: i, end: end}
	}
	return token{kind: kind, text: src[i:end], pos: i, end: end}
}

// dollar reads a PostgreSQL $tag$...$tag$ string.
func dollar(src string, i int) (token, bool) {
	j := i + 1
	for j < len(src) && (isWordByte(src[j]) && src[j] != '$') {
		j++
	}
	if j >= len(src) || src[j] != '$' {
		return token{}, false
	}
	tag := src[i : j+1]
	if len(tag) > 2 && tag[1] >= '0' && tag[1] <= '9' {
		return token{}, false
	}
	body := j + 1
	if k := strings.Index(src[body:], tag); k >= 0 {
		end := body + k + len(tag)
		return token{kind: tokString, text: src[i:end], pos: i, end: end}, true
	}
	return token{kind: tokString, text: src[i:], pos: i, end: len(src)}, true
}

// codeTokens returns the tokens of src without comments.
func codeTokens(src string, d Dialect) []token {
	var out []token
	for i := 0; i < len(src); {
		if isSpace(src[i]) {
			i++
			continue
		}
		t := lex(src, i, d)
		if t.kind != tokComment {
			out = append(out, t)
		}
		i = t.end
	}
	return out
}
```

注意 `$1`：`dollar` 读到 `$1` 后面不是 `$`（或 tag 以数字开头）返回 false，`$` 作为标点，`1` 作为单词。

`internal/sqlx/split.go`：

```go
package sqlx

import (
	"strings"
	"unicode"
)

// Statement is one SQL statement inside a larger text.
type Statement struct {
	Text       string // source text without the trailing delimiter
	Start, End int    // byte offsets of Text in the source; End is exclusive
}

// Split cuts src into statements. Delimiters inside strings, quoted
// identifiers and comments are ignored; MySQL DELIMITER lines change the
// delimiter. Statements that contain only comments are dropped.
func Split(src string, d Dialect) []Statement {
	var out []Statement
	delim := ";"
	start := -1     // start of the current statement; -1 between statements
	hasCode := false // the current statement has a non-comment token
	flush := func(end int) {
		if start >= 0 && hasCode {
			text := strings.TrimRightFunc(src[start:end], unicode.IsSpace)
			out = append(out, Statement{Text: text, Start: start, End: start + len(text)})
		}
		start, hasCode = -1, false
	}
	for i := 0; i < len(src); {
		if isSpace(src[i]) {
			i++
			continue
		}
		if d == MySQL && start < 0 {
			if nd, next, ok := delimiterDirective(src, i); ok {
				delim, i = nd, next
				continue
			}
		}
		if strings.HasPrefix(src[i:], delim) {
			flush(i)
			i += len(delim)
			continue
		}
		t := lex(src, i, d)
		if start < 0 {
			start = i
		}
		if t.kind != tokComment {
			hasCode = true
		}
		i = t.end
	}
	flush(len(src))
	return out
}

// delimiterDirective parses a "DELIMITER x" line starting at i.
func delimiterDirective(src string, i int) (delim string, next int, ok bool) {
	end := strings.IndexByte(src[i:], '\n')
	if end < 0 {
		end = len(src)
	} else {
		end += i
	}
	f := strings.Fields(src[i:end])
	if len(f) != 2 || !strings.EqualFold(f[0], "DELIMITER") {
		return "", 0, false
	}
	return f[1], end, true
}

// At returns the statement under offset: the last one starting at or before
// it, or the first one when offset precedes them all.
func At(stmts []Statement, offset int) (Statement, bool) {
	if len(stmts) == 0 {
		return Statement{}, false
	}
	best := stmts[0]
	for _, s := range stmts {
		if s.Start <= offset {
			best = s
		}
	}
	return best, true
}
```

### Task 3: sqlx 语句分类

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/sqlx/analyze.go`
- Test: `internal/sqlx/analyze_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 的 `codeTokens`、`token`、`tokWord`/`tokIdent`/`tokPunct`。
- Produces:
  - `type Kind int`，常量 `Other`、`Read`、`Write`、`DDL`、`Tx`；`func (k Kind) String() string`
  - `type Info struct { Kind Kind; ReturnsRows bool; MissingWhere bool; Target string }`
  - `func Analyze(stmt string, d Dialect) Info`

**规则（关键字大小写不敏感；字符串、引号标识符、注释里的词不算关键字）：**

- 跳过开头的 `(`，取第一个单词：
  - `SELECT`：顶层（括号深度 0）有 `INTO` → `Write`；否则 `Read` + `ReturnsRows`，`Target` 为单表查询的表名（`FROM` 后只有一张表、无 `JOIN`/逗号）。`FOR UPDATE` 仍是 `Read`。
  - `SHOW`/`DESCRIBE`/`DESC`/`VALUES`/`TABLE` → `Read` + `ReturnsRows`。
  - `EXPLAIN` → `Read` + `ReturnsRows`；若含 `ANALYZE` 且后面有 DML 关键字 → `Write`，并继承该 DML 的 `MissingWhere`/`Target`。
  - `INSERT`/`REPLACE`（`Target` 为 `INTO` 后的表，无 `INTO` 时为紧跟的表）、`UPDATE`、`DELETE`（`Target` 为 `FROM` 后的表）、`MERGE`（`INTO` 后） → `Write`；顶层有 `RETURNING` → `ReturnsRows`。
  - `UPDATE`/`DELETE` 在自身括号层级内没有 `WHERE` → `MissingWhere`。
  - `WITH` → 以顶层第一个 `SELECT`/`INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE`/`MERGE`/`REPLACE`/`VALUES`/`TABLE` 为主语句分类；任何 CTE 里出现 DML → `Write`；CTE 内的 `UPDATE`/`DELETE` 缺 WHERE 也算 `MissingWhere`。
  - `CALL` → `Write` + `ReturnsRows`；`LOAD`/`COPY`/`DO` → `Write`。
  - `CREATE`/`ALTER`/`DROP`/`TRUNCATE`/`RENAME`/`COMMENT`/`GRANT`/`REVOKE`/`REINDEX`/`CLUSTER`/`VACUUM` → `DDL`。
  - `BEGIN`/`START`/`COMMIT`/`ROLLBACK`/`SAVEPOINT`/`RELEASE`/`END`/`ABORT` → `Tx`。
  - 其他（`SET`、`USE`、`ANALYZE` …）→ `Other`。
- `Target` 是表名路径（`a.b.c`）的最后一段，去掉引号；无法确定时为空串。
- `UPDATE`/`DELETE` 前面紧跟 `FOR`/`ON`/`KEY` 时不是 DML（`FOR UPDATE`、`ON DELETE CASCADE`、`ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE`）。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试** `internal/sqlx/analyze_test.go`

```go
package sqlx

import "testing"

func TestAnalyze(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		d    Dialect
		sql  string
		want Info
	}{
		{MySQL, "select * from users", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true, Target: "users"}},
		{Postgres, "SELECT * FROM app.users u WHERE id = 1", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true, Target: "users"}},
		{MySQL, "select a, b from t order by a, b", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "select * from a join b on a.id = b.id", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{MySQL, "select * from a, b", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{MySQL, "select 1", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{MySQL, "select * from t for update", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "(select 1) union (select 2)", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{MySQL, "select 1 into @x", Info{Kind: Write}},
		{Postgres, "select * into new_t from t", Info{Kind: Write}},
		{MySQL, "show tables", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{MySQL, "desc users", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{Postgres, "values (1), (2)", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{Postgres, "explain select 1", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{Postgres, "explain (analyze) delete from t", Info{Kind: Write, ReturnsRows: true, MissingWhere: true, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "  /* hi */ delete from t", Info{Kind: Write, MissingWhere: true, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "DELETE FROM `order` WHERE id = 1", Info{Kind: Write, Target: "order"}},
		{MySQL, "delete low_priority quick from app.t limit 10", Info{Kind: Write, MissingWhere: true, Target: "t"}},
		{Postgres, `update "Users" set a = 1`, Info{Kind: Write, MissingWhere: true, Target: "Users"}},
		{Postgres, "update only t set a = 1 where id = 2", Info{Kind: Write, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "UPDATE t SET a = (SELECT 1 FROM x WHERE y = 1)", Info{Kind: Write, MissingWhere: true, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "update t set note = 'where'", Info{Kind: Write, MissingWhere: true, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "update t set note = 1 -- where id = 1", Info{Kind: Write, MissingWhere: true, Target: "t"}},
		{Postgres, "insert into s.t (a) values (1) returning id", Info{Kind: Write, ReturnsRows: true, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "insert ignore into t values (1) on duplicate key update a = 1", Info{Kind: Write, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "insert t values (1)", Info{Kind: Write, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "replace into t values (1)", Info{Kind: Write, Target: "t"}},
		{Postgres, "merge into t using s on t.id = s.id when matched then update set a = 1", Info{Kind: Write, Target: "t"}},
		{Postgres, "with d as (delete from t returning *) select * from d", Info{Kind: Write, ReturnsRows: true, MissingWhere: true, Target: "t"}},
		{Postgres, "with d as (delete from t where id = 1 returning *) select * from d", Info{Kind: Write, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{Postgres, "with x as (select 1) select * from x", Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{Postgres, "with recursive x(n) as (select 1 union all select n + 1 from x) delete from t", Info{Kind: Write, MissingWhere: true, Target: "t"}},
		{MySQL, "call p(1)", Info{Kind: Write, ReturnsRows: true}},
		{Postgres, "copy t from stdin", Info{Kind: Write}},
		{MySQL, "create table t (id int, p int references q (id) on delete cascade)", Info{Kind: DDL}},
		{MySQL, "DROP TABLE t", Info{Kind: DDL}},
		{MySQL, "truncate t", Info{Kind: DDL}},
		{Postgres, "begin", Info{Kind: Tx}},
		{MySQL, "start transaction", Info{Kind: Tx}},
		{Postgres, "set search_path = app", Info{Kind: Other}},
		{MySQL, "use app", Info{Kind: Other}},
		{MySQL, "", Info{Kind: Other}},
		{MySQL, "-- only a comment", Info{Kind: Other}},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		if got := Analyze(c.sql, c.d); got != c.want {
			t.Errorf("Analyze(%q)\n got  %+v\n want %+v", c.sql, got, c.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestKindString(t *testing.T) {
	for k, want := range map[Kind]string{Other: "other", Read: "read", Write: "write", DDL: "ddl", Tx: "transaction"} {
		if k.String() != want {
			t.Errorf("%d = %q", k, k.String())
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** `internal/sqlx/analyze.go`

```go
package sqlx

import "strings"

// Kind classifies a statement for the safety checks.
type Kind int

const (
	Other Kind = iota
	Read
	Write // DML, CALL, COPY, SELECT ... INTO
	DDL
	Tx
)

func (k Kind) String() string {
	return [...]string{"other", "read", "write", "ddl", "transaction"}[k]
}

// Info describes one statement.
type Info struct {
	Kind         Kind
	ReturnsRows  bool   // run it as a query rather than an exec
	MissingWhere bool   // an UPDATE or DELETE without a WHERE clause
	Target       string // the table written to (or read by a single-table SELECT)
}

func wordSet(ws ...string) map[string]bool {
	m := make(map[string]bool, len(ws))
	for _, w := range ws {
		m[w] = true
	}
	return m
}

var (
	readWords  = wordSet("SHOW", "DESCRIBE", "DESC", "VALUES", "TABLE")
	writeWords = wordSet("LOAD", "COPY", "DO")
	ddlWords   = wordSet("CREATE", "ALTER", "DROP", "TRUNCATE", "RENAME", "COMMENT", "GRANT", "REVOKE", "REINDEX", "CLUSTER", "VACUUM")
	txWords    = wordSet("BEGIN", "START", "COMMIT", "ROLLBACK", "SAVEPOINT", "RELEASE", "END", "ABORT")
	dmlWords   = wordSet("INSERT", "UPDATE", "DELETE", "REPLACE", "MERGE")
	mainWords  = wordSet("SELECT", "INSERT", "UPDATE", "DELETE", "MERGE", "REPLACE", "VALUES", "TABLE")
	modifiers  = wordSet("LOW_PRIORITY", "HIGH_PRIORITY", "DELAYED", "QUICK", "IGNORE", "ONLY")
	notDMLPrev = wordSet("FOR", "ON", "KEY")
	clauseEnd  = wordSet("WHERE", "GROUP", "ORDER", "LIMIT", "HAVING", "UNION", "WINDOW", "FOR", "OFFSET", "FETCH", "INTERSECT", "EXCEPT")
)

// Analyze classifies one statement (as produced by Split).
func Analyze(stmt string, d Dialect) Info {
	toks := codeTokens(stmt, d)
	i := 0
	for i < len(toks) && isPunct(toks[i], "(") {
		i++
	}
	return classify(toks, i)
}

func classify(toks []token, i int) Info {
	if i >= len(toks) || toks[i].kind != tokWord {
		return Info{}
	}
	switch first := upper(toks[i]); {
	case first == "WITH":
		return analyzeWith(toks, i)
	case first == "EXPLAIN":
		info := Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}
		if k := indexWord(toks, i+1, "ANALYZE"); k >= 0 {
			if m := indexIn(toks, k+1, dmlWords); m >= 0 {
				inner := classify(toks, m)
				info.Kind, info.MissingWhere, info.Target = Write, inner.MissingWhere, inner.Target
			}
		}
		return info
	case first == "SELECT":
		if depth0Word(toks, i, "INTO") {
			return Info{Kind: Write}
		}
		return Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true, Target: selectTarget(toks, i)}
	case readWords[first]:
		return Info{Kind: Read, ReturnsRows: true}
	case dmlWords[first]:
		return dml(toks, i, first)
	case first == "CALL":
		return Info{Kind: Write, ReturnsRows: true}
	case writeWords[first]:
		return Info{Kind: Write}
	case ddlWords[first]:
		return Info{Kind: DDL}
	case txWords[first]:
		return Info{Kind: Tx}
	}
	return Info{}
}

func dml(toks []token, i int, kw string) Info {
	info := Info{Kind: Write, ReturnsRows: depth0Word(toks, i, "RETURNING")}
	switch kw {
	case "INSERT", "REPLACE", "MERGE":
		k := indexWord(toks, i+1, "INTO")
		if k < 0 {
			k = i
		}
		info.Target = identAfter(toks, k)
	case "UPDATE":
		info.Target = identAfter(toks, i)
		info.MissingWhere = missingWhere(toks, i)
	case "DELETE":
		if k := depth0Index(toks, i, "FROM"); k >= 0 {
			info.Target = identAfter(toks, k)
		}
		info.MissingWhere = missingWhere(toks, i)
	}
	return info
}

func analyzeWith(toks []token, i int) Info {
	depth := 0
	main := -1
	for j := i + 1; j < len(toks) && main < 0; j++ {
		depth += depthDelta(toks[j])
		if depth == 0 && toks[j].kind == tokWord && mainWords[upper(toks[j])] {
			main = j
		}
	}
	if main < 0 {
		return Info{}
	}
	info := classify(toks, main)
	if !dmlWords[upper(toks[main])] {
		info.Target = "" // FROM may name a CTE rather than a table
	}
	for j := i + 1; j < main; j++ {
		if !isDML(toks, j) {
			continue
		}
		info.Kind = Write
		if w := upper(toks[j]); (w == "UPDATE" || w == "DELETE") && missingWhere(toks, j) && !info.MissingWhere {
			inner := dml(toks, j, w)
			info.MissingWhere, info.Target = true, inner.Target
		}
	}
	return info
}

// missingWhere reports whether the UPDATE/DELETE at k has no WHERE at its own
// parenthesis level before that level closes.
func missingWhere(toks []token, k int) bool {
	depth := 0
	for j := k + 1; j < len(toks); j++ {
		depth += depthDelta(toks[j])
		if depth < 0 {
			return true
		}
		if depth == 0 && isWord(toks[j], "WHERE") {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

func selectTarget(toks []token, i int) string {
	k := depth0Index(toks, i, "FROM")
	if k < 0 {
		return ""
	}
	name, next := identPath(toks, k+1)
	depth := 0
	for j := next; j < len(toks); j++ {
		depth += depthDelta(toks[j])
		if depth != 0 {
			continue
		}
		if toks[j].kind == tokWord && clauseEnd[upper(toks[j])] {
			break
		}
		if isWord(toks[j], "JOIN") || isPunct(toks[j], ",") {
			return ""
		}
	}
	return name
}

// identAfter skips modifiers after toks[k] and returns the table name there.
func identAfter(toks []token, k int) string {
	j := k + 1
	for j < len(toks) && toks[j].kind == tokWord && modifiers[upper(toks[j])] {
		j++
	}
	name, _ := identPath(toks, j)
	return name
}

// identPath reads a dotted name at j and returns its last part and the index after it.
func identPath(toks []token, j int) (string, int) {
	if j >= len(toks) || !isName(toks[j]) {
		return "", j
	}
	last := toks[j].text
	j++
	for j+1 < len(toks) && isPunct(toks[j], ".") && isName(toks[j+1]) {
		last = toks[j+1].text
		j += 2
	}
	return last, j
}

func isName(t token) bool { return t.kind == tokWord || t.kind == tokIdent }

func isDML(toks []token, j int) bool {
	if toks[j].kind != tokWord || !dmlWords[upper(toks[j])] {
		return false
	}
	return j == 0 || toks[j-1].kind != tokWord || !notDMLPrev[upper(toks[j-1])]
}

func depthDelta(t token) int {
	switch {
	case isPunct(t, "("):
		return 1
	case isPunct(t, ")"):
		return -1
	}
	return 0
}

func depth0Index(toks []token, from int, w string) int {
	depth := 0
	for j := from; j < len(toks); j++ {
		depth += depthDelta(toks[j])
		if depth == 0 && isWord(toks[j], w) {
			return j
		}
	}
	return -1
}

func depth0Word(toks []token, from int, w string) bool { return depth0Index(toks, from, w) >= 0 }

func indexWord(toks []token, from int, w string) int {
	for j := from; j < len(toks); j++ {
		if isWord(toks[j], w) {
			return j
		}
	}
	return -1
}

func indexIn(toks []token, from int, set map[string]bool) int {
	for j := from; j < len(toks); j++ {
		if toks[j].kind == tokWord && set[upper(toks[j])] {
			return j
		}
	}
	return -1
}

func upper(t token) string            { return strings.ToUpper(t.text) }
func isWord(t token, w string) bool   { return t.kind == tokWord && strings.EqualFold(t.text, w) }
func isPunct(t token, p string) bool  { return t.kind == tokPunct && t.text == p }
```

说明：`INSERT … ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE` 的 `UPDATE` 前一个词是 `KEY`，`isDML` 返回 false；`dml("INSERT")` 不检查 WHERE。`SELECT … FOR UPDATE` 只会走 `SELECT` 分支，不扫描 DML。

### Task 4: db 流式读取（Stream）

**Files:**
- Modify: `internal/db/driver.go`, `internal/db/postgres/postgres.go`, `internal/db/mysql/mysql.go`, `internal/db/dbtest/contract.go`, `internal/ui/fakes_test.go`
- Create: `internal/db/stream.go`
- Test: `internal/db/stream_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `var ErrStop = errors.New("stop streaming")`
  - `type Querier interface { Query(...); Exec(...); Stream(ctx context.Context, sql string, onColumns func([]ColumnInfo), onRow func([]Value) error) error }`
  - `Conn` 改为内嵌 `Querier`（`Meta`/`Dialect`/`Close` 不变）
  - `func Collect(stream func(onColumns func([]ColumnInfo), onRow func([]Value) error) error) (*ResultSet, error)`
  - PG 包内：`type pgxQuerier interface{ Query(ctx context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (pgx.Rows, error) }`、`func stream(ctx context.Context, q pgxQuerier, sql string, args []any, onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error`
  - MySQL 包内：`type queryer interface{ QueryContext(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (*sql.Rows, error) }`、`func stream(ctx context.Context, q queryer, query string, args []any, onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error, abandon func()) error`（Task 5 的 MySQL 会话用 `abandon` 发 `KILL QUERY`）

**语义：** `Stream` 先调用一次 `onColumns`（零行结果也调用），再逐行调用 `onRow`。`onRow` 返回 `ErrStop` 时放弃剩余结果（PG：取消 context；MySQL 连接池：取消 context），`Stream` 返回 nil。`Query` 由 `Collect` + 同一个 `stream` 实现。

```go
package db

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/go-cmp/cmp"
)

func TestCollect(t *testing.T) {
	rs, err := Collect(func(onColumns func([]ColumnInfo), onRow func([]Value) error) error {
		onColumns([]ColumnInfo{{Name: "id"}})
		for _, v := range []string{"1", "2"} {
			if err := onRow([]Value{{Text: v}}); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := &ResultSet{Columns: []ColumnInfo{{Name: "id"}}, Rows: [][]Value{{{Text: "1"}}, {{Text: "2"}}}}
	if diff := cmp.Diff(want, rs); diff != "" {
		t.Fatalf("(-want +got):\n%s", diff)
	}

	boom := errors.New("boom")
	if _, err := Collect(func(func([]ColumnInfo), func([]Value) error) error { return boom }); !errors.Is(err, boom) {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
}
```

```go
	t.Run("Stream delivers columns and can stop early", func(t *testing.T) {
		q := "SELECT id FROM " + d.QualifiedName(ref("users")) + " ORDER BY id"
		var cols []db.ColumnInfo
		var rows [][]db.Value
		err := conn.Stream(ctx, q,
			func(c []db.ColumnInfo) { cols = c },
			func(r []db.Value) error {

			t.Fatalf("cols=%v rows=%v", cols, rows)
		}
		if _, err := conn.Query(ctx, "SELECT 1"); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("conn unusable after an abandoned stream: %v", err)
		}

		cols = nil
		err = conn.Stream(ctx, "SELECT id, name FROM "+d.QualifiedName(ref("users"))+" WHERE id < 0",
			func(c []db.ColumnInfo) { cols = c },
			func([]db.Value) error { return errors.New("no rows expected") })
		if err != nil || len(cols) != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("empty result: cols=%v err=%v", cols, err)
		}
	})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`internal/db/driver.go`：在 `ErrNotFound` 下面加 `ErrStop`，并把 `Conn` 拆出 `Querier`：

```go
// ErrStop, returned by a Stream row callback, abandons the rest of the result.
var ErrStop = errors.New("stop streaming")

// Querier runs SQL. Conn and Session both implement it.
type Querier interface {
	Query(ctx context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (*ResultSet, error)
	Exec(ctx context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (ExecResult, error)
	// Stream runs a query, calling onColumns once (even for an empty result)
	// and onRow for each row. If onRow returns ErrStop the rest of the result
	// is abandoned and Stream returns nil.
	Stream(ctx context.Context, sql string, onColumns func([]ColumnInfo), onRow func([]Value) error) error
}

// Conn is an open, pooled connection. It is safe for concurrent use.
type Conn interface {
	Querier
	Meta() MetaReader
	Dialect() Dialect
	Close() error
}
```

`internal/db/stream.go`：

```go
package db

// Collect reads a whole result through a stream function.
func Collect(stream func(onColumns func([]ColumnInfo), onRow func([]Value) error) error) (*ResultSet, error) {
	rs := &ResultSet{}
	err := stream(
		func(cols []ColumnInfo) { rs.Columns = cols },
		func(row []Value) error {
			rs.Rows = append(rs.Rows, row)
			return nil
		})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return rs, nil
}
```

`internal/db/postgres/postgres.go`：用下面的代码替换原来的 `Query` 方法（import 增加 `"errors"`）：

```go
type pgxQuerier interface {
	Query(ctx context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (pgx.Rows, error)
}

func (c *conn) Query(ctx context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (*db.ResultSet, error) {
	return db.Collect(func(onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
		return stream(ctx, c.pool, sql, args, onColumns, onRow)
	})
}

func (c *conn) Stream(ctx context.Context, sql string, onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
	return stream(ctx, c.pool, sql, nil, onColumns, onRow)
}

// stream asks the server for text-format results so every value is shown
// exactly as psql would print it.
func stream(ctx context.Context, q pgxQuerier, sql string, args []any,
	onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error,
) error {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	defer cancel()
	rows, err := q.Query(ctx, sql, append([]any{pgx.QueryResultFormats{pgx.TextFormatCode}}, args...)...)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer rows.Close()

	typeMap := rows.Conn().TypeMap()
	fields := rows.FieldDescriptions()
	cols := make([]db.ColumnInfo, len(fields))
	for i, f := range fields {
		typ := fmt.Sprintf("oid:%d", f.DataTypeOID)
		if t, ok := typeMap.TypeForOID(f.DataTypeOID); ok {
			typ = t.Name
		}
		cols[i] = db.ColumnInfo{Name: f.Name, DatabaseType: typ}
	}
	onColumns(cols)
	for rows.Next() {
		raw := rows.RawValues()
		row := make([]db.Value, len(raw))
		for i, b := range raw {
			if b == nil {
				row[i] = db.Value{Null: true}
			} else {
				row[i] = db.Value{Text: string(b)}
			}
		}
		if err := onRow(row); err != nil {
			if errors.Is(err, db.ErrStop) {
				cancel() // abandon the rest instead of reading it
				return nil
			}
			return err
		}
	}
	return rows.Err()
}
```

`internal/db/mysql/mysql.go`：替换 `Query`（import 增加 `"errors"`）：

```go
type queryer interface {
	QueryContext(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (*sql.Rows, error)
}

func (c *conn) Query(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (*db.ResultSet, error) {
	return db.Collect(func(onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
		return stream(ctx, c.db, query, args, onColumns, onRow, func() {})
	})
}

func (c *conn) Stream(ctx context.Context, query string, onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	defer cancel()
	return stream(ctx, c.db, query, nil, onColumns, onRow, cancel)
}

// stream reads rows as text. When onRow returns db.ErrStop, abandon is called
// before the rows are closed so the rest of the result is not read.
func stream(ctx context.Context, q queryer, query string, args []any,
	onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error, abandon func(),
) error {
	rows, err := q.QueryContext(ctx, query, args...)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer func() { _ = rows.Close() }()
	types, err := rows.ColumnTypes()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	cols := make([]db.ColumnInfo, len(types))
	for i, ct := range types {
		cols[i] = db.ColumnInfo{Name: ct.Name(), DatabaseType: ct.DatabaseTypeName()}
	}
	onColumns(cols)
	dest := make([]sql.NullString, len(types))
	ptrs := make([]any, len(types))
	for i := range dest {
		ptrs[i] = &dest[i]
	}
	for rows.Next() {
		if err := rows.Scan(ptrs...); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		row := make([]db.Value, len(dest))
		for i, v := range dest {
			if v.Valid {
				row[i] = db.Value{Text: displayText(v.String)}
			} else {
				row[i] = db.Value{Null: true}
			}
		}
		if err := onRow(row); err != nil {
			if errors.Is(err, db.ErrStop) {
				abandon()
				return nil
			}
			return err
		}
	}
	return rows.Err()
}
```

```go
func (c *fakeConn) Stream(ctx context.Context, q string, onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
	rs, err := c.Query(ctx, q)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	onColumns(rs.Columns)
	for _, r := range rs.Rows {
		if err := onRow(r); err != nil {
			if errors.Is(err, db.ErrStop) {
				return nil
			}
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}
```

### Task 5: db 独占会话、服务端取消、只读兜底、断线识别

**Files:**
- Modify: `internal/db/driver.go`, `internal/db/postgres/postgres.go`, `internal/db/mysql/mysql.go`, `internal/db/dbtest/contract.go`, `internal/db/postgres/integration_test.go`, `internal/db/mysql/integration_test.go`, `internal/ui/fakes_test.go`
- Create: `internal/db/connlost.go`, `internal/db/postgres/session.go`, `internal/db/mysql/session.go`
- Test: `internal/db/connlost_test.go`, `internal/db/postgres/postgres_test.go`, `internal/db/mysql/mysql_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4 的 `Querier`、`stream`、`Collect`。
- Produces:
  - `ConnConfig.ReadOnly bool`
  - `Conn` 增加 `Session(ctx context.Context) (Session, error)`
  - `type Session interface { Querier; Ping(ctx context.Context) error; Close() error }`
  - `var ErrConnLost = errors.New("connection lost")`、`func IsConnLost(err error) bool`
  - `dbtest.Fixture{NS db.Namespace; SleepSQL string; OpenReadOnly func(ctx context.Context) (db.Conn, error)}`，`func RunContract(t *testing.T, conn db.Conn, f Fixture)`
  - PG：`func poolConfig(cfg db.ConnConfig) (*pgxpool.Config, error)`、`const cancelGrace = 5 * time.Second`
  - MySQL：`const killGrace = 5 * time.Second`
  - ui 测试替身：`fakeSession`

**会话语义（两个驱动一致，由合约测试保证）：**
- `Session` 占用一条专属连接：变量、临时表、`USE`、`search_path` 在多次调用间保持；`Close` 后连接关闭，不回连接池。
- 取消正在执行的调用的 context：服务器端停止该语句，调用返回 `ctx.Err()`（`errors.Is(err, context.Canceled)`），**会话仍可用**。PG：pgconn 的 `CancelRequestContextWatcherHandler` 发送 cancel request；MySQL：驱动看到的是不可取消的 context，取消时另开连接执行 `KILL QUERY <id>`；服务器 5 秒内不响应才丢弃连接。
- 会话中 `Stream` 的 `ErrStop`：PG 取消 context（发 cancel request）；MySQL 发 `KILL QUERY`；会话仍可用。
- 连接已断：错误包装为 `ErrConnLost`。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

`internal/db/connlost_test.go`：

```go
package db

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql/driver"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net"
	"syscall"
	"testing"
)

func TestIsConnLost(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		err  error
		want bool
	}{
		{nil, false},
		{fmt.Errorf("query: %w", ErrConnLost), true},
		{driver.ErrBadConn, true},
		{fmt.Errorf("read: %w", io.EOF), true},
		{io.ErrUnexpectedEOF, true},
		{&net.OpError{Op: "dial", Net: "tcp", Err: syscall.ECONNREFUSED}, true},
		{fmt.Errorf("write: %w", syscall.EPIPE), true},
		{context.Canceled, false},
		{fmt.Errorf("x: %w", context.DeadlineExceeded), false},
		{errors.New("ERROR: syntax error at or near \"selec\""), false},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		if got := IsConnLost(c.err); got != c.want {
			t.Errorf("IsConnLost(%v) = %v, want %v", c.err, got, c.want)
		}
	}
}
```

```go
func TestPoolConfig(t *testing.T) {
	pc, err := poolConfig(db.ConnConfig{Host: "h", Port: 5432, User: "u", Database: "app"})

		t.Fatal(err)

	if _, ok := pc.ConnConfig.RuntimeParams["default_transaction_read_only"]; ok {
		t.Fatal("read-write connections must not set default_transaction_read_only")
	}
	ro, err := poolConfig(db.ConnConfig{Host: "h", Port: 5432, User: "u", Database: "app", ReadOnly: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := ro.ConnConfig.RuntimeParams["default_transaction_read_only"]; got != "on" {
		t.Fatalf("default_transaction_read_only = %q", got)
	}
}
```

`internal/db/dbtest/contract.go`：把签名改为 `func RunContract(t *testing.T, conn db.Conn, f Fixture)`，函数开头改为：

```go
// Fixture describes the standard fixture and engine-specific bits of the contract.
type Fixture struct {
	NS db.Namespace
	// SleepSQL is a query that runs for at least 30 seconds.
	SleepSQL string
	// OpenReadOnly opens another connection to the same database with ReadOnly set.
	OpenReadOnly func(ctx context.Context) (db.Conn, error)
}

// RunContract checks conn against the standard fixture created in f.NS.
func RunContract(t *testing.T, conn db.Conn, f Fixture) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	ns := f.NS
	d := conn.Dialect()
	ref := func(name string) db.TableRef { return db.TableRef{Namespace: ns, Name: name} }
```

（其余已有子测试不变，继续使用 `ns`、`d`、`ref`。）在末尾追加（import 增加 `"time"`）：

```go
	t.Run("Session keeps state, survives cancel and early stop", func(t *testing.T) {
		s, err := conn.Session(ctx)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		defer func() { _ = s.Close() }()
		for _, q := range []string{"CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE sqlvim_tmp (id int)", "INSERT INTO sqlvim_tmp VALUES (1), (2)"} {
			if _, err := s.Exec(ctx, q); err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("%s: %v", q, err)
			}
		}
		countTmp := func() int {
			t.Helper()
			rs, err := s.Query(ctx, "SELECT id FROM sqlvim_tmp")
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("session lost its state: %v", err)
			}
			return len(rs.Rows)
		}
		if n := countTmp(); n != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("rows = %d", n)
		}

		cctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
		time.AfterFunc(300*time.Millisecond, cancel)
		start := time.Now()
		err = s.Stream(cctx, f.SleepSQL, func([]db.ColumnInfo) {}, func([]db.Value) error { return nil })
		if !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
			t.Fatalf("cancelled stream err = %v", err)
		}
		if el := time.Since(start); el > 10*time.Second {
			t.Fatalf("cancel took %v", el)
		}
		if n := countTmp(); n != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("after cancel rows = %d", n)
		}

		var got int
		err = s.Stream(ctx, "SELECT id FROM sqlvim_tmp ORDER BY id", func([]db.ColumnInfo) {}, func([]db.Value) error {
			got++
			return db.ErrStop
		})
		if err != nil || got != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("early stop: got=%d err=%v", got, err)
		}
		if n := countTmp(); n != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("after early stop rows = %d", n)
		}
		if err := s.Ping(ctx); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	})

	t.Run("pooled queries do not see session state", func(t *testing.T) {
		s, err := conn.Session(ctx)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if _, err := s.Exec(ctx, "CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE sqlvim_private (id int)"); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := s.Close(); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		for range 4 { // touch every pooled connection
			if _, err := conn.Query(ctx, "SELECT * FROM sqlvim_private"); err == nil {
				t.Fatal("a closed session's temporary table leaked into the pool")
			}
		}
	})

	t.Run("ReadOnly connections reject writes on the server", func(t *testing.T) {
		ro, err := f.OpenReadOnly(ctx)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		defer func() { _ = ro.Close() }()
		update := "UPDATE " + d.QualifiedName(ref("users")) + " SET note = 'x' WHERE id = 1"
		if _, err := ro.Exec(ctx, update); err == nil {
			t.Fatal("pooled write succeeded on a read-only connection")
		}
		s, err := ro.Session(ctx)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		defer func() { _ = s.Close() }()
		if _, err := s.Exec(ctx, update); err == nil {
			t.Fatal("session write succeeded on a read-only connection")
		}
		if _, err := s.Query(ctx, "SELECT id FROM "+d.QualifiedName(ref("users"))); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("reads must still work: %v", err)
		}
	})
```

`internal/db/driver.go`：`ConnConfig` 加字段，`Conn` 加方法，新增 `Session`：

```go
// ConnConfig is what a driver needs to open a connection.
type ConnConfig struct {
	Host     string
	Port     int
	User     string
	Password string
	Database string
	Params   map[string]string
	// ReadOnly makes the server reject writes for every connection opened.
	ReadOnly bool
}

// Conn is an open, pooled connection. It is safe for concurrent use.
type Conn interface {
	Querier
	// Session reserves a dedicated server connection (see Session).
	Session(ctx context.Context) (Session, error)
	Meta() MetaReader
	Dialect() Dialect
	Close() error
}

// Session is one dedicated server connection, as used by a console: session
// state (variables, temporary tables, USE, search_path) persists across calls.
// Cancelling the context of a running call stops the statement on the server
// and returns ctx.Err(); the session stays usable. Errors caused by a dead
// connection wrap ErrConnLost. Not safe for concurrent use.
type Session interface {
	Querier
	Ping(ctx context.Context) error
	Close() error
}
```

`internal/db/connlost.go`：

```go
package db

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql/driver"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"net"
	"syscall"
)

// ErrConnLost marks errors caused by a connection that is no longer usable.
var ErrConnLost = errors.New("connection lost")

// IsConnLost reports whether err means the server connection is gone, so
// reconnecting may help. Cancellation and timeouts of the caller's own
// context are not connection losses.
func IsConnLost(err error) bool {
	if err == nil || errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) || errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
		return false
	}
	var ne net.Error
	return errors.Is(err, ErrConnLost) || errors.Is(err, driver.ErrBadConn) ||
		errors.Is(err, io.EOF) || errors.Is(err, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF) ||
		errors.Is(err, net.ErrClosed) || errors.Is(err, syscall.ECONNRESET) ||
		errors.Is(err, syscall.EPIPE) || errors.As(err, &ne)
}
```

`internal/db/postgres/postgres.go`：`Open` 改用 `poolConfig`（import 增加 `"time"`、`"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"`、`"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn/ctxwatch"`）：

```go
// cancelGrace is how long a cancelled statement may take to stop before the
// connection is dropped.
const cancelGrace = 5 * time.Second

// Open connects and pings the server.
func (Driver) Open(ctx context.Context, cfg db.ConnConfig) (db.Conn, error) {
	pcfg, err := poolConfig(cfg)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	pool, err := pgxpool.NewWithConfig(ctx, pcfg)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := pool.Ping(ctx); err != nil {
		pool.Close()
		return nil, err
	}
	return &conn{pool: pool, database: pcfg.ConnConfig.Database}, nil
}

func poolConfig(cfg db.ConnConfig) (*pgxpool.Config, error) {
	pcfg, err := pgxpool.ParseConfig(connString(cfg))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	pcfg.MaxConns = 4
	cc := pcfg.ConnConfig
	// Cancelling a context sends a cancel request and keeps the connection,
	// instead of pgx's default of closing it.
	cc.BuildContextWatcherHandler = func(pc *pgconn.PgConn) ctxwatch.Handler {
		return &pgconn.CancelRequestContextWatcherHandler{Conn: pc, DeadlineDelay: cancelGrace}
	}
	if cfg.ReadOnly {
		if cc.RuntimeParams == nil {
			cc.RuntimeParams = map[string]string{}
		}
		cc.RuntimeParams["default_transaction_read_only"] = "on"
	}
	return pcfg, nil
}
```

`internal/db/postgres/session.go`：

```go
package postgres

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

type session struct{ c *pgx.Conn }

// Session hijacks a pooled connection so its state never returns to the pool.
func (c *conn) Session(ctx context.Context) (db.Session, error) {
	pc, err := c.pool.Acquire(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &session{c: pc.Hijack()}, nil
}

// run reports cancellation as ctx.Err() and a dead connection as db.ErrConnLost.
func (s *session) run(ctx context.Context, fn func(context.Context) error) error {
	err := fn(ctx)
	if ctx.Err() != nil {
		return ctx.Err()
	}
	if err != nil && s.c.IsClosed() {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", db.ErrConnLost, err)
	}
	return err
}

func (s *session) stream(ctx context.Context, sql string, args []any,
	onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error,
) error {
	return s.run(ctx, func(ctx context.Context) error { return stream(ctx, s.c, sql, args, onColumns, onRow) })
}

func (s *session) Query(ctx context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (*db.ResultSet, error) {
	return db.Collect(func(onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
		return s.stream(ctx, sql, args, onColumns, onRow)
	})
}

func (s *session) Stream(ctx context.Context, sql string, onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
	return s.stream(ctx, sql, nil, onColumns, onRow)
}

func (s *session) Exec(ctx context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (db.ExecResult, error) {
	var res db.ExecResult
	err := s.run(ctx, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		tag, err := s.c.Exec(ctx, sql, args...)
		res.RowsAffected = tag.RowsAffected()
		return err
	})
	return res, err
}

func (s *session) Ping(ctx context.Context) error { return s.run(ctx, s.c.Ping) }

func (s *session) Close() error {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), cancelGrace)
	defer cancel()
	return s.c.Close(ctx)
}
```

`internal/db/mysql/mysql.go`：`dsnConfig` 改为在两条路径之后统一处理只读；连接池方法包装断线错误（import 增加 `"database/sql/driver"`、`"fmt"`）：

```go
func dsnConfig(cfg db.ConnConfig) (*gomysql.Config, error) {
	mc := gomysql.NewConfig()
	mc.User = cfg.User
	mc.Passwd = cfg.Password
	mc.Net = "tcp"
	mc.Addr = net.JoinHostPort(cfg.Host, strconv.Itoa(cfg.Port))
	mc.DBName = cfg.Database
	mc.InterpolateParams = true
	mc.Timeout = 10 * time.Second
	if len(cfg.Params) > 0 {
		// Round-trip through the DSN so special keys (tls, charset, ...) are
		// parsed by the driver exactly as in a hand-written DSN.
		q := url.Values{}
		for k, v := range cfg.Params {
			q.Set(k, v)
		}
		dsn := mc.FormatDSN()
		sep := "?"
		if strings.Contains(dsn[strings.LastIndex(dsn, "/"):], "?") {
			sep = "&"
		}
		var err error
		if mc, err = gomysql.ParseDSN(dsn + sep + q.Encode()); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	if cfg.ReadOnly {
		if mc.Params == nil {
			mc.Params = map[string]string{}
		}
		// Sent as "SET transaction_read_only = 1" on every new connection.
		mc.Params["transaction_read_only"] = "1"
	}
	return mc, nil
}

func isBadConn(err error) bool {
	return errors.Is(err, driver.ErrBadConn) || errors.Is(err, gomysql.ErrInvalidConn)
}

// wrap marks dead-connection errors with db.ErrConnLost.
func wrap(err error) error {
	if isBadConn(err) {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", db.ErrConnLost, err)
	}
	return err
}

func (c *conn) Exec(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (db.ExecResult, error) {
	res, err := c.db.ExecContext(ctx, query, args...)
	if err != nil {
		return db.ExecResult{}, wrap(err)
	}
	n, err := res.RowsAffected()
	return db.ExecResult{RowsAffected: n}, err
}

func (c *conn) Query(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (*db.ResultSet, error) {
	rs, err := db.Collect(func(onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
		return stream(ctx, c.db, query, args, onColumns, onRow, func() {})
	})
	return rs, wrap(err)
}

func (c *conn) Stream(ctx context.Context, query string, onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	defer cancel()
	return wrap(stream(ctx, c.db, query, nil, onColumns, onRow, cancel))
}
```

`internal/db/mysql/session.go`：

```go
package mysql

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

// killGrace is how long a killed statement may take to stop before the
// connection is dropped.
const killGrace = 5 * time.Second

type session struct {
	c    *sql.Conn
	pool *sql.DB
	id   int64
}

// Session reserves one connection and remembers its id for KILL QUERY.
func (c *conn) Session(ctx context.Context) (db.Session, error) {
	sc, err := c.db.Conn(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, wrap(err)
	}
	var id int64
	if err := sc.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT CONNECTION_ID()").Scan(&id); err != nil {
		_ = sc.Close()
		return nil, wrap(err)
	}
	return &session{c: sc, pool: c.db, id: id}, nil
}

// kill stops the statement running on the session from another connection.
func (s *session) kill() {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), killGrace)
	defer cancel()
	_, _ = s.pool.ExecContext(ctx, "KILL QUERY "+strconv.FormatInt(s.id, 10))
}

// run hides cancellation from the driver (which would close the connection)
// and sends KILL QUERY instead, so session state survives. If the server does
// not stop within killGrace the driver context is cancelled after all.
func (s *session) run(ctx context.Context, fn func(context.Context) error) error {
	dctx, drop := context.WithCancel(context.WithoutCancel(ctx))
	defer drop()
	stop := context.AfterFunc(ctx, func() {
		s.kill()
		time.AfterFunc(killGrace, drop)
	})
	defer stop()
	err := fn(dctx)
	if ctx.Err() != nil {
		return ctx.Err() // SLEEP() returns normally when killed; report the cancel anyway
	}
	return wrap(err)
}

func (s *session) stream(ctx context.Context, query string, args []any,
	onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error,
) error {
	return s.run(ctx, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		return stream(ctx, s.c, query, args, onColumns, onRow, s.kill)
	})
}

func (s *session) Query(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (*db.ResultSet, error) {
	return db.Collect(func(onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
		return s.stream(ctx, query, args, onColumns, onRow)
	})
}

func (s *session) Stream(ctx context.Context, query string, onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
	return s.stream(ctx, query, nil, onColumns, onRow)
}

func (s *session) Exec(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (db.ExecResult, error) {
	var res db.ExecResult
	err := s.run(ctx, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		r, err := s.c.ExecContext(ctx, query, args...)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		res.RowsAffected, err = r.RowsAffected()
		return err
	})
	return res, err
}

func (s *session) Ping(ctx context.Context) error { return s.run(ctx, s.c.PingContext) }

func (s *session) Close() error { return s.c.Close() }
```

```go
func TestConnPassesReadOnly(t *testing.T) {
	drv := &fakeDriver{name: "postgres", scoped: true}
	sec := &fakeSecrets{m: map[string]string{"prod": "pw"}}
	m := NewManager([]config.Connection{{Name: "prod", Driver: "postgres", Env: config.EnvProd}}, sec, drv)
	if _, err := m.Conn(context.Background(), "prod", ""); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !drv.opens[0].ReadOnly {
		t.Fatal("prod connections default to read-only")
	}
}

func TestAddConnection(t *testing.T) {
	m, drv, sec := setup(true)
	var persisted []config.Connection
	m.Persist = func(c config.Connection) error { persisted = append(persisted, c); return nil }

	if err := m.AddConnection(config.Connection{Name: "new", Driver: "postgres", User: "u"}, "pw", true); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	c, ok := m.Connection("new")
	if !ok || c.Port != 5432 || c.Env != config.EnvDev {
		t.Fatalf("connection = %+v, %v (want normalized)", c, ok)
	}
	if len(persisted) != 1 || persisted[0].Name != "new" {
		t.Fatalf("persisted = %v", persisted)
	}
	if sec.m["new"] != "pw" {
		t.Fatal("password saved to the keychain")
	}
	if names := len(m.Connections()); names != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("connections = %d", names)
	}
	if _, err := m.Conn(context.Background(), "new", ""); err != nil || drv.opens[0].Password != "pw" {
		t.Fatalf("connect new: %v %+v", err, drv.opens)
	}

	if err := m.AddConnection(config.Connection{Name: "new", Driver: "postgres"}, "", false); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("duplicate names are rejected")
	}
	if err := m.AddConnection(config.Connection{Name: "x", Driver: "mysql"}, "", false); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("drivers that are not registered are rejected")
	}
	m.Persist = func(config.Connection) error { return errors.New("disk full") }
	if err := m.AddConnection(config.Connection{Name: "y", Driver: "postgres"}, "", false); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("persist errors are returned")
	}
	if _, ok := m.Connection("y"); ok {
		t.Fatal("a connection that failed to persist is not added")
	}
}

func TestResetClosesOnlyThatConnection(t *testing.T) {
	m, _, sec := setup(true)
	sec.m["local"] = "pw"
	ctx := context.Background()
	a, _ := m.Conn(ctx, "local", "app")
	b, _ := m.Conn(ctx, "local", "other")
	m.Reset("local")
	if !a.(*fakeConn).closed || !b.(*fakeConn).closed {
		t.Fatal("all databases of the connection are closed")
	}
	c, _ := m.Conn(ctx, "local", "app")
	if c == a {
		t.Fatal("the next Conn reconnects")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** `internal/session/manager.go`

```go
// Manager opens connections lazily and reuses them. Safe for concurrent use.
type Manager struct {
	drivers map[string]db.Driver
	secrets secret.Store

	// Persist, if set, saves connections added with AddConnection.
	Persist func(config.Connection) error

	mu        sync.Mutex
	conns     []config.Connection
	passwords map[string]string
	open      map[connKey]db.Conn
}

// Connections returns the configured connections in order.
func (m *Manager) Connections() []config.Connection {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	return slices.Clone(m.conns)
}

// Connection returns the named connection's configuration.
func (m *Manager) Connection(name string) (config.Connection, bool) {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	for _, c := range m.conns {
		if c.Name == name {
			return c, true
		}
	}
	return config.Connection{}, false
}

// AddConnection validates, persists and registers a new connection, then
// remembers its password (saving it to the keychain when save is set).
func (m *Manager) AddConnection(c config.Connection, password string, save bool) error {
	c, err := config.Normalize(c)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if _, ok := m.drivers[c.Driver]; !ok {
		return fmt.Errorf("connection %q: driver %q not available", c.Name, c.Driver)
	}
	if _, ok := m.Connection(c.Name); ok {
		return fmt.Errorf("connection %q already exists", c.Name)
	}
	if m.Persist != nil {
		if err := m.Persist(c); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	m.mu.Lock()
	m.conns = append(m.conns, c)
	m.mu.Unlock()
	if password == "" {
		return nil
	}
	return m.SetPassword(c.Name, password, save)
}

// Reset closes every open connection of name; the next Conn reconnects.
func (m *Manager) Reset(name string) {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	m.resetLocked(name)
}

func (m *Manager) resetLocked(name string) {
	for k, c := range m.open {
		if k.conn == name {
			_ = c.Close()
			delete(m.open, k)
		}
	}
}

// SetPassword remembers a password for this run and optionally saves it to
// the keychain. Open connections for name are closed so the next use reconnects.
func (m *Manager) SetPassword(name, password string, save bool) error {
	m.mu.Lock()
	m.passwords[name] = password
	m.resetLocked(name)
	m.mu.Unlock()
	if save {
		return m.secrets.Set(name, password)
	}
	return nil
}

func (m *Manager) lookup(name string) (config.Connection, db.Driver, error) {
	c, ok := m.Connection(name)
	if !ok {
		return config.Connection{}, nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown connection %q", name)
	}
	drv, ok := m.drivers[c.Driver]
	if !ok {
		return c, nil, fmt.Errorf("connection %q: driver %q not available", name, c.Driver)
	}
	return c, drv, nil
}
```

`Conn` 中打开连接的配置加上只读：

`internal/ui/backend.go`：

```go
// Backend is what the UI needs from the session layer.
type Backend interface {
	Connections() []config.Connection
	Connection(name string) (config.Connection, bool)
	Conn(ctx context.Context, name, database string) (db.Conn, error)
	SetPassword(name, password string, save bool) error
	// Reset drops the open connections of name so the next Conn reconnects.
	Reset(name string)
	// AddConnection saves a new connection to connections.toml (and its
	// password to the keychain when save is set).
	AddConnection(c config.Connection, password string, save bool) error
}
```

`internal/ui/fakes_test.go`：`fakeBackend` 增加字段与方法：

```go
type fakeBackend struct {
	mu           sync.Mutex
	conns        []config.Connection
	conn         *fakeConn
	needPassword bool
	connErr      error
	passwords    []string
	resets       []string
	added        []config.Connection
	addErr       error
}

func (f *fakeBackend) Connections() []config.Connection {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return slices.Clone(f.conns)
}

func (f *fakeBackend) Connection(name string) (config.Connection, bool) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for _, c := range f.conns {
		if c.Name == name {
			return c, true
		}
	}
	return config.Connection{}, false
}

func (f *fakeBackend) Reset(name string) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.resets = append(f.resets, name)
}

func (f *fakeBackend) AddConnection(c config.Connection, password string, save bool) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if f.addErr != nil {
		return f.addErr
	}
	c, err := config.Normalize(c)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	f.added = append(f.added, c)
	f.conns = append(f.conns, c)
	return nil
}
```

```go
package history

import (
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func open(t *testing.T) (*Store, string) {
	t.Helper()
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "state.db")
	s, err := Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = s.Close() })
	return s, path
}

func TestHistoryNewestFirstWithFilter(t *testing.T) {
	s, path := open(t)
	base := time.Date(2026, 10, 13, 9, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	for i, q := range []string{"select * from users", "update t set a = '50%'", "SELECT name FROM Users"} {
		e := Entry{Time: base.Add(time.Duration(i) * time.Minute), Conn: "local", Database: "app", SQL: q, Duration: 12 * time.Millisecond}
		if i == 1 {
			e.Err = "read-only"
		}
		if err := s.AddHistory(e); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	all, err := s.History("", 10)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(all) != 3 || all[0].SQL != "SELECT name FROM Users" || all[2].SQL != "select * from users" {
		t.Fatalf("order: %+v", all)
	}
	if !all[2].Time.Equal(base) || all[2].Duration != 12*time.Millisecond || all[1].Err != "read-only" || all[0].Conn != "local" {
		t.Fatalf("fields not round-tripped: %+v", all)
	}
	users, _ := s.History("USERS", 10)
	if len(users) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("case-insensitive filter: %+v", users)
	}
	pct, _ := s.History("50%", 10)
	if len(pct) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%% is literal: %+v", pct)
	}
	under, _ := s.History("_", 10)
	if len(under) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("_ is literal: %+v", under)
	}
	one, _ := s.History("", 1)
	if len(one) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("limit: %+v", one)
	}

	info, err := os.Stat(path)
	if err != nil || info.Mode().Perm() != 0o600 {
		t.Fatalf("state.db must be private: %v %v", info.Mode(), err)
	}
	_ = s.Close()
	again, err := Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = again.Close() }()
	if kept, _ := again.History("", 10); len(kept) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("history survives a restart: %d", len(kept))
	}
}

func TestDrafts(t *testing.T) {
	s, _ := open(t)
	if got, err := s.Draft("local/app"); err != nil || got != "" {
		t.Fatalf("missing draft = %q, %v", got, err)
	}
	for _, text := range []string{"select 1", "select 2"} {
		if err := s.SaveDraft("local/app", text); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if got, _ := s.Draft("local/app"); got != "select 2" {
		t.Fatalf("draft = %q", got)
	}
	if err := s.SaveDraft("local/app", ""); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, _ := s.Draft("local/app"); got != "" {
		t.Fatalf("empty text deletes the draft: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestMemoryStore(t *testing.T) {
	s, err := Open(":memory:")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = s.Close() }()
	if err := s.AddHistory(Entry{Time: time.Now(), SQL: "select 1"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, _ := s.History("", 5); len(got) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("memory store: %v", got)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现** `internal/history/history.go`

```go
// Package history stores SQL history and console drafts in a local SQLite file.
package history

import (
	"database/sql"
	"errors"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"time"

	_ "modernc.org/sqlite" // registers the "sqlite" driver
)

// maxEntries bounds the history table; older entries are pruned.
const maxEntries = 10000

// Entry is one executed statement.
type Entry struct {
	Time     time.Time
	Conn     string
	Database string
	SQL      string
	Duration time.Duration
	Err      string // empty on success
}

// Store is the state database. Safe for concurrent use.
type Store struct{ db *sql.DB }

var schema = []string{
	`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS history (
		id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
		at          INTEGER NOT NULL,
		conn        TEXT NOT NULL,
		db_name     TEXT NOT NULL,
		sql         TEXT NOT NULL,
		duration_ms INTEGER NOT NULL,
		error       TEXT NOT NULL
	)`,
	`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS drafts (
		key     TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
		text    TEXT NOT NULL,
		updated INTEGER NOT NULL
	)`,
}

// Open opens or creates the state database at path (mode 0600).
// ":memory:" opens a private in-memory database.
func Open(path string) (*Store, error) {
	dsn := ":memory:"
	if path != ":memory:" {
		f, err := os.OpenFile(path, os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0o600)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		_ = f.Close()
		dsn = path + "?_pragma=busy_timeout(5000)&_pragma=journal_mode(WAL)"
	}
	sqldb, err := sql.Open("sqlite", dsn)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	sqldb.SetMaxOpenConns(1) // a single writer; also keeps :memory: one database
	for _, stmt := range schema {
		if _, err := sqldb.Exec(stmt); err != nil {
			_ = sqldb.Close()
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return &Store{db: sqldb}, nil
}

// Close closes the database.
func (s *Store) Close() error { return s.db.Close() }

// AddHistory records one statement and prunes old entries.
func (s *Store) AddHistory(e Entry) error {
	_, err := s.db.Exec(`INSERT INTO history (at, conn, db_name, sql, duration_ms, error) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
		e.Time.UnixMilli(), e.Conn, e.Database, e.SQL, e.Duration.Milliseconds(), e.Err)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, err = s.db.Exec(`DELETE FROM history WHERE id <= (SELECT max(id) FROM history) - ?`, maxEntries)
	return err
}

// History returns up to limit entries whose SQL contains filter, newest first.
func (s *Store) History(filter string, limit int) ([]Entry, error) {
	rows, err := s.db.Query(`SELECT at, conn, db_name, sql, duration_ms, error FROM history
		WHERE sql LIKE ? ESCAPE '\' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?`, "%"+escapeLike(filter)+"%", limit)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer func() { _ = rows.Close() }()
	var out []Entry
	for rows.Next() {
		var e Entry
		var at, ms int64
		if err := rows.Scan(&at, &e.Conn, &e.Database, &e.SQL, &ms, &e.Err); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		e.Time = time.UnixMilli(at).UTC()
		e.Duration = time.Duration(ms) * time.Millisecond
		out = append(out, e)
	}
	return out, rows.Err()
}

func escapeLike(s string) string {
	return strings.NewReplacer(`\`, `\\`, `%`, `\%`, `_`, `\_`).Replace(s)
}

// SaveDraft stores the console text for key; empty text deletes the draft.
func (s *Store) SaveDraft(key, text string) error {
	if text == "" {
		_, err := s.db.Exec(`DELETE FROM drafts WHERE key = ?`, key)
		return err
	}
	_, err := s.db.Exec(`INSERT INTO drafts (key, text, updated) VALUES (?, ?, ?)
		ON CONFLICT (key) DO UPDATE SET text = excluded.text, updated = excluded.updated`,
		key, text, time.Now().UnixMilli())
	return err
}

// Draft returns the stored console text for key, or "".
func (s *Store) Draft(key string) (string, error) {
	var text string
	err := s.db.QueryRow(`SELECT text FROM drafts WHERE key = ?`, key).Scan(&text)
	if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
		return "", nil
	}
	return text, err
}
```
