### Task 8: 导出：文本格式（TSV/CSV/JSON/Markdown）

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/export/export.go`, `internal/export/text.go`, `internal/export/types.go`
- Test: `internal/export/text_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `type Format string`，常量 `TSV="tsv"`、`CSV="csv"`、`JSON="json"`、`Markdown="md"`、`SQL="sql"`、`XLSX="xlsx"`；`func (f Format) Label() string`
  - `type Options struct { NoHeader bool; Driver string; Table []string }`（`NoHeader` 只对 TSV/CSV 生效；`Driver`/`Table` 供 SQL 使用）
  - `type Writer interface { WriteHeader(cols []db.ColumnInfo) error; WriteRow(row []db.Value) error; Close() error }`（`Close` 只刷新，不关闭底层 `io.Writer`）
  - `func New(f Format, w io.Writer, o Options) (Writer, error)`
  - `func WriteResult(w Writer, rs *db.ResultSet) error`（写完并 `Close`）
  - `func Encode(f Format, rs *db.ResultSet, o Options) (string, error)`（XLSX 返回错误）
  - 包内：`isNumericType`、`isBinaryType`、`isNumber`（Task 9 使用）

**各格式对值的表示：**

| 格式 | NULL | 空串 | 特殊字符 |
|---|---|---|---|
| TSV | 空字段 | 空字段 | 含制表符/换行/引号的字段按 CSV 规则加引号（Excel 可正确粘贴） |
| CSV | 空字段 | 空字段 | encoding/csv 标准引号规则 |
| JSON | `null` | `""` | 数值列输出数字，PG `bool` 输出 `true`/`false`，`json`/`jsonb` 列中合法 JSON 原样嵌入；键按列顺序；不转义 `<>&` |
| Markdown | `NULL` | 空单元格 | `\|` 转义；换行写成 `<br>`；制表符写成空格 |

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试** `internal/export/text_test.go`

```go
package export

import (
	"encoding/csv"
	"encoding/json"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/go-cmp/cmp"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

func tricky() *db.ResultSet {
	return &db.ResultSet{
		Columns: []db.ColumnInfo{
			{Name: "id", DatabaseType: "int4"}, {Name: "name", DatabaseType: "text"},
			{Name: "note", DatabaseType: "text"}, {Name: "data", DatabaseType: "jsonb"},
			{Name: "ok", DatabaseType: "bool"},
		},
		Rows: [][]db.Value{
			{{Text: "1"}, {Text: "a\tb"}, {Text: "line1\nline2"}, {Text: `{"k": 1}`}, {Text: "t"}},
			{{Text: "2"}, {Text: `say "hi"|x<y>`}, {Text: ""}, {Null: true}, {Null: true}},
			{{Text: "3"}, {Text: "李雷"}, {Text: `back\slash`}, {Text: "not json"}, {Text: "f"}},
		},
	}
}

func encode(t *testing.T, f Format, rs *db.ResultSet, o Options) string {
	t.Helper()
	s, err := Encode(f, rs, o)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return s
}

func TestDelimitedRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	want := [][]string{
		{"id", "name", "note", "data", "ok"},
		{"1", "a\tb", "line1\nline2", `{"k": 1}`, "t"},
		{"2", `say "hi"|x<y>`, "", "", ""},
		{"3", "李雷", `back\slash`, "not json", "f"},
	}
	for f, comma := range map[Format]rune{TSV: '\t', CSV: ','} {
		r := csv.NewReader(strings.NewReader(encode(t, f, tricky(), Options{})))
		r.Comma = comma
		got, err := r.ReadAll()
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("%s: %v", f, err)
		}
		if diff := cmp.Diff(want, got); diff != "" {
			t.Errorf("%s (-want +got):\n%s", f, diff)
		}
	}
}

func TestTSVNoHeader(t *testing.T) {
	rs := &db.ResultSet{Columns: []db.ColumnInfo{{Name: "a"}, {Name: "b"}}, Rows: [][]db.Value{{{Text: "1"}, {Null: true}}}}
	if got := encode(t, TSV, rs, Options{NoHeader: true}); got != "1\t\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", got)
	}
}

func TestJSON(t *testing.T) {
	out := encode(t, JSON, tricky(), Options{})
	var got []map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(out), &got); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("invalid JSON: %v\n%s", err, out)
	}
	want := []map[string]any{
		{"id": 1.0, "name": "a\tb", "note": "line1\nline2", "data": map[string]any{"k": 1.0}, "ok": true},
		{"id": 2.0, "name": `say "hi"|x<y>`, "note": "", "data": nil, "ok": nil},
		{"id": 3.0, "name": "李雷", "note": `back\slash`, "data": "not json", "ok": false},
	}
	if diff := cmp.Diff(want, got); diff != "" {
		t.Fatalf("(-want +got):\n%s", diff)
	}
	if strings.Index(out, `"id"`) > strings.Index(out, `"name"`) {
		t.Error("keys keep column order")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(out, "<y>") {
		t.Error("HTML characters are not escaped")
	}
	empty := encode(t, JSON, &db.ResultSet{Columns: []db.ColumnInfo{{Name: "a"}}}, Options{})
	if empty != "[]\n" {
		t.Fatalf("empty = %q", empty)
	}
}

func TestMarkdown(t *testing.T) {
	want := "| id | name | note | data | ok |\n" +
		"| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n" +
		"| 1 | a b | line1<br>line2 | {\"k\": 1} | t |\n" +
		"| 2 | say \"hi\"\\|x<y> |  | NULL | NULL |\n" +
		"| 3 | 李雷 | back\\slash | not json | f |\n"
	if got := encode(t, Markdown, tricky(), Options{}); got != want {
		t.Fatalf("got:\n%s\nwant:\n%s", got, want)
	}
}

func TestEncodeRejectsXLSXAndUnknown(t *testing.T) {
	if _, err := Encode(XLSX, tricky(), Options{}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("xlsx is a file format")
	}
	if _, err := Encode("yaml", tricky(), Options{}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("unknown format")
	}
}

func TestTypeHelpers(t *testing.T) {
	for _, typ := range []string{"INT", "UNSIGNED BIGINT", "DECIMAL", "int8", "numeric", "float8", "DOUBLE"} {
		if !isNumericType(typ) {
			t.Errorf("%s is numeric", typ)
		}
	}
	for _, typ := range []string{"interval", "VARCHAR", "text", "point"} {
		if isNumericType(typ) {
			t.Errorf("%s is not numeric", typ)
		}
	}
	for s, want := range map[string]bool{"1": true, "-2.5": true, "1e10": true, "NaN": false, "Infinity": false, "1.": false, "": false} {
		if isNumber(s) != want {
			t.Errorf("isNumber(%q) != %v", s, want)
		}
	}
	if !isBinaryType("VARBINARY") || !isBinaryType("LONGBLOB") || isBinaryType("bytea") {
		t.Error("isBinaryType")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`internal/export/export.go`：

```go
// Package export writes result sets as TSV, CSV, JSON, Markdown, SQL or XLSX.
package export

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"strings"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

// Format is an output format; its value is also the file extension.
type Format string

const (
	TSV      Format = "tsv"
	CSV      Format = "csv"
	JSON     Format = "json"
	Markdown Format = "md"
	SQL      Format = "sql"
	XLSX     Format = "xlsx"
)

// Label is the human name shown in messages.
func (f Format) Label() string {
	switch f {
	case TSV:
		return "TSV"
	case CSV:
		return "CSV"
	case JSON:
		return "JSON"
	case Markdown:
		return "Markdown"
	case SQL:
		return "SQL INSERT"
	case XLSX:
		return "Excel"
	}
	return string(f)
}

// Options tunes a writer.
type Options struct {
	NoHeader bool     // TSV and CSV: leave out the header line
	Driver   string   // SQL: "mysql" or "postgres", selects quoting rules
	Table    []string // SQL: target table name parts, e.g. {"app", "users"}
}

// Writer writes one result set. Close flushes but never closes the io.Writer.
type Writer interface {
	WriteHeader(cols []db.ColumnInfo) error
	WriteRow(row []db.Value) error
	Close() error
}

// New returns a writer for f.
func New(f Format, w io.Writer, o Options) (Writer, error) {
	switch f {
	case TSV:
		return newDelimited(w, '\t', o), nil
	case CSV:
		return newDelimited(w, ',', o), nil
	case JSON:
		return newJSON(w), nil
	case Markdown:
		return newMarkdown(w), nil
	}
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown export format %q", f)
}

// WriteResult writes rs through w and closes w.
func WriteResult(w Writer, rs *db.ResultSet) error {
	if err := w.WriteHeader(rs.Columns); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, r := range rs.Rows {
		if err := w.WriteRow(r); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return w.Close()
}

// Encode renders rs as text in any format except XLSX.
func Encode(f Format, rs *db.ResultSet, o Options) (string, error) {
	if f == XLSX {
		return "", errors.New("xlsx can only be exported to a file")
	}
	var b strings.Builder
	w, err := New(f, &b, o)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if err := WriteResult(w, rs); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return b.String(), nil
}
```

`internal/export/types.go`：

```go
package export

import (
	"regexp"
	"strings"
)

func set(ws ...string) map[string]bool {
	m := make(map[string]bool, len(ws))
	for _, w := range ws {
		m[w] = true
	}
	return m
}

var (
	numericTypes = set("INT", "INTEGER", "TINYINT", "SMALLINT", "MEDIUMINT", "BIGINT", "DECIMAL", "NUMERIC",
		"FLOAT", "DOUBLE", "REAL", "YEAR", "INT2", "INT4", "INT8", "FLOAT4", "FLOAT8", "OID")
	binaryTypes = set("BINARY", "VARBINARY", "BLOB", "TINYBLOB", "MEDIUMBLOB", "LONGBLOB", "BIT", "GEOMETRY")
	jsonTypes   = set("JSON", "JSONB")
	numberRe    = regexp.MustCompile(`^-?\d+(\.\d+)?([eE][-+]?\d+)?$`)
)

func normType(t string) string { return strings.TrimPrefix(strings.ToUpper(t), "UNSIGNED ") }

func isNumericType(t string) bool { return numericTypes[normType(t)] }
func isBinaryType(t string) bool  { return binaryTypes[normType(t)] }
func isJSONType(t string) bool    { return jsonTypes[normType(t)] }
func isBoolType(t string) bool    { return normType(t) == "BOOL" }
func isNumber(s string) bool      { return numberRe.MatchString(s) }
```

`internal/export/text.go`：

```go
package export

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"encoding/csv"
	"encoding/json"
	"io"
	"strings"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

type delimited struct {
	cw       *csv.Writer
	noHeader bool
}

func newDelimited(w io.Writer, comma rune, o Options) *delimited {
	cw := csv.NewWriter(w)
	cw.Comma = comma
	return &delimited{cw: cw, noHeader: o.NoHeader}
}

func (d *delimited) WriteHeader(cols []db.ColumnInfo) error {
	if d.noHeader {
		return nil
	}
	names := make([]string, len(cols))
	for i, c := range cols {
		names[i] = c.Name
	}
	return d.cw.Write(names)
}

func (d *delimited) WriteRow(row []db.Value) error {
	rec := make([]string, len(row))
	for i, v := range row {
		rec[i] = v.Text // NULL is an empty field
	}
	return d.cw.Write(rec)
}

func (d *delimited) Close() error {
	d.cw.Flush()
	return d.cw.Error()
}

type jsonWriter struct {
	w    *bufio.Writer
	cols []db.ColumnInfo
	keys []string
	n    int
}

func newJSON(w io.Writer) *jsonWriter { return &jsonWriter{w: bufio.NewWriter(w)} }

func (j *jsonWriter) WriteHeader(cols []db.ColumnInfo) error {
	j.cols = cols
	j.keys = make([]string, len(cols))
	for i, c := range cols {
		j.keys[i] = jsonString(c.Name)
	}
	return nil
}

func (j *jsonWriter) WriteRow(row []db.Value) error {
	if j.n == 0 {
		j.w.WriteString("[\n  {")
	} else {
		j.w.WriteString(",\n  {")
	}
	j.n++
	for i, v := range row {
		if i > 0 {
			j.w.WriteString(", ")
		}
		j.w.WriteString(j.keys[i])
		j.w.WriteString(": ")
		j.w.WriteString(jsonValue(j.cols[i], v))
	}
	_, err := j.w.WriteString("}")
	return err
}

func (j *jsonWriter) Close() error {
	if j.n == 0 {
		j.w.WriteString("[]\n")
	} else {
		j.w.WriteString("\n]\n")
	}
	return j.w.Flush()
}

func jsonValue(c db.ColumnInfo, v db.Value) string {
	switch {
	case v.Null:
		return "null"
	case isNumericType(c.DatabaseType) && isNumber(v.Text):
		return v.Text
	case isBoolType(c.DatabaseType) && (v.Text == "t" || v.Text == "f"):
		if v.Text == "t" {
			return "true"
		}
		return "false"
	case isJSONType(c.DatabaseType) && json.Valid([]byte(v.Text)):
		var b bytes.Buffer
		if err := json.Compact(&b, []byte(v.Text)); err == nil {
			return b.String()
		}
	}
	return jsonString(v.Text)
}

// jsonString encodes s without escaping <, > and &.
func jsonString(s string) string {
	var b bytes.Buffer
	enc := json.NewEncoder(&b)
	enc.SetEscapeHTML(false)
	_ = enc.Encode(s)
	return strings.TrimSuffix(b.String(), "\n")
}

type markdown struct{ w *bufio.Writer }

func newMarkdown(w io.Writer) *markdown { return &markdown{w: bufio.NewWriter(w)} }

func (m *markdown) line(cells []string) error {
	_, err := m.w.WriteString("| " + strings.Join(cells, " | ") + " |\n")
	return err
}

func (m *markdown) WriteHeader(cols []db.ColumnInfo) error {
	names := make([]string, len(cols))
	rule := make([]string, len(cols))
	for i, c := range cols {
		names[i] = mdCell(c.Name)
		rule[i] = "---"
	}
	if err := m.line(names); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return m.line(rule)
}

func (m *markdown) WriteRow(row []db.Value) error {
	cells := make([]string, len(row))
	for i, v := range row {
		if v.Null {
			cells[i] = "NULL"
		} else {
			cells[i] = mdCell(v.Text)
		}
	}
	return m.line(cells)
}

func (m *markdown) Close() error { return m.w.Flush() }

var mdEscaper = strings.NewReplacer("|", `\|`, "\r\n", "<br>", "\n", "<br>", "\r", "", "\t", " ")

func mdCell(s string) string { return mdEscaper.Replace(s) }
```

### Task 9: 导出：INSERT 语句、xlsx、文件

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/export/sqlinsert.go`, `internal/export/xlsx.go`, `internal/export/file.go`
- Modify: `internal/export/export.go`（`New` 增加 `SQL`、`XLSX`），`go.mod`、`go.sum`（`go get github.com/xuri/excelize/v2@v2.11.0`）
- Test: `internal/export/sqlinsert_test.go`, `internal/export/xlsx_test.go`, `internal/export/file_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 8 的 `Writer`、`Options`、`isNumericType`/`isBinaryType`/`isNumber`。
- Produces:
  - `New(SQL, …)`：每行一条 `INSERT INTO <table> (<cols>) VALUES (…);`；`Options.Table` 为空时表名为 `query`
  - `New(XLSX, …)`：单个 `Sheet1`，首行列名；最多 1,048,576 行（含表头），超出返回错误
  - `func FileName(base string, f Format, now time.Time) string`
  - `func ToFile(path string, f Format, o Options, fill func(Writer) error) error`（目标已存在时报错，不覆盖；失败时删除半成品；`fill` 不调用 `Close`）
  - `func WriteRows(w Writer, rs *db.ResultSet) error`（写表头和全部行，不 `Close`）

**INSERT 字面量规则：** NULL → `NULL`；数值列且文本是合法数字 → 原样；MySQL 二进制列且文本以 `0x` 开头 → 原样（驱动已把二进制显示为十六进制）；其余 → 单引号字符串，`'` 写成 `''`，MySQL 另外把 `\` 写成 `\\`。标识符用驱动的引号（MySQL 反引号，PG 双引号）并转义。

```go
package export

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

func TestInsertMySQL(t *testing.T) {
	rs := &db.ResultSet{
		Columns: []db.ColumnInfo{{Name: "id", DatabaseType: "INT"}, {Name: "name", DatabaseType: "VARCHAR"},
			{Name: "bin", DatabaseType: "VARBINARY"}, {Name: "my`col", DatabaseType: "TEXT"}},
		Rows: [][]db.Value{
			{{Text: "1"}, {Text: "O'Brien"}, {Text: "0xDEAD"}, {Text: `C:\dir`}},
			{{Text: "2"}, {Null: true}, {Null: true}, {Text: ""}},
		},
	}
	want := "INSERT INTO `app`.`users` (`id`, `name`, `bin`, `my``col`) VALUES (1, 'O''Brien', 0xDEAD, 'C:\\\\dir');\n" +
		"INSERT INTO `app`.`users` (`id`, `name`, `bin`, `my``col`) VALUES (2, NULL, NULL, '');\n"
	got := encode(t, SQL, rs, Options{Driver: "mysql", Table: []string{"app", "users"}})
	if got != want {
		t.Fatalf("got:\n%s\nwant:\n%s", got, want)
	}
}

func TestInsertPostgres(t *testing.T) {
	rs := &db.ResultSet{
		Columns: []db.ColumnInfo{{Name: "id", DatabaseType: "numeric"}, {Name: "b", DatabaseType: "bytea"}, {Name: "note", DatabaseType: "text"}},
		Rows: [][]db.Value{
			{{Text: "NaN"}, {Text: `\xdead`}, {Text: `C:\dir 'x'`}},
		},
	}
	want := `INSERT INTO "app"."Users" ("id", "b", "note") VALUES ('NaN', '\xdead', 'C:\dir ''x''');` + "\n"
	got := encode(t, SQL, rs, Options{Driver: "postgres", Table: []string{"app", "Users"}})
	if got != want {
		t.Fatalf("got:\n%s\nwant:\n%s", got, want)
	}
}

func TestInsertDefaultTable(t *testing.T) {
	rs := &db.ResultSet{Columns: []db.ColumnInfo{{Name: "a"}}, Rows: [][]db.Value{{{Text: "x"}}}}
	if got := encode(t, SQL, rs, Options{Driver: "postgres"}); got != `INSERT INTO "query" ("a") VALUES ('x');`+"\n" {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", got)
	}
}
```

```go
package export

import (
	"bytes"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/go-cmp/cmp"
	"github.com/xuri/excelize/v2"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

func TestXLSX(t *testing.T) {
	rs := &db.ResultSet{
		Columns: []db.ColumnInfo{{Name: "id", DatabaseType: "INT"}, {Name: "name", DatabaseType: "VARCHAR"}},
		Rows: [][]db.Value{
			{{Text: "1"}, {Text: "李雷"}},
			{{Text: "12345678901234567890"}, {Null: true}},
		},
	}
	var buf bytes.Buffer
	w, err := New(XLSX, &buf, Options{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := WriteResult(w, rs); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f, err := excelize.OpenReader(&buf)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = f.Close() }()
	rows, err := f.GetRows("Sheet1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := [][]string{{"id", "name"}, {"1", "李雷"}, {"12345678901234567890"}}
	if diff := cmp.Diff(want, rows); diff != "" {
		t.Fatalf("(-want +got):\n%s", diff)
	}
}
```

```go
package export

import (
	"errors"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

func TestFileName(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 10, 13, 15, 4, 5, 0, time.UTC)
	cases := map[string]string{
		"users":        "users-20261013-150405.csv",
		"app/users v2": "app_users_v2-20261013-150405.csv",
		"":             "query-20261013-150405.csv",
		"李雷":           "__-20261013-150405.csv",
	}
	for base, want := range cases {
		if got := FileName(base, CSV, now); got != want {
			t.Errorf("FileName(%q) = %q, want %q", base, got, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestToFile(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "out.csv")
	rs := &db.ResultSet{Columns: []db.ColumnInfo{{Name: "a"}}, Rows: [][]db.Value{{{Text: "1"}}}}
	fill := func(w Writer) error { return WriteRows(w, rs) }
	if err := ToFile(path, CSV, Options{}, fill); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	data, _ := os.ReadFile(path)
	if string(data) != "a\n1\n" {
		t.Fatalf("content = %q", data)
	}
	if err := ToFile(path, CSV, Options{}, fill); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("existing files are not overwritten")
	}
	if data, _ := os.ReadFile(path); string(data) != "a\n1\n" {
		t.Fatal("existing file was modified")
	}

	broken := filepath.Join(dir, "broken.csv")
	boom := errors.New("connection reset")
	if err := ToFile(broken, CSV, Options{}, func(Writer) error { return boom }); !errors.Is(err, boom) {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(broken); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		t.Fatal("partial file must be removed")
	}
}
```

`internal/export/sqlinsert.go`：

```go
package export

import (
	"bufio"
	"io"
	"strings"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

type insertWriter struct {
	w      *bufio.Writer
	driver string
	table  []string
	cols   []db.ColumnInfo
	head   string
}

func newInsert(w io.Writer, o Options) *insertWriter {
	table := o.Table
	if len(table) == 0 {
		table = []string{"query"}
	}
	return &insertWriter{w: bufio.NewWriter(w), driver: o.Driver, table: table}
}

func (i *insertWriter) ident(s string) string {
	if i.driver == "postgres" {
		return db.Quote(s, '"')
	}
	return db.Quote(s, '`')
}

func (i *insertWriter) WriteHeader(cols []db.ColumnInfo) error {
	i.cols = cols
	parts := make([]string, len(i.table))
	for k, p := range i.table {
		parts[k] = i.ident(p)
	}
	names := make([]string, len(cols))
	for k, c := range cols {
		names[k] = i.ident(c.Name)
	}
	i.head = "INSERT INTO " + strings.Join(parts, ".") + " (" + strings.Join(names, ", ") + ") VALUES ("
	return nil
}

func (i *insertWriter) WriteRow(row []db.Value) error {
	vals := make([]string, len(row))
	for k, v := range row {
		vals[k] = i.literal(i.cols[k], v)
	}
	_, err := i.w.WriteString(i.head + strings.Join(vals, ", ") + ");\n")
	return err
}

func (i *insertWriter) literal(c db.ColumnInfo, v db.Value) string {
	switch {
	case v.Null:
		return "NULL"
	case isNumericType(c.DatabaseType) && isNumber(v.Text):
		return v.Text
	case i.driver == "mysql" && isBinaryType(c.DatabaseType) && strings.HasPrefix(v.Text, "0x"):
		return v.Text
	}
	s := v.Text
	if i.driver == "mysql" {
		s = strings.ReplaceAll(s, `\`, `\\`)
	}
	return "'" + strings.ReplaceAll(s, "'", "''") + "'"
}

func (i *insertWriter) Close() error { return i.w.Flush() }
```

`internal/export/xlsx.go`：

```go
package export

import (
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/xuri/excelize/v2"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

const (
	xlsxMaxRows      = 1048576
	xlsxMaxCellChars = 32767
	// Larger integers lose precision as float64, so they stay text.
	xlsxMaxDigits = 15
)

type xlsxWriter struct {
	out  io.Writer
	f    *excelize.File
	sw   *excelize.StreamWriter
	cols []db.ColumnInfo
	row  int
}

func newXLSX(w io.Writer) (*xlsxWriter, error) {
	f := excelize.NewFile()
	sw, err := f.NewStreamWriter("Sheet1")
	if err != nil {
		_ = f.Close()
		return nil, err
	}
	return &xlsxWriter{out: w, f: f, sw: sw}, nil
}

func (x *xlsxWriter) setRow(vals []any) error {
	x.row++
	if x.row > xlsxMaxRows {
		return fmt.Errorf("xlsx holds at most %d rows", xlsxMaxRows)
	}
	cell, err := excelize.CoordinatesToCellName(1, x.row)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return x.sw.SetRow(cell, vals)
}

func (x *xlsxWriter) WriteHeader(cols []db.ColumnInfo) error {
	x.cols = cols
	vals := make([]any, len(cols))
	for i, c := range cols {
		vals[i] = c.Name
	}
	return x.setRow(vals)
}

func (x *xlsxWriter) WriteRow(row []db.Value) error {
	vals := make([]any, len(row))
	for i, v := range row {
		vals[i] = x.cell(x.cols[i], v)
	}
	return x.setRow(vals)
}

func (x *xlsxWriter) cell(c db.ColumnInfo, v db.Value) any {
	if v.Null {
		return nil
	}
	if isNumericType(c.DatabaseType) && isNumber(v.Text) && len(v.Text) <= xlsxMaxDigits {
		if f, err := strconv.ParseFloat(v.Text, 64); err == nil {
			return f
		}
	}
	if r := []rune(v.Text); len(r) > xlsxMaxCellChars {
		return string(r[:xlsxMaxCellChars])
	}
	return v.Text
}

func (x *xlsxWriter) Close() error {
	defer func() { _ = x.f.Close() }()
	if err := x.sw.Flush(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, err := x.f.WriteTo(x.out)
	return err
}
```

`internal/export/file.go`：

```go
package export

import (
	"errors"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

// FileName builds "<base>-<YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS>.<ext>"; characters other than
// [A-Za-z0-9_.-] in base become "_" and an empty base becomes "query".
func FileName(base string, f Format, now time.Time) string {
	if base == "" {
		base = "query"
	}
	safe := strings.Map(func(r rune) rune {
		switch {
		case r >= 'a' && r <= 'z', r >= 'A' && r <= 'Z', r >= '0' && r <= '9', r == '_', r == '.', r == '-':
			return r
		}
		return '_'
	}, base)
	return safe + "-" + now.Format("20060102-150405") + "." + string(f)
}

// ToFile creates path (failing if it exists), writes through fill and
// closes the writer. On any error the partial file is removed.
func ToFile(path string, f Format, o Options, fill func(Writer) error) (err error) {
	file, err := os.OpenFile(path, os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL|os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer func() {
		err = errors.Join(err, file.Close())
		if err != nil {
			_ = os.Remove(path)
		}
	}()
	w, err := New(f, file, o)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := fill(w); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return w.Close()
}
```

`fill` 只写表头和行、**不调用** `Close`（由 `ToFile` 关闭）。在 `export.go` 中新增 `WriteRows`，并让 `WriteResult` 复用它：

```go
// WriteRows writes rs through w without closing it (for ToFile's fill).
func WriteRows(w Writer, rs *db.ResultSet) error {
	if err := w.WriteHeader(rs.Columns); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, r := range rs.Rows {
		if err := w.WriteRow(r); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// WriteResult writes rs through w and closes w.
func WriteResult(w Writer, rs *db.ResultSet) error {
	if err := WriteRows(w, rs); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return w.Close()
}
```

**Interfaces:**

  - 动作：`Cancel`、`OpenConsole`、`NewConnection`、`History`、`ExternalEdit`、`InsertMode`、`Append`、`RunStatement`、`RunAll`、`FocusResults`、`FocusEditor`、`ToggleMessages`、`LoadMore`、`Search`、`SearchNext`、`SearchPrev`、`ScrollLeft`、`ScrollRight`、`YankCell`、`YankRow`
  - `func CopyAs(format string) Action`、`func CopyFormat(a Action) (string, bool)`、`func ExportAs(format string) Action`、`func ExportFormat(a Action) (string, bool)`（format 用 `export.Format` 的字符串值：`tsv` `csv` `json` `md` `sql` `xlsx`）

**绑定表（新增/变更）：**

| 上下文 | 按键 | 动作 |
|---|---|---|
| Global | `ctrl+c` | `Cancel`（**替换**原来的 `Quit`） |
| Global | `<leader>c` / `<leader>n` / `<leader>h` | `OpenConsole` / `NewConnection` / `History` |
| Global | `<leader>yt` `yc` `yj` `ym` `yi` | 复制为 TSV / CSV / JSON / Markdown / INSERT |
| Global | `<leader>xc` `xj` `xs` `xx` | 导出为 CSV / JSON / SQL / xlsx |
| Tree | `/` `n` `N` | 搜索 / 下一个 / 上一个 |
| Grid、Results | `/` `n` `N`、`zh` `zl`、`yl` `yy`、`h` `l` `0` `$` `K` + 纵向移动 | 搜索、横向滚动、复制单元格 / 行 |
| Grid | `[` `]` | 上一页 / 下一页（不变） |
| Results | `]`、`M`、`ctrl+k` | 加载更多、切换消息日志、回到编辑器 |
| Console | 纵向移动、`h` `l` `0` `$` | 移动编辑器光标 |
| Console | `i` / `a` | 进入 INSERT（`a` 先右移一格） |
| Console | `enter` / `R` | 执行光标处语句 / 执行全部语句 |
| Console | `ctrl+j`、`M`、`<leader>v` | 去结果区、切换消息日志、用外部编辑器编辑 |

`yy`/`yl` 用两键序列，而不是 spec §5.9 写的 `y`/`yy`：keymap 不允许一个序列是另一个的前缀，`yl` 正是 vim 里「复制一个字符」的写法。

```go
func TestV02Bindings(t *testing.T) {
	k := Default()
	tests := []struct {
		ctx    Context
		seq    []string
		action Action
	}{
		{Tree, []string{"ctrl+c"}, Cancel},
		{Console, []string{"enter"}, RunStatement},
		{Console, []string{"R"}, RunAll},
		{Console, []string{"i"}, InsertMode},
		{Console, []string{"j"}, Down},
		{Console, []string{"ctrl+j"}, FocusResults},
		{Console, []string{"space", "v"}, ExternalEdit},
		{Results, []string{"ctrl+k"}, FocusEditor},
		{Results, []string{"]"}, LoadMore},
		{Results, []string{"y", "y"}, YankRow},
		{Grid, []string{"y", "l"}, YankCell},
		{Grid, []string{"z", "l"}, ScrollRight},
		{Grid, []string{"/"}, Search},
		{Tree, []string{"N"}, SearchPrev},
		{Grid, []string{"space", "y", "c"}, CopyAs("csv")},
		{Results, []string{"space", "x", "x"}, ExportAs("xlsx")},
		{Tree, []string{"space", "c"}, OpenConsole},
		{Tree, []string{"space", "n"}, NewConnection},
		{Tree, []string{"space", "h"}, History},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		if a, r := k.Resolve(tt.ctx, tt.seq); a != tt.action || r != Matched {
			t.Errorf("Resolve(%s, %v) = %q, %v; want %q", tt.ctx, tt.seq, a, r, tt.action)
		}
	}
	if _, r := k.Resolve(Console, []string{"space", "y"}); r != Pending {
		t.Error("<space>y waits for the format key")
	}
	if a, _ := k.Resolve(Tree, []string{"ctrl+c"}); a == Quit {
		t.Error("ctrl+c must not quit")
	}
}

func TestCopyAndExportActions(t *testing.T) {
	if f, ok := CopyFormat(CopyAs("md")); !ok || f != "md" {
		t.Fatalf("CopyFormat = %q, %v", f, ok)
	}
	if f, ok := ExportFormat(ExportAs("sql")); !ok || f != "sql" {
		t.Fatalf("ExportFormat = %q, %v", f, ok)
	}
	if _, ok := CopyFormat(ExportAs("csv")); ok {
		t.Fatal("an export action is not a copy action")
	}
	if _, ok := ExportFormat(Quit); ok {
		t.Fatal("Quit is not an export action")
	}
}
```

```go
	InsertMode     Action = "editor.insert"

	RunAll         Action = "sql.runall"

	FocusEditor    Action = "console.editor"
```

```go
const (
	copyPrefix   = "copy."
	exportPrefix = "export."
)

// CopyAs is the action that copies the result in format (e.g. "csv").
func CopyAs(format string) Action { return Action(copyPrefix + format) }

// CopyFormat extracts the format from a CopyAs action.
func CopyFormat(a Action) (string, bool) { return strings.CutPrefix(string(a), copyPrefix) }

// ExportAs is the action that exports the result in format (e.g. "xlsx").
func ExportAs(format string) Action { return Action(exportPrefix + format) }

// ExportFormat extracts the format from an ExportAs action.
func ExportFormat(a Action) (string, bool) { return strings.CutPrefix(string(a), exportPrefix) }
```

`internal/ui/keymap/defaults.go` 整体替换为：

```go
package keymap

import "strconv"

func keys(k ...string) []string { return k }

// DefaultBindings is the built-in keymap. See spec §6.
func DefaultBindings() []Binding {
	b := []Binding{
		{Global, keys("tab"), FocusNext, "Next pane"},
		{Global, keys("shift+tab"), FocusPrev, "Previous pane"},
		{Global, keys("ctrl+h"), FocusLeft, "Focus tree"},
		{Global, keys("ctrl+l"), FocusRight, "Focus tabs"},
		{Global, keys("?"), Help, "Help"},
		{Global, keys(":"), CommandLine, "Command line"},
		{Global, keys("ctrl+c"), Cancel, "Cancel query"},
		{Global, keys(LeaderToken, "b"), ToggleTree, "Toggle tree"},
		{Global, keys(LeaderToken, "s"), OpenStructure, "Open structure"},
		{Global, keys(LeaderToken, "r"), Refresh, "Refresh"},
		{Global, keys(LeaderToken, "c"), OpenConsole, "Open console"},
		{Global, keys(LeaderToken, "n"), NewConnection, "New connection"},
		{Global, keys(LeaderToken, "h"), History, "SQL history"},
	}
	for _, c := range []struct{ key, format, desc string }{
		{"t", "tsv", "Copy as TSV"}, {"c", "csv", "Copy as CSV"}, {"j", "json", "Copy as JSON"},
		{"m", "md", "Copy as Markdown"}, {"i", "sql", "Copy as INSERT"},
	} {
		b = append(b, Binding{Global, keys(LeaderToken, "y", c.key), CopyAs(c.format), c.desc})
	}
	for _, e := range []struct{ key, format, desc string }{
		{"c", "csv", "Export as CSV"}, {"j", "json", "Export as JSON"},
		{"s", "sql", "Export as SQL"}, {"x", "xlsx", "Export as xlsx"},
	} {
		b = append(b, Binding{Global, keys(LeaderToken, "x", e.key), ExportAs(e.format), e.desc})
	}
	for n := 1; n <= 9; n++ {
		b = append(b, Binding{Global, keys(strconv.Itoa(n)), GotoTab(n), "Go to tab " + strconv.Itoa(n)})
	}

	b = append(b, vertical(Tree)...)
	b = append(b, search(Tree)...)
	b = append(b,
		Binding{Tree, keys("l"), Expand, "Expand / open"},
		Binding{Tree, keys("enter"), Expand, "Expand / open"},
		Binding{Tree, keys("right"), Expand, "Expand / open"},
		Binding{Tree, keys("h"), Collapse, "Collapse / parent"},
		Binding{Tree, keys("left"), Collapse, "Collapse / parent"},
		Binding{Tree, keys("p"), Password, "Enter password"},
	)

	b = append(b, gridBindings(Grid)...)
	b = append(b,
		Binding{Grid, keys("["), PrevPage, "Previous page"},
		Binding{Grid, keys("]"), NextPage, "Next page"},
	)

	b = append(b, gridBindings(Results)...)
	b = append(b,
		Binding{Results, keys("]"), LoadMore, "Load more rows"},
		Binding{Results, keys("M"), ToggleMessages, "Toggle messages"},
		Binding{Results, keys("ctrl+k"), FocusEditor, "Focus editor"},
	)

	b = append(b, vertical(Console)...)
	b = append(b,
		Binding{Console, keys("h"), Left, "Move left"},
		Binding{Console, keys("left"), Left, "Move left"},
		Binding{Console, keys("l"), Right, "Move right"},
		Binding{Console, keys("right"), Right, "Move right"},
		Binding{Console, keys("0"), LineStart, "Line start"},
		Binding{Console, keys("$"), LineEnd, "Line end"},
		Binding{Console, keys("i"), InsertMode, "Insert"},
		Binding{Console, keys("a"), Append, "Append"},
		Binding{Console, keys("enter"), RunStatement, "Run statement under cursor"},
		Binding{Console, keys("R"), RunAll, "Run all statements"},
		Binding{Console, keys("ctrl+j"), FocusResults, "Focus results"},
		Binding{Console, keys("M"), ToggleMessages, "Toggle messages"},
		Binding{Console, keys(LeaderToken, "v"), ExternalEdit, "Edit in $EDITOR"},
	)

	b = append(b, vertical(Structure)...)
	b = append(b,
		Binding{Structure, keys("["), PrevSection, "Previous section"},
		Binding{Structure, keys("]"), NextSection, "Next section"},
	)
	return b
}

func vertical(ctx Context) []Binding {
	return []Binding{
		{ctx, keys("j"), Down, "Move down"},
		{ctx, keys("down"), Down, "Move down"},
		{ctx, keys("k"), Up, "Move up"},
		{ctx, keys("up"), Up, "Move up"},
		{ctx, keys("g", "g"), Top, "Go to top"},
		{ctx, keys("G"), Bottom, "Go to bottom"},
		{ctx, keys("ctrl+d"), HalfPageDown, "Half page down"},
		{ctx, keys("ctrl+u"), HalfPageUp, "Half page up"},
	}
}

func search(ctx Context) []Binding {
	return []Binding{
		{ctx, keys("/"), Search, "Search"},
		{ctx, keys("n"), SearchNext, "Next match"},
		{ctx, keys("N"), SearchPrev, "Previous match"},
	}
}

// gridBindings are shared by the table-data grid and console results.
func gridBindings(ctx Context) []Binding {
	b := vertical(ctx)
	b = append(b, search(ctx)...)
	return append(b,
		Binding{ctx, keys("h"), Left, "Move left"},
		Binding{ctx, keys("left"), Left, "Move left"},
		Binding{ctx, keys("l"), Right, "Move right"},
		Binding{ctx, keys("right"), Right, "Move right"},
		Binding{ctx, keys("0"), LineStart, "First column"},
		Binding{ctx, keys("$"), LineEnd, "Last column"},
		Binding{ctx, keys("z", "h"), ScrollLeft, "Scroll left"},
		Binding{ctx, keys("z", "l"), ScrollRight, "Scroll right"},
		Binding{ctx, keys("y", "l"), YankCell, "Copy cell"},
		Binding{ctx, keys("y", "y"), YankRow, "Copy row"},
		Binding{ctx, keys("K"), CellDetail, "Show cell value"},
	)
}
```

### Task 11: 表格：横向滚动、搜索、取数

**Files:**
- Modify: `internal/ui/panes/grid/grid.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `func (m *Model) ScrollColumns(n int)`：视图左右滚动 n 列，光标被挤出视野时拉回到可见范围
  - `func (m *Model) Find(q string, forward bool) bool`：从当前单元格之后（或之前）按行优先、循环查找文本包含 `q`（不区分大小写）的单元格；NULL 不匹配；找到则移动光标
  - `func (m *Model) Result() *db.ResultSet`
  - `func (m *Model) Row() ([]db.Value, bool)`：光标所在行
  - `func (m *Model) ReplaceResult(rs *db.ResultSet)`：替换数据但保留光标（夹在范围内），供「加载更多」使用

```go
func TestScrollColumns(t *testing.T) {
	m := New(rs(), 40)
	m.SetSize(14, 10) // id(2) + name(5) fit; email(7) does not
	m.ScrollColumns(1)
	if !strings.HasPrefix(view(m, true)[0], "name") {
		t.Fatalf("zl scrolls one column: %q", view(m, true)[0])
	}
	if col, _, _ := m.Cell(); col.Name != "name" {
		t.Fatalf("cursor pulled into view, got %s", col.Name)
	}
	m.ScrollColumns(1)
	if col, _, _ := m.Cell(); col.Name != "email" {
		t.Fatalf("cursor follows, got %s", col.Name)
	}
	m.ScrollColumns(-5)
	if !strings.HasPrefix(view(m, true)[0], "id") {
		t.Fatalf("clamped at the first column: %q", view(m, true)[0])
	}
	if col, _, _ := m.Cell(); col.Name != "name" {
		t.Fatalf("cursor kept inside the view, got %s", col.Name)
	}
	m.ScrollColumns(99)
	if col, _, _ := m.Cell(); col.Name != "note" {
		t.Fatalf("clamped at the last column, got %s", col.Name)
	}
	New(nil, 40).ScrollColumns(1) // must not panic
}

func TestFind(t *testing.T) {
	m := New(rs(), 40)
	m.SetSize(80, 10)
	if !m.Find("BOB", true) {
		t.Fatal("case-insensitive match")
	}
	if _, v, _ := m.Cell(); v.Text != "bob" {
		t.Fatalf("at %q", v.Text)
	}
	if !m.Find("a", true) { // wraps to row 0: "alice"
		t.Fatal("wraps around")
	}
	if _, v, _ := m.Cell(); v.Text != "alice" {
		t.Fatalf("at %q", v.Text)
	}
	// cursor is on alice (0,1); searching backwards wraps to the last match, a@x.com (0,2)
	if !m.Find("a", false) {
		t.Fatal("backward search")
	}
	if _, v, _ := m.Cell(); v.Text != "a@x.com" {
		t.Fatalf("backward at %q", v.Text)
	}
	if m.Find("NULL", true) {
		t.Fatal("NULL cells never match")
	}
	if m.Find("", true) || New(nil, 40).Find("x", true) {
		t.Fatal("empty query or grid")
	}
}

func TestRowAndReplaceResult(t *testing.T) {
	m := New(rs(), 40)
	m.SetSize(80, 10)
	m.Move(1, 1)
	row, ok := m.Row()
	if !ok || row[1].Text != "李雷" {
		t.Fatalf("Row = %v, %v", row, ok)
	}
	bigger := rs()
	bigger.Rows = append(bigger.Rows, []db.Value{{Text: "4"}, {Text: "dan"}, {Null: true}, {Null: true}})
	m.ReplaceResult(bigger)
	if _, v, _ := m.Cell(); v.Text != "李雷" {
		t.Fatalf("cursor kept, at %q", v.Text)
	}
	if m.Result() != bigger || m.RowCount() != 4 {
		t.Fatal("data replaced")
	}
	m.Bottom()
	m.ReplaceResult(rs())
	if _, v, _ := m.Cell(); v.Text != "bob" {
		t.Fatalf("cursor clamped to the last row, at %q", v.Text)
	}
}
```

Expected: 编译失败：`ScrollColumns`、`Find`、`Row`、`ReplaceResult`、`Result` 未定义。

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**（追加到 `internal/ui/panes/grid/grid.go`，import 增加 `"strings"` 已有）

```go
// Result returns the data shown (nil when empty).
func (m *Model) Result() *db.ResultSet { return m.rs }

// Row returns the row under the cursor.
func (m *Model) Row() ([]db.Value, bool) {
	if m.RowCount() == 0 {
		return nil, false
	}
	return m.rs.Rows[m.row], true
}

// ReplaceResult swaps in new data (e.g. more rows of the same query) and
// keeps the cursor where it was, clamped to the new data.
func (m *Model) ReplaceResult(rs *db.ResultSet) {
	row, col, rowOff, colOff := m.row, m.col, m.rowOff, m.colOff
	m.SetResult(rs)
	m.rowOff, m.colOff = rowOff, colOff
	m.row, m.col = row, col
	m.Move(0, 0)
	m.colOff = textutil.Clamp(m.colOff, 0, max(m.colCount()-1, 0))
}

// ScrollColumns scrolls the view n columns right (negative: left) and pulls
// the cursor back into view if it scrolled off.
func (m *Model) ScrollColumns(n int) {
	if m.colCount() == 0 {
		return
	}
	m.colOff = textutil.Clamp(m.colOff+n, 0, m.colCount()-1)
	vis := m.visibleColumns()
	if m.col < m.colOff {
		m.col = m.colOff
	}
	if last := vis[len(vis)-1]; m.col > last {
		m.col = last
	}
}

// Find moves to the next cell (row-major, wrapping) whose text contains q,
// ignoring case; backwards when forward is false. NULL cells never match.
func (m *Model) Find(q string, forward bool) bool {
	rows, cols := m.RowCount(), m.colCount()
	if q == "" || rows == 0 || cols == 0 {
		return false
	}
	q = strings.ToLower(q)
	total := rows * cols
	step := 1
	if !forward {
		step = -1
	}
	start := m.row*cols + m.col
	for i := 1; i <= total; i++ {
		k := ((start+step*i)%total + total) % total
		r, c := k/cols, k%cols
		if v := m.rs.Rows[r][c]; !v.Null && strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(v.Text), q) {
			m.row, m.col = r, c
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}
```

```go
func TestBrokenConnection(t *testing.T) {
	m := fixture()
	m.SetBroken("local", true)
	if v := ansi.Strip(m.View(40, true)); !strings.Contains(v, "disconnected") {
		t.Fatalf("view:\n%s", v)
	}
	m.ResetConn("local")
	if v := ansi.Strip(m.View(40, true)); strings.Contains(v, "disconnected") {
		t.Fatal("ResetConn clears the mark")
	}

	m.AddRoot(&Node{Kind: KindConn, Name: "new", Conn: "new"})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`Node` 增加字段（放在 `Err` 后面）：

```go
	Broken   bool // the connection was lost and reconnecting failed
```

`ResetConn` 中把 `r.Err = ""` 那一行改为同时清 `Broken`：

```go
			r.Children, r.Loaded, r.Expanded, r.Loading, r.Err, r.Broken = nil, false, false, false, "", false
```

新增方法：

```go
// Empty reports whether there are no connections.
func (m *Model) Empty() bool { return len(m.roots) == 0 }

// AddRoot appends a connection and selects it.
func (m *Model) AddRoot(n *Node) {
	m.roots = append(m.roots, n)
	m.selected = n
}

// SetBroken marks the root of conn as disconnected (or clears the mark).
func (m *Model) SetBroken(conn string, broken bool) {
	for _, r := range m.roots {
		if r.Conn == conn {
			r.Broken = broken
		}
	}
}

// Find selects the next visible node (wrapping; backwards when forward is
// false) whose name contains q, ignoring case.
func (m *Model) Find(q string, forward bool) bool {
	rows := m.visible()
	if q == "" || len(rows) == 0 {
		return false
	}
	q = strings.ToLower(q)
	step := 1
	if !forward {
		step = -1
	}
	cur := m.cursor(rows)
	for i := 1; i <= len(rows); i++ {
		k := ((cur+step*i)%len(rows) + len(rows)) % len(rows)
		if strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(rows[k].node.Name), q) {
			m.selected = rows[k].node
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}
```

```go
func newField(s string) field { return field{r: []rune(s)} }
```

`internal/ui/overlay/cmdline.go` 整体替换为：

```go
package overlay

import tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

// CommandMsg is an entered ":" command without the colon.
type CommandMsg struct{ Text string }

// SearchMsg is an entered "/" search without the slash.
type SearchMsg struct{ Text string }

// CmdLine is the vim-style ":" command line or "/" search line drawn in the status bar.
type CmdLine struct {
	prefix string
	input  field
}

// NewCmdLine opens an empty command line.
func NewCmdLine() *CmdLine { return &CmdLine{prefix: ":"} }

// NewSearchLine opens an empty search line.
func NewSearchLine() *CmdLine { return &CmdLine{prefix: "/"} }

func (c *CmdLine) HandleKey(msg tea.KeyPressMsg) (Overlay, tea.Cmd) {
	switch msg.String() {
	case "esc":
		return nil, nil
	case "enter":
		if c.prefix == "/" {
			return nil, emit(SearchMsg{Text: c.input.String()})
		}
		return nil, emit(CommandMsg{Text: c.input.String()})
	case "backspace":
		if c.input.Len() == 0 {
			return nil, nil
		}
	}
	c.input.handle(msg)
	return c, nil
}

// View renders the single status-bar line.
func (c *CmdLine) View(_, _ int) string { return c.prefix + c.input.String() + "█" }
```

`internal/ui/overlay/confirm.go`：

```go
package overlay

import (
	"strings"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/textutil"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/theme"
)

// Confirm asks before something risky. With expect set the user must type it
// exactly; otherwise y confirms. On success it emits ok.
type Confirm struct {
	title  string
	lines  []string
	expect string
	ok     tea.Msg
	input  field
	wrong  bool
}

// NewConfirm builds a confirmation box.
func NewConfirm(title string, lines []string, expect string, ok tea.Msg) *Confirm {
	return &Confirm{title: title, lines: lines, expect: expect, ok: ok}
}

func (c *Confirm) HandleKey(msg tea.KeyPressMsg) (Overlay, tea.Cmd) {
	key := msg.String()
	if key == "esc" {
		return nil, nil
	}
	if c.expect == "" {
		switch key {
		case "y", "Y":
			return nil, emit(c.ok)
		case "n", "N":
			return nil, nil
		}
		return c, nil
	}
	if key == "enter" {
		if c.input.String() == c.expect {
			return nil, emit(c.ok)
		}
		c.wrong = true
		return c, nil
	}
	c.wrong = false
	c.input.handle(msg)
	return c, nil
}

func (c *Confirm) View(width, height int) string {
	w := max(min(width-4, 80), 20)
	var body []string
	for _, l := range c.lines {
		for _, part := range strings.Split(l, "\n") {
			body = append(body, textutil.Wrap(textutil.Sanitize(part), w-2)...)
		}
	}
	body = append(body, "")
	if c.expect == "" {
		body = append(body, theme.Muted.Render("y confirm · n/esc cancel"))
	} else {
		body = append(body, "Type "+theme.Title.Render(textutil.Sanitize(c.expect))+" to continue:",
			"> "+c.input.String()+"█")
		if c.wrong {
			body = append(body, theme.Error.Render("does not match"))
		}
		body = append(body, theme.Muted.Render("enter confirm · esc cancel"))
	}
	h := min(len(body)+2, max(height-2, 3))
	return theme.Box(c.title, strings.Join(body, "\n"), w, h, true)
}
```

`internal/ui/overlay/prompt.go`：

```go
package overlay

import (
	"strings"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/textutil"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/theme"
)

// Prompt asks for one line of text with an editable initial value.
type Prompt struct {
	title, label string
	input        field
	submit       func(string) tea.Msg
}

// NewPrompt builds a prompt; submit turns the entered text into a message.
func NewPrompt(title, label, initial string, submit func(string) tea.Msg) *Prompt {
	return &Prompt{title: title, label: label, input: newField(initial), submit: submit}
}

func (p *Prompt) HandleKey(msg tea.KeyPressMsg) (Overlay, tea.Cmd) {
	switch msg.String() {
	case "esc":
		return nil, nil
	case "enter":
		return nil, emit(p.submit(p.input.String()))
	}
	p.input.handle(msg)
	return p, nil
}

func (p *Prompt) View(width, _ int) string {
	w := max(min(width-4, 80), 20)
	value := textutil.Sanitize(p.input.String()) + "█"
	if room := w - 4; ansi.StringWidth(value) > room {
		value = "…" + ansi.TruncateLeft(value, ansi.StringWidth(value)-room+1, "")
	}
	body := strings.Join([]string{
		p.label,
		"> " + value,
		"",
		theme.Muted.Render("enter ok · esc cancel"),
	}, "\n")
	return theme.Box(p.title, body, w, 6, true)
}
```

`internal/ui/overlay/choice.go`：

```go
package overlay

import (
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/textutil"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/theme"
)

// Choice picks one of a few options.
type Choice struct {
	title   string
	options []string
	cur     int
	pick    func(int) tea.Msg
}

// NewChoice builds a chooser; pick turns the chosen index into a message.
func NewChoice(title string, options []string, pick func(int) tea.Msg) *Choice {
	return &Choice{title: title, options: options, pick: pick}
}

func (c *Choice) HandleKey(msg tea.KeyPressMsg) (Overlay, tea.Cmd) {
	switch key := msg.String(); key {
	case "esc", "q":
		return nil, nil
	case "j", "down":
		c.cur = textutil.Clamp(c.cur+1, 0, len(c.options)-1)
	case "k", "up":
		c.cur = textutil.Clamp(c.cur-1, 0, len(c.options)-1)
	case "enter":
		return nil, emit(c.pick(c.cur))
	default:
		if n, err := strconv.Atoi(key); err == nil && n >= 1 && n <= len(c.options) {
			return nil, emit(c.pick(n - 1))
		}
	}
	return c, nil
}

func (c *Choice) View(width, _ int) string {
	lines := make([]string, 0, len(c.options)+2)
	for i, o := range c.options {
		line := "  " + o
		if i == c.cur {
			line = theme.Selected.Render("> " + o)
		}
		lines = append(lines, line)
	}
	lines = append(lines, "", theme.Muted.Render("j/k move · enter choose · esc cancel"))
	return theme.Box(c.title, strings.Join(lines, "\n"), max(min(width-4, 50), 20), len(lines)+2, true)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`internal/ui/overlay/picker.go`：

```go
package overlay

import (
	"strings"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/textutil"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/theme"
)

// Picker is a filterable list; typing narrows it.
type Picker struct {
	title   string
	items   []string
	filter  field
	matches []int
	cur     int
	offset  int
	pick    func(int) tea.Msg
}

// NewPicker builds a picker; pick receives the index into items.
func NewPicker(title string, items []string, pick func(index int) tea.Msg) *Picker {
	p := &Picker{title: title, items: items, pick: pick}
	p.refilter()
	return p
}

func (p *Picker) refilter() {
	q := strings.ToLower(p.filter.String())
	p.matches = p.matches[:0]
	for i, it := range p.items {
		if strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(it), q) {
			p.matches = append(p.matches, i)
		}
	}
	p.cur, p.offset = 0, 0
}

func (p *Picker) HandleKey(msg tea.KeyPressMsg) (Overlay, tea.Cmd) {
	switch msg.String() {
	case "esc":
		return nil, nil
	case "enter":
		if len(p.matches) == 0 {
			return p, nil
		}
		return nil, emit(p.pick(p.matches[p.cur]))
	case "down", "ctrl+n":
		p.cur = textutil.Clamp(p.cur+1, 0, len(p.matches)-1)
	case "up", "ctrl+p":
		p.cur = textutil.Clamp(p.cur-1, 0, len(p.matches)-1)
	default:
		before := p.filter.String()
		p.filter.handle(msg)
		if p.filter.String() != before {
			p.refilter()
		}
	}
	return p, nil
}

func (p *Picker) View(width, height int) string {
	w := max(min(width-4, 100), 20)
	h := max(min(height-2, 22), 5)
	listH := h - 4 // border, filter line, footer
	if p.cur < p.offset {
		p.offset = p.cur
	}
	if p.cur >= p.offset+listH {
		p.offset = p.cur - listH + 1
	}
	lines := []string{"> " + textutil.Sanitize(p.filter.String()) + "█"}
	if len(p.matches) == 0 {
		lines = append(lines, theme.Muted.Render("(no matches)"))
	}
	for i := p.offset; i < len(p.matches) && i < p.offset+listH; i++ {
		label := textutil.Fit(textutil.Sanitize(p.items[p.matches[i]]), w-2)
		if i == p.cur {
			label = theme.Selected.Render(label)
		}
		lines = append(lines, label)
	}
	body := textutil.PadLines(strings.Join(lines, "\n"), h-3) + "\n" +
		theme.Muted.Render("type to filter · ↑/↓ move · enter pick · esc close")
	return theme.Box(p.title, body, w, h, true)
}
```

`internal/ui/overlay/connform.go`：

```go
package overlay

import (
	"fmt"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/config"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/textutil"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/theme"
)

// ConnFormMsg carries a new connection from the form.
type ConnFormMsg struct {
	Conn     config.Connection
	Password string
	Save     bool
}

type formField struct {
	label   string
	input   field
	choices []string
	choice  int
	secret  bool
}

const (
	ffName = iota
	ffDriver
	ffHost
	ffPort
	ffUser
	ffPassword
	ffDatabase
	ffEnv
	ffSave
)

// ConnForm is the <space>n new-connection form.
type ConnForm struct {
	fields []formField
	cur    int
	err    string
}

// NewConnForm builds an empty form.
func NewConnForm() *ConnForm {
	return &ConnForm{fields: []formField{
		ffName:     {label: "Name"},
		ffDriver:   {label: "Driver", choices: []string{"mysql", "postgres"}},
		ffHost:     {label: "Host", input: newField("localhost")},
		ffPort:     {label: "Port"},
		ffUser:     {label: "User"},
		ffPassword: {label: "Password", secret: true},
		ffDatabase: {label: "Database"},
		ffEnv:      {label: "Env", choices: []string{"dev", "staging", "prod"}},
		ffSave:     {label: "Save password", choices: []string{"yes", "no"}},
	}}
}

func (f *ConnForm) HandleKey(msg tea.KeyPressMsg) (Overlay, tea.Cmd) {
	fd := &f.fields[f.cur]
	switch key := msg.String(); key {
	case "esc":
		return nil, nil
	case "tab", "down":
		f.cur = (f.cur + 1) % len(f.fields)
	case "shift+tab", "up":
		f.cur = (f.cur + len(f.fields) - 1) % len(f.fields)
	case "ctrl+s":
		return f.submit()
	case "enter":
		if f.cur == len(f.fields)-1 {
			return f.submit()
		}
		f.cur++
	default:
		if fd.choices == nil {
			fd.input.handle(msg)
			return f, nil
		}
		switch key {
		case "space", "right", "l":
			fd.choice = (fd.choice + 1) % len(fd.choices)
		case "left", "h":
			fd.choice = (fd.choice + len(fd.choices) - 1) % len(fd.choices)
		}
	}
	return f, nil
}

func (f *ConnForm) text(i int) string { return strings.TrimSpace(f.fields[i].input.String()) }

func (f *ConnForm) value(i int) string { return f.fields[i].choices[f.fields[i].choice] }

func (f *ConnForm) submit() (Overlay, tea.Cmd) {
	port := 0
	if p := f.text(ffPort); p != "" {
		n, err := strconv.Atoi(p)
		if err != nil || n < 1 || n > 65535 {
			f.err = fmt.Sprintf("port must be a number between 1 and 65535, got %q", p)
			return f, nil
		}
		port = n
	}
	c, err := config.Normalize(config.Connection{
		Name: f.text(ffName), Driver: f.value(ffDriver), Host: f.text(ffHost), Port: port,
		User: f.text(ffUser), Database: f.text(ffDatabase), Env: config.Env(f.value(ffEnv)),
	})
	if err != nil {
		f.err = err.Error()
		return f, nil
	}
	return nil, emit(ConnFormMsg{Conn: c, Password: f.fields[ffPassword].input.String(), Save: f.value(ffSave) == "yes"})
}

func (f *ConnForm) View(width, _ int) string {
	w := max(min(width-4, 60), 30)
	lines := make([]string, 0, len(f.fields)+4)
	for i, fd := range f.fields {
		var v string
		switch {
		case fd.choices != nil:
			v = "‹ " + fd.choices[fd.choice] + " ›"
		case fd.secret:
			v = strings.Repeat("•", fd.input.Len())
		default:
			v = textutil.Sanitize(fd.input.String())
		}
		label := fmt.Sprintf("%-14s", fd.label)
		if i == f.cur {
			if fd.choices == nil {
				v += "█"
			}
			label = theme.ActiveTab.Render(label)
		}
		lines = append(lines, label+v)
	}
	lines = append(lines, "")
	if f.err != "" {
		lines = append(lines, theme.Error.Render(textutil.Sanitize(f.err)))
	}
	lines = append(lines, theme.Muted.Render("tab next · enter next/save · ctrl+s save · esc cancel"))
	return theme.Box("New connection", strings.Join(lines, "\n"), w, len(lines)+2, true)
}
```

`config.Normalize` 对空名字返回 `connection: name is required`，测试据此断言。

```go
package editor

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/uitest"
)

func typeText(m *Model, s string) {
	for _, k := range uitest.Type(s) {
		m.Update(k)
	}
}

func TestInsertModeEditsAndNormalModeDoesNot(t *testing.T) {
	m := New()
	m.SetSize(40, 6)
	typeText(m, "x")
	if m.Value() != "" {
		t.Fatal("NORMAL mode ignores typing")
	}
	m.EnterInsert()
	typeText(m, "select 1;")
	m.Update(uitest.Key("enter"))
	typeText(m, "select 2;")
	if got := m.Value(); got != "select 1;\nselect 2;" {
		t.Fatalf("value = %q", got)
	}
	if m.Offset() != len(m.Value()) {
		t.Fatalf("offset = %d", m.Offset())
	}
	m.Update(tea.PasteMsg{Content: " -- x"})
	if !strings.HasSuffix(m.Value(), "select 2; -- x") {
		t.Fatalf("paste: %q", m.Value())
	}
	m.ExitInsert()
	if m.Insert() {
		t.Fatal("ExitInsert")
	}
	typeText(m, "zz")
	if strings.Contains(m.Value(), "zz") {
		t.Fatal("NORMAL mode ignores typing")
	}
}

func TestMovesAndOffset(t *testing.T) {
	m := New()
	m.SetSize(40, 6)
	m.SetValue("select 1;\nselect 2;")
	m.Up(1)
	m.LineStart()
	if m.Offset() != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("start offset = %d", m.Offset())
	}
	m.Right()
	m.Right()
	if m.Offset() != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("right offset = %d", m.Offset())
	}
	m.Left()
	if m.Offset() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("left offset = %d", m.Offset())
	}
	m.Down(1)
	m.LineEnd()
	if m.Offset() != len(m.Value()) {
		t.Fatalf("end offset = %d", m.Offset())
	}
	m.Top()
	if m.Offset() != 0 {
		t.Fatal("Top")
	}
	m.Bottom()
	if m.Offset() != len(m.Value()) {
		t.Fatal("Bottom")
	}
}

func TestOffsetCountsBytes(t *testing.T) {
	m := New()
	m.SetSize(40, 6)
	m.SetValue("select '李雷';")
	m.Top()
	for range 9 { // past "select '李"
		m.Right()
	}
	if got, want := m.Offset(), len("select '李"); got != want {
		t.Fatalf("offset = %d, want %d", got, want)
	}
}

func TestViewFitsAndShowsText(t *testing.T) {
	m := New()
	m.SetSize(30, 4)
	m.SetValue("select 1")
	v := ansi.Strip(m.View())
	if !strings.Contains(v, "select 1") {
		t.Fatalf("view:\n%s", v)
	}
	for _, l := range strings.Split(v, "\n") {
		if ansi.StringWidth(l) > 30 {
			t.Fatalf("line wider than 30: %q", l)
		}
	}
	m.SetSize(0, 0)
	_ = m.View()
}
```

```go
// Package editor is the console's SQL editor: a Bubbles textarea with a
// NORMAL mode (cursor moves only) and an INSERT mode (textarea gets keys).
package editor

import (
	"strings"

	"charm.land/bubbles/v2/textarea"
	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/textutil"
)

// Model wraps a textarea.
type Model struct {
	ta     textarea.Model
	insert bool
	height int
}

// New returns an empty editor in NORMAL mode.
func New() *Model {
	ta := textarea.New()
	ta.ShowLineNumbers = true
	ta.Prompt = ""
	ta.CharLimit = 0
	ta.MaxHeight = 9999
	ta.Placeholder = "i to write SQL · enter runs the statement under the cursor"
	st := ta.Styles()
	st.Cursor.Blink = false
	ta.SetStyles(st)
	_ = ta.Focus() // always focused so the cursor is drawn; insert decides who gets keys
	return &Model{ta: ta}
}

// SetSize sets the drawing area.
func (m *Model) SetSize(w, h int) {
	m.height = max(h, 0)
	m.ta.SetWidth(max(w, 1))
	m.ta.SetHeight(max(h, 1))
}

func (m *Model) Value() string { return m.ta.Value() }

// SetValue replaces the text and puts the cursor at the end.
func (m *Model) SetValue(s string) { m.ta.SetValue(s) }

// InsertString inserts s at the cursor.
func (m *Model) InsertString(s string) { m.ta.InsertString(s) }

func (m *Model) Insert() bool { return m.insert }
func (m *Model) EnterInsert() { m.insert = true }
func (m *Model) ExitInsert()  { m.insert = false }

// Update forwards key presses and pastes to the textarea in INSERT mode.
func (m *Model) Update(msg tea.Msg) tea.Cmd {
	if !m.insert {
		return nil
	}
	var cmd tea.Cmd
	m.ta, cmd = m.ta.Update(msg)
	return cmd
}

func (m *Model) Up(n int) {
	for range n {
		m.ta.CursorUp()
	}
}

func (m *Model) Down(n int) {
	for range n {
		m.ta.CursorDown()
	}
}

func (m *Model) Left()      { m.ta.SetCursorColumn(m.ta.Column() - 1) }
func (m *Model) Right()     { m.ta.SetCursorColumn(m.ta.Column() + 1) }
func (m *Model) LineStart() { m.ta.CursorStart() }
func (m *Model) LineEnd()   { m.ta.CursorEnd() }
func (m *Model) Top()       { m.ta.MoveToBegin() }
func (m *Model) Bottom()    { m.ta.MoveToEnd() }

// HalfPage moves half the visible height in direction dir (+1 / -1).
func (m *Model) HalfPage(dir int) {
	n := max(m.height/2, 1)
	if dir > 0 {
		m.Down(n)
	} else {
		m.Up(n)
	}
}

// Offset is the cursor position as a byte offset into Value().
func (m *Model) Offset() int {
	lines := strings.Split(m.ta.Value(), "\n")
	row := textutil.Clamp(m.ta.Line(), 0, len(lines)-1)
	off := 0
	for _, l := range lines[:row] {
		off += len(l) + 1
	}
	r := []rune(lines[row])
	return off + len(string(r[:textutil.Clamp(m.ta.Column(), 0, len(r))]))
}

func (m *Model) View() string {
	if m.height == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	return m.ta.View()
}
```

### Task 16: runner：保护检查与依次执行

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/runner/runner.go`
- Test: `internal/runner/runner_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2/3 的 `sqlx.Statement`、`sqlx.Analyze`、`sqlx.Dialect`、`sqlx.Write`/`sqlx.DDL`；Task 4 的 `db.Querier`、`db.ErrStop`。
- Produces:
  - `type Verdict int`，常量 `Allow`、`Reject`、`Confirm`
  - `type Check struct { Verdict Verdict; Reason, Expect, SQL string }`
  - `func Guard(stmts []sqlx.Statement, d sqlx.Dialect, readOnly bool) Check`
  - `type Result struct { SQL string; Rows *db.ResultSet; Truncated bool; Affected int64; Duration time.Duration; Err error }`
  - `func Run(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, stmts []sqlx.Statement, d sqlx.Dialect, maxRows int) []Result`
  - `func RunOne(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, sql string, d sqlx.Dialect, maxRows int) Result`

**规则：** 只读连接上任一语句为 `Write`/`DDL` → `Reject`（拒绝优先于确认）；否则第一条 `MissingWhere` 的语句 → `Confirm`，`Expect` 为目标表名（无法确定时为 `yes`）。`Run` 按顺序执行，遇到错误或 context 已取消就停止；`ReturnsRows` 的语句用 `Stream` 读取，最多保留 `maxRows` 行，多出则 `Truncated` 并放弃剩余结果；其余语句用 `Exec`。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试** `internal/runner/runner_test.go`

```go
package runner

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/sqlx"
)

type fakeQ struct {
	rows     int
	affected int64
	fail     map[string]error
	ran      []string
}

func (f *fakeQ) Query(ctx context.Context, q string, _ ...any) (*db.ResultSet, error) {
	return db.Collect(func(oc func([]db.ColumnInfo), or func([]db.Value) error) error { return f.Stream(ctx, q, oc, or) })
}

func (f *fakeQ) Exec(_ context.Context, q string, _ ...any) (db.ExecResult, error) {
	f.ran = append(f.ran, q)
	return db.ExecResult{RowsAffected: f.affected}, f.fail[q]
}

func (f *fakeQ) Stream(_ context.Context, q string, onColumns func([]db.ColumnInfo), onRow func([]db.Value) error) error {
	f.ran = append(f.ran, q)
	if err := f.fail[q]; err != nil {
		return err
	}
	onColumns([]db.ColumnInfo{{Name: "n"}})
	for i := range f.rows {
		if err := onRow([]db.Value{{Text: strconv.Itoa(i)}}); err != nil {
			if errors.Is(err, db.ErrStop) {
				return nil
			}
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func stmts(src string) []sqlx.Statement { return sqlx.Split(src, sqlx.MySQL) }

func TestGuard(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		src      string
		readOnly bool
		verdict  Verdict
		expect   string
	}{
		{"select 1", true, Allow, ""},
		{"delete from t where id = 1", false, Allow, ""},
		{"delete from t where id = 1", true, Reject, ""},
		{"create table x (id int)", true, Reject, ""},
		{"set @a = 1", true, Allow, ""},
		{"update t set a = 1", false, Confirm, "t"},
		{"delete", false, Confirm, "yes"},
		{"select 1; update t set a = 1; delete from u", false, Confirm, "t"},
		{"update t set a = 1; insert into u values (1)", true, Reject, ""},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		got := Guard(stmts(c.src), sqlx.MySQL, c.readOnly)
		if got.Verdict != c.verdict || got.Expect != c.expect {
			t.Errorf("Guard(%q, ro=%v) = %+v; want verdict %d expect %q", c.src, c.readOnly, got, c.verdict, c.expect)
		}
	}
	ro := Guard(stmts("delete from t where id = 1"), sqlx.MySQL, true)
	if !strings.Contains(ro.Reason, "read-only") || ro.SQL != "delete from t where id = 1" {
		t.Fatalf("reject = %+v", ro)
	}
	cf := Guard(stmts("update t set a = 1"), sqlx.MySQL, false)
	if !strings.Contains(cf.Reason, "WHERE") || cf.SQL != "update t set a = 1" {
		t.Fatalf("confirm = %+v", cf)
	}
}

func TestRunInOrderAndStopOnError(t *testing.T) {
	q := &fakeQ{rows: 2, affected: 3, fail: map[string]error{"bad": errors.New("syntax error")}}
	res := Run(context.Background(), q, stmts("select 1; update t set a = 1 where id = 2; bad; select 2"), sqlx.MySQL, 10)
	if len(res) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("results = %d (stops after the error)", len(res))
	}
	if res[0].Rows == nil || len(res[0].Rows.Rows) != 2 || res[0].Truncated {
		t.Fatalf("select: %+v", res[0])
	}
	if res[1].Rows != nil || res[1].Affected != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("update: %+v", res[1])
	}
	if res[2].Err == nil || res[2].SQL != "bad" {
		t.Fatalf("bad: %+v", res[2])
	}
	if strings.Join(q.ran, "|") != "select 1|update t set a = 1 where id = 2|bad" {
		t.Fatalf("ran = %v", q.ran)
	}
}

func TestRunTruncates(t *testing.T) {
	q := &fakeQ{rows: 5}
	r := RunOne(context.Background(), q, "select * from big", sqlx.MySQL, 3)
	if r.Err != nil || len(r.Rows.Rows) != 3 || !r.Truncated {
		t.Fatalf("result = %+v", r)
	}
	exact := RunOne(context.Background(), &fakeQ{rows: 3}, "select * from big", sqlx.MySQL, 3)
	if exact.Truncated {
		t.Fatal("exactly maxRows rows is not truncated")
	}
}

func TestRunStopsWhenCancelled(t *testing.T) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	cancel()
	q := &fakeQ{}
	if res := Run(ctx, q, stmts("select 1; select 2"), sqlx.MySQL, 10); len(res) != 0 || len(q.ran) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("res=%v ran=%v", res, q.ran)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** `internal/runner/runner.go`

```go
// Package runner checks console statements against the safety rules and
// executes them one by one.
package runner

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/sqlx"
)

// Verdict says whether a batch may run.
type Verdict int

const (
	Allow Verdict = iota
	Reject
	Confirm
)

// Check is the guard's decision for a batch of statements.
type Check struct {
	Verdict Verdict
	Reason  string // Reject: why; Confirm: what is risky
	Expect  string // Confirm: the text the user must type to go on
	SQL     string // the statement that triggered the check
}

// Guard applies the v0.2 safety rules (spec §5.3): writes and DDL are
// rejected on read-only connections; an UPDATE or DELETE without WHERE needs
// its table name typed. A rejection wins over any confirmation.
func Guard(stmts []sqlx.Statement, d sqlx.Dialect, readOnly bool) Check {
	var confirm *Check
	for _, s := range stmts {
		info := sqlx.Analyze(s.Text, d)
		if readOnly && (info.Kind == sqlx.Write || info.Kind == sqlx.DDL) {
			return Check{
				Verdict: Reject, SQL: s.Text,
				Reason: fmt.Sprintf("read-only connection: %s statements are blocked (set readonly = false in connections.toml to allow them)", info.Kind),
			}
		}
		if info.MissingWhere && confirm == nil {
			target, expect := info.Target, info.Target
			if target == "" {
				target, expect = "the table", "yes"
			}
			confirm = &Check{
				Verdict: Confirm, SQL: s.Text, Expect: expect,
				Reason: fmt.Sprintf("This statement has no WHERE clause and changes every row of %s.", target),
			}
		}
	}
	if confirm != nil {
		return *confirm
	}
	return Check{Verdict: Allow}
}

// Result is the outcome of one statement.
type Result struct {
	SQL       string
	Rows      *db.ResultSet // nil for statements without a result set
	Truncated bool          // more than maxRows rows existed; only maxRows are kept
	Affected  int64
	Duration  time.Duration
	Err       error
}

// Run executes stmts in order on q, stopping after the first error or once
// ctx is cancelled. Result sets keep at most maxRows rows.
func Run(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, stmts []sqlx.Statement, d sqlx.Dialect, maxRows int) []Result {
	var out []Result
	for _, s := range stmts {
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			break
		}
		r := RunOne(ctx, q, s.Text, d, maxRows)
		out = append(out, r)
		if r.Err != nil {
			break
		}
	}
	return out
}

// RunOne executes one statement.
func RunOne(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, sql string, d sqlx.Dialect, maxRows int) Result {
	start := time.Now()
	r := Result{SQL: sql}
	if sqlx.Analyze(sql, d).ReturnsRows {
		rs := &db.ResultSet{}
		r.Err = q.Stream(ctx, sql,
			func(cols []db.ColumnInfo) { rs.Columns = cols },
			func(row []db.Value) error {
				if len(rs.Rows) >= maxRows {
					r.Truncated = true
					return db.ErrStop
				}
				rs.Rows = append(rs.Rows, row)
				return nil
			})
		if r.Err == nil {
			r.Rows = rs
		}
	} else {
		res, err := q.Exec(ctx, sql)
		r.Affected, r.Err = res.RowsAffected, err
	}
	r.Duration = time.Since(start)
	return r
}
```

### Task 17: 控制台 tab

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/ui/console.go`
- Modify: `internal/ui/tabs.go`（抽出 `gridAction`，数据 tab 获得 `zh`/`zl` 和 `Find`）、`internal/ui/fakes_test.go`
- Test: `internal/ui/console_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5 `db.Session`/`db.IsConnLost`；Task 6 `Backend.Connection`/`Reset`；Task 10 动作；Task 11 `grid.Find`/`ReplaceResult`/`ScrollColumns`；Task 13 `overlay.NewConfirm`；Task 15 `editor`；Task 16 `runner.Guard`/`Run`/`RunOne`；`session.ErrPasswordRequired`。
- Produces（`package ui`）：
  - `type timings struct{ tick, draft time.Duration }`、`var defaultTimings`
  - `func newConsoleTab(id int, b Backend, conn string, ns db.Namespace, s config.Settings, tm timings) *consoleTab`，实现 `tab`
  - `func consoleTabKey(conn string, ns db.Namespace) string`
  - 可选接口（Task 18 的根 Model 用类型断言调用）：
    - `type inputCapturer interface { CapturesInput() bool; Input(msg tea.Msg) tea.Cmd }`
    - `type moder interface { Mode() string }`
    - `type finder interface { Find(q string, forward bool) bool }`（`dataTab` 也实现）
    - `type canceler interface { cancelRun() tea.Cmd }`
    - `type shutdowner interface { shutdown() func() }`（返回在 Cmd 中执行的清理函数：关闭会话；Task 19 增加保存草稿）
  - 消息：`runDoneMsg{tabID, gen int; session db.Session; results []runner.Result; reconnected bool; err error; more bool; limit int}`、`runTickMsg{tabID, gen int}`、`confirmRunMsg{tabID, gen int}`（都实现 `tabMsg`）
  - `func ensureSession(ctx context.Context, b Backend, conn string, ns db.Namespace, s db.Session) (db.Session, bool, error)`
  - `func gridAction(g *grid.Model, a keymap.Action) (tea.Cmd, bool)`

**行为：**
- 布局：编辑器（高度约 2/5）、分隔线（`── Results · N rows` / `── Messages`）、结果区（表格或消息日志）、状态行。
- NORMAL：`Console` 上下文按键移动编辑器光标；`i`/`a` 进入 INSERT；`enter` 执行光标处语句；`R` 执行全部；`ctrl+j` 进入结果区（`Results` 上下文），`ctrl+k` 返回；`M` 切换消息日志。
- INSERT：除 `esc`（回 NORMAL）、`ctrl+enter`/`ctrl+j`（执行光标处语句）、`ctrl+c`（取消）外，所有按键与粘贴交给编辑器。
- 执行前 `runner.Guard`：`Reject` 写入消息日志与状态行，不执行；`Confirm` 弹出 `overlay.NewConfirm`，确认后发 `confirmRunMsg`。
- 执行：`execute` 把会话交给 Cmd（期间 `t.session = nil`），Cmd 中 `ensureSession`（已有会话先 ping，失败则关闭重开；打开时遇到断线先 `Reset` 再试一次），然后 `runner.Run`；最后一条结果是断线错误时关闭并丢弃会话。打开会话后：MySQL 且 `ns.Database` 非空执行 ``USE `db` ``；PG 且 `ns.Schema` 非空执行 `SET search_path TO "schema", public`。
- 结果：最后一个结果集显示在表格中，每条语句一行写入消息日志（`✓ … — N rows in 12ms` / `✓ … — N rows affected` / `✗ … — 错误`）；只有非查询语句或出错时自动切到消息日志。截断时分隔线提示 `truncated · ] more`，`]` 只对 `Read` 语句重跑，上限加 `max_rows`。
- 运行中状态行显示转圈与耗时（每 `timings.tick` 刷新），`ctrl+c` 取消。

- [ ] **Step 1: 扩展测试替身**

`internal/ui/fakes_test.go` 中 `fakeConn` 改为（`Query` 增加失败注入与可取消的慢查询，`Exec` 记录语句，`Session` 记录会话）：

```go
type fakeConn struct {
	mu       sync.Mutex
	queries  []string
	execs    []string
	rows     int              // rows returned by every Query
	fail     map[string]error // statement text -> error
	pingErr  error            // returned by every session Ping
	sessions []*fakeSession
}

// slowQuery blocks until its context is cancelled.
const slowQuery = "select sleep"

func (c *fakeConn) Query(ctx context.Context, q string, _ ...any) (*db.ResultSet, error) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	c.queries = append(c.queries, q)
	err, rows := c.fail[q], c.rows
	c.mu.Unlock()
	if q == slowQuery {
		<-ctx.Done()
		return nil, ctx.Err()
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	rs := &db.ResultSet{Columns: []db.ColumnInfo{{Name: "id"}, {Name: "name"}}}
	for i := range rows {
		rs.Rows = append(rs.Rows, []db.Value{{Text: strconv.Itoa(i + 1)}, {Text: fmt.Sprintf("user%d", i+1)}})
	}
	return rs, nil
}

func (c *fakeConn) Exec(_ context.Context, q string, _ ...any) (db.ExecResult, error) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	c.execs = append(c.execs, q)
	if err := c.fail[q]; err != nil {
		return db.ExecResult{}, err
	}
	return db.ExecResult{RowsAffected: 1}, nil
}

func (c *fakeConn) execList() []string {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return slices.Clone(c.execs)
}

func (c *fakeConn) setFail(q string, err error) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	if c.fail == nil {
		c.fail = map[string]error{}
	}
	c.fail[q] = err
}

func (c *fakeConn) Session(context.Context) (db.Session, error) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	s := &fakeSession{conn: c}
	c.sessions = append(c.sessions, s)
	return s, nil
}

func (c *fakeConn) sessionCount() int {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return len(c.sessions)
}
```

`fakeSession` 的 `Ping` 与 `Close` 改为：

```go
func (s *fakeSession) Ping(context.Context) error {
	s.conn.mu.Lock()
	defer s.conn.mu.Unlock()
	return s.conn.pingErr
}

func (s *fakeSession) Close() error {
	s.conn.mu.Lock()
	defer s.conn.mu.Unlock()
	s.closed = true
	return nil
}
```

```go
package ui

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/config"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/keymap"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/uitest"
)

func newConsole(t *testing.T, b *fakeBackend, conn string) *consoleTab {
	t.Helper()
	c := newConsoleTab(1, b, conn, db.Namespace{Database: "app"}, config.Settings{MaxRows: 2, MaxColumnWidth: 40}, timings{})
	c.SetSize(80, 24)
	return c
}

// runTab runs cmd, feeding messages addressed to tab back into it, and
// returns every other message (e.g. overlays).
func runTab(t *testing.T, tab tab, cmd tea.Cmd) []tea.Msg {
	t.Helper()
	var other []tea.Msg
	queue := []tea.Cmd{cmd}
	for steps := 0; len(queue) > 0; steps++ {
		if steps > 100 {
			t.Fatal("runTab: too many steps")
		}
		c := queue[0]
		queue = queue[1:]
		if c == nil {
			continue
		}
		switch msg := c().(type) {
		case nil:
		case tea.BatchMsg:
			queue = append(queue, msg...)
		case tabMsg:
			if msg.TabID() == tab.ID() {
				queue = append(queue, tab.Update(msg))
			} else {
				other = append(other, msg)
			}
		default:
			other = append(other, msg)
		}
	}
	return other
}

func consoleView(c *consoleTab) string { return ansi.Strip(c.View(true)) }

func TestConsoleRunsStatementUnderCursor(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	c := newConsole(t, b, "local")
	c.editor.SetValue("select 1;\nselect 2;")
	c.editor.Top()
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement))
	if got := b.conn.lastQuery(); got != "select 1" {
		t.Fatalf("ran %q", got)
	}
	if got := b.conn.execList(); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"USE `app`"}) {
		t.Fatalf("session scope: %v", got)
	}
	v := consoleView(c)
	for _, want := range []string{"user1", "user2", "2 rows", "truncated"} {
		if !strings.Contains(v, want) {
			t.Errorf("view missing %q:\n%s", want, v)
		}
	}
	if strings.Contains(v, "user3") {
		t.Error("max_rows caps the result")
	}
	c.Handle(keymap.ToggleMessages)
	if v := consoleView(c); !strings.Contains(v, "✓ select 1") {
		t.Fatalf("messages:\n%s", v)
	}
}

func TestConsoleRunAllShowsLastResultSet(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	c := newConsole(t, b, "local")
	c.editor.SetValue("select 1; update t set a = 1 where id = 1")
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunAll))
	if got := b.conn.execList(); !slices.Contains(got, "update t set a = 1 where id = 1") {
		t.Fatalf("execs = %v", got)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(consoleView(c), "user1") {
		t.Fatalf("the select result stays visible:\n%s", consoleView(c))
	}
	c.Handle(keymap.ToggleMessages)
	if v := consoleView(c); !strings.Contains(v, "1 rows affected") {
		t.Fatalf("messages:\n%s", v)
	}
}

func TestConsoleOnlyExecShowsMessages(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	c := newConsole(t, b, "local")
	c.editor.SetValue("set @a = 1")
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement))
	if v := consoleView(c); !strings.Contains(v, "Messages") || !strings.Contains(v, "set @a = 1") {
		t.Fatalf("view:\n%s", v)
	}
}

func TestConsoleReadOnlyRejectsWrites(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	c := newConsole(t, b, "prod") // env = prod -> read-only
	c.editor.SetValue("delete from t where id = 1")
	if cmd := c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement); cmd != nil {
		t.Fatal("nothing runs")
	}
	if len(b.conn.execList()) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("no statement reached the server")
	}
	if v := consoleView(c); !strings.Contains(v, "read-only") {
		t.Fatalf("view:\n%s", v)
	}
}

func TestConsoleMissingWhereNeedsTableName(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	c := newConsole(t, b, "local")
	c.editor.SetValue("update t set a = 1")
	other := runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement))
	if len(other) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("messages = %#v", other)
	}
	o := other[0].(openOverlayMsg).overlay
	if !strings.Contains(ansi.Strip(o.View(80, 20)), "no WHERE clause") {
		t.Fatalf("confirm:\n%s", ansi.Strip(o.View(80, 20)))
	}
	if len(b.conn.execList()) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("nothing runs before confirming")
	}
	o, _ = o.HandleKey(uitest.Key("t"))
	_, cmd := o.HandleKey(uitest.Key("enter"))
	runTab(t, c, cmd)
	if got := b.conn.execList(); !slices.Contains(got, "update t set a = 1") {
		t.Fatalf("execs = %v", got)
	}
}

func TestConsoleShowsErrors(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	b.conn.setFail("select bad", errors.New("syntax error near bad"))
	c := newConsole(t, b, "local")
	c.editor.SetValue("select bad")
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement))
	if v := consoleView(c); !strings.Contains(v, "syntax error near bad") || !strings.Contains(v, "Messages") {
		t.Fatalf("view:\n%s", v)
	}
}

func TestConsoleCancel(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	c := newConsole(t, b, "local")
	c.editor.SetValue(slowQuery)
	cmds := c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement)().(tea.BatchMsg)
	done := make(chan tea.Msg, 1)
	go func() { done <- cmds[0]() }()
	if v := consoleView(c); !strings.Contains(v, "ctrl+c cancel") {
		t.Fatalf("running view:\n%s", v)
	}
	if cmd := c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement); cmd != nil {
		t.Fatal("only one query runs at a time")
	}
	c.Handle(keymap.Cancel)
	select {
	case msg := <-done:
		c.Update(msg)
	case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("cancel did not stop the query")
	}
	if c.running || !strings.Contains(consoleView(c), "canceled") {
		t.Fatalf("view:\n%s", consoleView(c))
	}
	if c.session == nil {
		t.Fatal("the session survives a cancel")
	}
}

func TestConsoleReusesAndReopensSession(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	c := newConsole(t, b, "local")
	c.editor.SetValue("select 1")
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement))
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement))
	if n := b.conn.sessionCount(); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("sessions = %d, want 1", n)
	}
	b.conn.mu.Lock()
	b.conn.pingErr = fmt.Errorf("read: %w", io.EOF)
	b.conn.mu.Unlock()
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement))
	if n := b.conn.sessionCount(); n != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("sessions = %d, want a new one after a failed ping", n)
	}
	c.Handle(keymap.ToggleMessages)
	if !strings.Contains(consoleView(c), "reconnected") {
		t.Fatalf("view:\n%s", consoleView(c))
	}
}

func TestConsoleDropsSessionOnConnectionLoss(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	b.conn.setFail("select 1", fmt.Errorf("%w: broken pipe", db.ErrConnLost))
	c := newConsole(t, b, "local")
	c.editor.SetValue("select 1")
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement))
	if c.session != nil {
		t.Fatal("a dead session is dropped")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(consoleView(c), "connection lost") {
		t.Fatalf("view:\n%s", consoleView(c))
	}
	if n := len(b.conn.queries); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("statements are never retried automatically: %d queries", n)
	}
}

func TestConsoleLoadMore(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	c := newConsole(t, b, "local")
	c.editor.SetValue("select * from users")
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement))
	c.Handle(keymap.FocusResults)
	if c.Context() != keymap.Results {
		t.Fatal("ctrl+j moves to the results")
	}
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.LoadMore))
	v := consoleView(c)
	if !strings.Contains(v, "user3") || strings.Contains(v, "truncated") {
		t.Fatalf("view:\n%s", v)
	}
	if cmd := c.Handle(keymap.LoadMore); cmd != nil {
		t.Fatal("nothing more to load")
	}
}

func TestConsoleInsertMode(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	c := newConsole(t, b, "local")
	if c.CapturesInput() || c.Mode() != "NORMAL" {
		t.Fatal("starts in NORMAL")
	}
	c.Handle(keymap.InsertMode)
	if !c.CapturesInput() || c.Mode() != "INSERT" {
		t.Fatal("i enters INSERT")
	}
	for _, k := range uitest.Type("select 1") {
		c.Input(k)
	}
	runTab(t, c, c.Input(uitest.Key("ctrl+j")))
	if b.conn.lastQuery() != "select 1" {
		t.Fatalf("ctrl+j runs in INSERT mode, ran %q", b.conn.lastQuery())
	}
	c.Input(uitest.Key("esc"))
	if c.CapturesInput() {
		t.Fatal("esc leaves INSERT")
	}
}

func TestConsoleNothingToRunAndTinySizes(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsole(t, newFakeBackend(), "local")
	if c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement) != nil || !strings.Contains(consoleView(c), "nothing to run") {
		t.Fatal("empty editor")
	}
	for _, s := range [][2]int{{0, 0}, {1, 1}, {10, 3}, {30, 5}} {
		c.SetSize(s[0], s[1])
		_ = c.View(true)
	}
}

func TestDataTabScrollAndFind(t *testing.T) {
	tab := loadedDataTab(t, newFakeBackend())
	if !tab.Find("USER2", true) {
		t.Fatal("find")
	}
	if _, v, _ := tab.grid.Cell(); v.Text != "user2" {
		t.Fatalf("at %q", v.Text)
	}
	tab.Handle(keymap.ScrollRight) // must not panic
}
```
