- [ ] **Step 4: 实现**

`internal/ui/tabs.go`：新增 `gridAction`，`dataTab.Handle` 先交给它，再处理分页/刷新；加 `Find`：

```go
// gridAction handles the cursor, scroll and cell-detail actions every grid shares.
func gridAction(g *grid.Model, a keymap.Action) (tea.Cmd, bool) {
	switch a {
	case keymap.Down:
		g.Move(1, 0)
	case keymap.Up:
		g.Move(-1, 0)
	case keymap.Left:
		g.Move(0, -1)
	case keymap.Right:
		g.Move(0, 1)
	case keymap.Top:
		g.Top()
	case keymap.Bottom:
		g.Bottom()
	case keymap.HalfPageDown:
		g.HalfPage(1)
	case keymap.HalfPageUp:
		g.HalfPage(-1)
	case keymap.LineStart:
		g.LineStart()
	case keymap.LineEnd:
		g.LineEnd()
	case keymap.ScrollLeft:
		g.ScrollColumns(-1)
	case keymap.ScrollRight:
		g.ScrollColumns(1)
	case keymap.CellDetail:
		if col, v, ok := g.Cell(); ok {
			text := v.Text
			if v.Null {
				text = "NULL"
			}
			return openOverlay(overlay.NewDetail(col.Name, text)), true
		}
	default:
		return nil, false
	}
	return nil, true
}

func (t *dataTab) Find(q string, forward bool) bool { return t.grid.Find(q, forward) }

func (t *dataTab) Handle(a keymap.Action) tea.Cmd {
	if cmd, ok := gridAction(t.grid, a); ok {
		return cmd
	}
	switch a {
	case keymap.NextPage:
		if t.hasMore && !t.loading {
			return t.load(t.page + 1)
		}
	case keymap.PrevPage:
		if t.page > 0 && !t.loading {
			return t.load(t.page - 1)
		}
	case keymap.Refresh:
		if !t.loading {
			t.orderKnown = false
			return t.load(t.page)
		}
	}
	return nil
}
```

`internal/ui/console.go`：

```go
package ui

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"strings"
	"time"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/config"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/runner"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/session"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/sqlx"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/keymap"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/overlay"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/panes/editor"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/panes/grid"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/textutil"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/theme"
)

const (
	pingTimeout = 5 * time.Second
	maxMessages = 500
)

// timings are the console's clocks; tests set them to zero.
type timings struct {
	tick  time.Duration // spinner refresh while a query runs
	draft time.Duration // idle time before a draft is saved
}

var defaultTimings = timings{tick: 100 * time.Millisecond, draft: time.Second}

var spinner = []string{"⠋", "⠙", "⠹", "⠸", "⠼", "⠴", "⠦", "⠧", "⠇", "⠏"}

// Optional tab capabilities, checked by the root model with type assertions.
type (
	inputCapturer interface {
		CapturesInput() bool
		Input(msg tea.Msg) tea.Cmd
	}
	moder      interface{ Mode() string }
	finder     interface{ Find(q string, forward bool) bool }
	canceler   interface{ cancelRun() tea.Cmd }
	shutdowner interface{ shutdown() func() }
)

type runDoneMsg struct {
	tabID       int
	gen         int
	session     db.Session
	results     []runner.Result
	reconnected bool
	err         error // opening the session failed
	more        bool  // re-run of the shown statement with a higher limit
	limit       int
}

type runTickMsg struct{ tabID, gen int }

type confirmRunMsg struct{ tabID, gen int }

func (m runDoneMsg) TabID() int    { return m.tabID }
func (m runTickMsg) TabID() int    { return m.tabID }
func (m confirmRunMsg) TabID() int { return m.tabID }

func consoleTabKey(conn string, ns db.Namespace) string {
	return strings.Join([]string{"console", conn, ns.Database, ns.Schema}, "\x00")
}

type consoleTab struct {
	id      int
	backend Backend
	conn    string
	ns      db.Namespace
	dialect sqlx.Dialect
	maxRows int
	timings timings

	editor       *editor.Model
	grid         *grid.Model
	onResults    bool
	showMessages bool
	messages     []string

	session   db.Session
	running   bool
	cancel    context.CancelFunc
	started   time.Time
	gen       int
	pending   []sqlx.Statement // waiting for confirmation
	last      *runner.Result   // the result set on screen
	lastLimit int
	status    string
	statusErr bool

	width, height, editorH int
}

func newConsoleTab(id int, b Backend, conn string, ns db.Namespace, s config.Settings, tm timings) *consoleTab {
	cfg, _ := b.Connection(conn)
	return &consoleTab{
		id: id, backend: b, conn: conn, ns: ns, dialect: sqlx.DialectOf(cfg.Driver),
		maxRows: s.MaxRows, timings: tm, editor: editor.New(), grid: grid.New(nil, s.MaxColumnWidth),
	}
}

func (t *consoleTab) ID() int     { return t.id }
func (t *consoleTab) Key() string { return consoleTabKey(t.conn, t.ns) }
func (t *consoleTab) Init() tea.Cmd { return nil }

func (t *consoleTab) Title() string {
	if t.ns.Database != "" {
		return "console:" + t.ns.Database
	}
	return "console:" + t.conn
}

func (t *consoleTab) Context() keymap.Context {
	if t.onResults {
		return keymap.Results
	}
	return keymap.Console
}

func (t *consoleTab) Target() (string, db.TableRef) { return t.conn, db.TableRef{Namespace: t.ns} }

func (t *consoleTab) CapturesInput() bool { return !t.onResults && t.editor.Insert() }

func (t *consoleTab) Mode() string {
	if t.CapturesInput() {
		return "INSERT"
	}
	return "NORMAL"
}

func (t *consoleTab) Find(q string, forward bool) bool {
	if t.grid.Find(q, forward) {
		t.onResults = true
		return true
	}
	return false
}

func (t *consoleTab) SetSize(w, h int) {
	t.width, t.height = max(w, 0), max(h, 0)
	body := max(t.height-2, 0) // separator and status line
	t.editorH = min(max(body*2/5, 3), body)
	t.editor.SetSize(t.width, t.editorH)
	t.grid.SetSize(t.width, body-t.editorH)
}

func (t *consoleTab) setStatus(s string, isErr bool) { t.status, t.statusErr = s, isErr }

func (t *consoleTab) log(line string) {
	t.messages = append(t.messages, line)
	if over := len(t.messages) - maxMessages; over > 0 {
		t.messages = t.messages[over:]
	}
}

// ---- input ----

func (t *consoleTab) Input(msg tea.Msg) tea.Cmd {
	if k, ok := msg.(tea.KeyPressMsg); ok {
		switch k.String() {
		case "esc":
			t.editor.ExitInsert()
			return nil
		case "ctrl+enter", "ctrl+j":
			return t.runAtCursor()
		case "ctrl+c":
			return t.cancelRun()
		}
	}
	return t.editor.Update(msg)
}

func (t *consoleTab) Handle(a keymap.Action) tea.Cmd {
	switch a {
	case keymap.Cancel:
		return t.cancelRun()
	case keymap.FocusResults:
		t.onResults = true
		return nil
	case keymap.FocusEditor:
		t.onResults = false
		return nil
	case keymap.ToggleMessages:
		t.showMessages = !t.showMessages
		return nil
	}
	if t.onResults {
		if cmd, ok := gridAction(t.grid, a); ok {
			return cmd
		}
		if a == keymap.LoadMore {
			return t.loadMore()
		}
		return nil
	}
	return t.editorAction(a)
}

func (t *consoleTab) editorAction(a keymap.Action) tea.Cmd {
	e := t.editor
	switch a {
	case keymap.Down:
		e.Down(1)
	case keymap.Up:
		e.Up(1)
	case keymap.Left:
		e.Left()
	case keymap.Right:
		e.Right()
	case keymap.Top:
		e.Top()
	case keymap.Bottom:
		e.Bottom()
	case keymap.HalfPageDown:
		e.HalfPage(1)
	case keymap.HalfPageUp:
		e.HalfPage(-1)
	case keymap.LineStart:
		e.LineStart()
	case keymap.LineEnd:
		e.LineEnd()
	case keymap.InsertMode:
		e.EnterInsert()
	case keymap.Append:
		e.Right()
		e.EnterInsert()
	case keymap.RunStatement:
		return t.runAtCursor()
	case keymap.RunAll:
		return t.run(sqlx.Split(e.Value(), t.dialect))
	}
	return nil
}

// ---- running ----

func (t *consoleTab) runAtCursor() tea.Cmd {
	s, ok := sqlx.At(sqlx.Split(t.editor.Value(), t.dialect), t.editor.Offset())
	if !ok {
		t.setStatus("nothing to run", true)
		return nil
	}
	return t.run([]sqlx.Statement{s})
}

func (t *consoleTab) run(stmts []sqlx.Statement) tea.Cmd {
	if t.running {
		t.setStatus("a query is already running · ctrl+c to cancel", true)
		return nil
	}
	if len(stmts) == 0 {
		t.setStatus("nothing to run", true)
		return nil
	}
	cfg, _ := t.backend.Connection(t.conn)
	switch chk := runner.Guard(stmts, t.dialect, cfg.IsReadOnly()); chk.Verdict {
	case runner.Reject:
		t.log("✗ " + oneLine(chk.SQL) + " — " + chk.Reason)
		t.setStatus(chk.Reason, true)
		return nil
	case runner.Confirm:
		t.gen++
		t.pending = stmts
		return openOverlay(overlay.NewConfirm("Statement has no WHERE clause",
			[]string{chk.Reason, "", chk.SQL}, chk.Expect, confirmRunMsg{tabID: t.id, gen: t.gen}))
	}
	return t.execute(stmts, t.maxRows, false)
}

func (t *consoleTab) execute(stmts []sqlx.Statement, limit int, more bool) tea.Cmd {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	t.gen++
	t.running, t.cancel, t.started = true, cancel, time.Now()
	t.setStatus("", false)
	gen, id, b, conn, ns, d, s := t.gen, t.id, t.backend, t.conn, t.ns, t.dialect, t.session
	t.session = nil // owned by the command until runDoneMsg hands it back
	run := func() tea.Msg {
		defer cancel()
		msg := runDoneMsg{tabID: id, gen: gen, more: more, limit: limit}
		sess, reconnected, err := ensureSession(ctx, b, conn, ns, s)
		msg.reconnected = reconnected
		if err != nil {
			msg.err = err
			return msg
		}
		msg.results = runner.Run(ctx, sess, stmts, d, limit)
		if n := len(msg.results); n > 0 && db.IsConnLost(msg.results[n-1].Err) {
			_ = sess.Close()
			sess = nil
		}
		msg.session = sess
		return msg
	}
	return tea.Batch(run, t.tick(gen))
}

func (t *consoleTab) tick(gen int) tea.Cmd {
	id := t.id
	return tea.Tick(t.timings.tick, func(time.Time) tea.Msg { return runTickMsg{tabID: id, gen: gen} })
}

func (t *consoleTab) cancelRun() tea.Cmd {
	if !t.running {
		t.setStatus("no query is running · :qa quits", false)
		return nil
	}
	t.cancel()
	t.setStatus("canceling…", false)
	return nil
}

func (t *consoleTab) loadMore() tea.Cmd {
	switch {
	case t.running:
		t.setStatus("a query is already running", true)
	case t.last == nil || !t.last.Truncated:
		t.setStatus("all rows are loaded", false)
	case sqlx.Analyze(t.last.SQL, t.dialect).Kind != sqlx.Read:
		t.setStatus("only read statements are re-run to load more rows", true)
	default:
		return t.execute([]sqlx.Statement{{Text: t.last.SQL}}, t.lastLimit+t.maxRows, true)
	}
	return nil
}

func (t *consoleTab) Update(msg tea.Msg) tea.Cmd {
	switch m := msg.(type) {
	case runTickMsg:
		if t.running && m.gen == t.gen {
			return t.tick(m.gen)
		}
	case confirmRunMsg:
		if m.gen == t.gen && t.pending != nil {
			stmts := t.pending
			t.pending = nil
			return t.execute(stmts, t.maxRows, false)
		}
	case runDoneMsg:
		t.done(m)
	}
	return nil
}

func (t *consoleTab) done(m runDoneMsg) {
	t.running, t.cancel = false, nil
	t.session = m.session
	if m.reconnected {
		t.log("reconnected to " + t.conn)
	}
	if m.err != nil {
		text := m.err.Error()
		if errors.Is(m.err, session.ErrPasswordRequired) {
			text = "password required: select " + t.conn + " in the tree and press p"
		}
		t.log("✗ " + text)
		t.setStatus(text, true)
		t.showMessages = true
		return
	}
	var shown *runner.Result
	for i := range m.results {
		r := &m.results[i]
		t.log(describe(r))
		if r.Err == nil && r.Rows != nil {
			shown = r
		}
	}
	if len(m.results) == 0 {
		t.setStatus("canceled", true)
		return
	}
	last := m.results[len(m.results)-1]
	if shown != nil {
		if m.more {
			t.grid.ReplaceResult(shown.Rows)
		} else {
			t.grid.SetResult(shown.Rows)
		}
		t.last, t.lastLimit = shown, m.limit
	}
	switch {
	case last.Err != nil:
		t.setStatus(errorText(last.Err), true)
		t.showMessages = true
	case shown == nil || len(shown.Rows.Columns) == 0:
		t.setStatus(fmt.Sprintf("%d statement(s) ok", len(m.results)), false)
		t.showMessages = true
	default:
		t.showMessages = false
	}
}

func describe(r *runner.Result) string {
	took := r.Duration.Round(time.Millisecond)
	switch {
	case r.Err != nil:
		return fmt.Sprintf("✗ %s — %s", oneLine(r.SQL), errorText(r.Err))
	case r.Rows != nil && len(r.Rows.Columns) > 0:
		s := fmt.Sprintf("✓ %s — %d rows in %s", oneLine(r.SQL), len(r.Rows.Rows), took)
		if r.Truncated {
			s += " (truncated)"
		}
		return s
	case r.Rows != nil:
		return fmt.Sprintf("✓ %s — ok in %s", oneLine(r.SQL), took)
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("✓ %s — %d rows affected in %s", oneLine(r.SQL), r.Affected, took)
}

func errorText(err error) string {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, context.Canceled):
		return "canceled"
	case db.IsConnLost(err):
		return "connection lost — the statement may or may not have run; the next run reconnects"
	}
	return err.Error()
}

// oneLine shortens a statement for the message log.
func oneLine(sql string) string {
	s := strings.Join(strings.Fields(sql), " ")
	if r := []rune(s); len(r) > 60 {
		s = string(r[:59]) + "…"
	}
	return s
}

// ensureSession returns a live session: s if it still answers a ping,
// otherwise a newly opened one (reconnected reports that). Statements are
// never retried here.
func ensureSession(ctx context.Context, b Backend, conn string, ns db.Namespace, s db.Session) (db.Session, bool, error) {
	if s != nil {
		pctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, pingTimeout)
		err := s.Ping(pctx)
		cancel()
		if err == nil {
			return s, false, nil
		}
		slog.Info("console session lost, reconnecting", "conn", conn, "err", err)
		_ = s.Close()
	}
	reconnected := s != nil
	ns2, err := openSession(ctx, b, conn, ns)
	if db.IsConnLost(err) {
		b.Reset(conn)
		ns2, err = openSession(ctx, b, conn, ns)
		reconnected = true
	}
	return ns2, reconnected, err
}

func openSession(ctx context.Context, b Backend, conn string, ns db.Namespace) (db.Session, error) {
	octx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, loadTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	c, err := b.Conn(octx, conn, ns.Database)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	s, err := c.Session(octx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	cfg, _ := b.Connection(conn)
	if q := scopeSQL(cfg.Driver, c.Dialect(), ns); q != "" {
		if _, err := s.Exec(octx, q); err != nil {
			_ = s.Close()
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return s, nil
}

// scopeSQL points a fresh session at the namespace the console was opened on.
func scopeSQL(driver string, d db.Dialect, ns db.Namespace) string {
	switch {
	case driver == "mysql" && ns.Database != "":
		return "USE " + d.QuoteIdent(ns.Database)
	case driver == "postgres" && ns.Schema != "":
		return "SET search_path TO " + d.QuoteIdent(ns.Schema) + ", public"
	}
	return ""
}

func (t *consoleTab) shutdown() func() {
	s, cancel := t.session, t.cancel
	t.session = nil
	return func() {
		if cancel != nil {
			cancel()
		}
		if s != nil {
			_ = s.Close()
		}
	}
}

// ---- view ----

func (t *consoleTab) View(focused bool) string {
	if t.height == 0 || t.width == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	body := max(t.height-2, 0)
	parts := []string{textutil.PadLines(t.editor.View(), t.editorH), t.separator()}
	resultsH := body - t.editorH
	if t.showMessages {
		parts = append(parts, t.messagesView(resultsH))
	} else {
		parts = append(parts, textutil.PadLines(t.grid.View(focused && t.onResults), resultsH))
	}
	parts = append(parts, t.statusLine())
	return strings.Join(parts, "\n")
}

func (t *consoleTab) separator() string {
	label := "Results"
	if t.showMessages {
		label = "Messages"
	} else if rs := t.grid.Result(); rs != nil {
		label += fmt.Sprintf(" · %d rows", len(rs.Rows))
		if t.last != nil && t.last.Truncated {
			label += " · truncated · ] more"
		}
	}
	line := "── " + label + " " + strings.Repeat("─", t.width)
	return theme.Muted.Render(textutil.Fit(line, t.width))
}

func (t *consoleTab) messagesView(h int) string {
	start := max(len(t.messages)-h, 0)
	lines := make([]string, 0, h)
	for _, m := range t.messages[start:] {
		lines = append(lines, textutil.Fit(textutil.Sanitize(m), t.width))
	}
	return textutil.PadLines(strings.Join(lines, "\n"), h)
}

func (t *consoleTab) statusLine() string {
	switch {
	case t.running:
		el := time.Since(t.started)
		frame := spinner[int(el/(100*time.Millisecond))%len(spinner)]
		return theme.Muted.Render(textutil.Fit(fmt.Sprintf("%s running %.1fs · ctrl+c cancel", frame, el.Seconds()), t.width))
	case t.status != "" && t.statusErr:
		return theme.Error.Render(textutil.Fit("✗ "+textutil.Sanitize(t.status), t.width))
	case t.status != "":
		return theme.Muted.Render(textutil.Fit(textutil.Sanitize(t.status), t.width))
	}
	return theme.Muted.Render(textutil.Fit("enter run · R run all · i insert · ctrl+j results · <space>v $EDITOR", t.width))
}
```

### Task 18: 根 Model 接线：控制台、INSERT 输入、搜索、Ctrl-c、退出清理

**Files:**
- Modify: `internal/ui/app.go`
- Test: `internal/ui/app_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 17 的 `newConsoleTab`、`consoleTabKey`、`inputCapturer`、`moder`、`finder`、`canceler`、`shutdowner`、`runDoneMsg`、`timings`/`defaultTimings`；Task 13 的 `overlay.NewSearchLine`/`SearchMsg`；Task 12 的 `tree.Find`。
- Produces:
  - `Model.timings timings`（`New` 中为 `defaultTimings`；测试置零）
  - `func (m *Model) openConsole(conn string, ns db.Namespace) tea.Cmd`、`func (m *Model) contextNamespace() (string, db.Namespace)`
  - `func (m *Model) closeTab(i int) tea.Cmd`（签名变更：返回清理 Cmd）、`func (m *Model) quit() tea.Cmd`
  - `func (m *Model) find(forward bool)`

**按键处理顺序（更新）：** 命令行/搜索行 → 模态浮层 → **当前 tab 处于 INSERT（`inputCapturer.CapturesInput()`）时把原始按键交给 tab** → `esc` 取消未完成序列 → keymap。`tea.PasteMsg` 在 INSERT 时同样交给 tab。

**其他规则：**
- `Cancel`（`ctrl+c`）：当前 tab 是 `canceler` 时交给它；否则状态栏提示 `no query is running · :qa quits`。
- `OpenConsole`（`<space>c`）：用当前上下文（tab 的 Target 或树中选中节点）的连接与命名空间打开或切换到控制台；没有连接时提示 `select a connection first`。
- `/` 打开搜索行，回车后记住搜索词并在树或当前 tab（`finder`）中查找；`n`/`N` 重复；找不到时提示 `pattern not found: …`。
- `:q` 关闭 tab 时运行其 `shutdown()`；`:qa` 依次运行所有 tab 的 `shutdown()` 后退出。
- 发给已关闭 tab 的 `runDoneMsg` 若带会话，关闭它。
- `<space>s` 在没有表名的 tab（控制台）中提示 `select a table first`。
- 状态栏模式块显示当前 tab 的 `Mode()`（默认 `NORMAL`）。
- 欢迎文字加入 `<space>c console`。

```go
func typeKeys(t *testing.T, m *Model, s string) {
	t.Helper()
	for _, r := range s {
		press(t, m, string(r))
	}
}

func TestOpenConsoleTypeAndRun(t *testing.T) {
	m, b := newTestModel(t)
	press(t, m, "l", "j") // cursor on database "app"
	press(t, m, "space", "c")
	if s := screen(m); !strings.Contains(s, "[1]console:app") {
		t.Fatalf("console tab:\n%s", s)
	}
	press(t, m, "i")
	if !strings.Contains(screen(m), " INSERT ") {
		t.Fatalf("status shows INSERT:\n%s", screen(m))
	}
	typeKeys(t, m, "select 1")
	press(t, m, "1") // digits are text in INSERT, not "go to tab"
	press(t, m, "esc", "enter")
	if got := b.conn.lastQuery(); got != "select 11" {
		t.Fatalf("ran %q", got)
	}
	if s := screen(m); !strings.Contains(s, "user1") || !strings.Contains(s, " NORMAL ") {
		t.Fatalf("result:\n%s", s)
	}
	press(t, m, "ctrl+h", "space", "c")
	if len(m.tabs) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("<space>c on the same database reuses the console")
	}
}

func TestSearchTreeAndGrid(t *testing.T) {
	m, _ := newTestModel(t)
	press(t, m, "/")
	if !strings.Contains(screen(m), "/█") {
		t.Fatal("search line in the status bar")
	}
	press(t, m, "p", "r", "enter")
	if m.tree.Selected().Name != "prod" {
		t.Fatalf("selected %q", m.tree.Selected().Name)
	}
	press(t, m, "n")
	if m.tree.Selected().Name != "prod" {
		t.Fatal("n repeats (only one match)")
	}
	press(t, m, "/", "z", "z", "enter")
	if !strings.Contains(screen(m), "pattern not found: zz") {
		t.Fatalf("screen:\n%s", screen(m))
	}

	press(t, m, "g", "g") // back to the first connection
	openUsers(t, m)
	press(t, m, "/", "u", "s", "e", "r", "2", "enter")
	tab := m.activeTab().(*dataTab)
	if _, v, _ := tab.grid.Cell(); v.Text != "user2" {
		t.Fatalf("grid search at %q", v.Text)
	}
	press(t, m, "N")
	if _, v, _ := tab.grid.Cell(); v.Text != "user2" {
		t.Fatal("N wraps back to the only match")
	}
}

func TestQuitClosesConsoleSessions(t *testing.T) {
	m, b := newTestModel(t)
	press(t, m, "l", "j", "space", "c", "enter") // empty editor: nothing runs
	c := m.activeTab().(*consoleTab)
	c.editor.SetValue("select 1")
	press(t, m, "enter")
	if b.conn.sessionCount() != 1 {
		t.Fatal("a session was opened")
	}
	if !press(t, m, ":", "q", "a", "enter") {
		t.Fatal(":qa quits")
	}
	if !b.conn.sessions[0].closed {
		t.Fatal("sessions are closed on quit")
	}
}

func TestStructureFromConsoleNeedsATable(t *testing.T) {

	press(t, m, "l", "j", "space", "c", "space", "s")
	if !strings.Contains(screen(m), "select a table first") {
		t.Fatalf("screen:\n%s", screen(m))
	}
}
```

新增辅助方法：

```go
// capturingTab returns the focused tab when it wants raw input (INSERT mode).
func (m *Model) capturingTab() inputCapturer {
	if m.focus != focusTabs {
		return nil
	}
	if c, ok := m.activeTab().(inputCapturer); ok && c.CapturesInput() {
		return c
	}
	return nil
}

func (m *Model) mode() string {
	if m.focus == focusTabs {
		if md, ok := m.activeTab().(moder); ok {
			return md.Mode()
		}
	}
	return "NORMAL"
}

func (m *Model) find(forward bool) {
	q := m.lastSearch
	if q == "" {
		m.setStatus("no previous search", true)
		return
	}
	found := false
	if m.focus == focusTree {
		found = m.tree.Find(q, forward)
	} else if f, ok := m.activeTab().(finder); ok {
		found = f.Find(q, forward)
	}
	if !found {
		m.setStatus("pattern not found: "+q, true)
	}
}

// contextNamespace is the connection and namespace the user is looking at.
func (m *Model) contextNamespace() (string, db.Namespace) {
	if m.focus == focusTabs {
		if t := m.activeTab(); t != nil {
			conn, ref := t.Target()
			return conn, ref.Namespace
		}
	}
	if n := m.tree.Selected(); n != nil {
		return n.Conn, n.NS
	}
	return "", db.Namespace{}
}

func (m *Model) openConsole(conn string, ns db.Namespace) tea.Cmd {
	return m.openTab(consoleTabKey(conn, ns), func(id int) tab {
		return newConsoleTab(id, m.backend, conn, ns, m.settings, m.timings)
	})
}

func shutdownCmd(t tab) tea.Cmd {
	s, ok := t.(shutdowner)
	if !ok {
		return nil
	}
	f := s.shutdown()
	return func() tea.Msg { f(); return nil }
}

// quit runs every tab's cleanup, then quits.
func (m *Model) quit() tea.Cmd {
	var fs []func()
	for _, t := range m.tabs {
		if s, ok := t.(shutdowner); ok {
			fs = append(fs, s.shutdown())
		}
	}
	return func() tea.Msg {
		for _, f := range fs {
			f()
		}
		return tea.QuitMsg{}
	}
}
```

```go
func (m *Model) closeTab(i int) tea.Cmd {
	cmd := shutdownCmd(m.tabs[i])
	m.tabs = append(m.tabs[:i], m.tabs[i+1:]...)
	m.active = textutil.Clamp(m.active, 0, len(m.tabs)-1)
	if len(m.tabs) == 0 {
		m.showTree = true
		m.focus = focusTree
	}
	m.layout()
	return cmd
}
```

### Task 19: SQL 历史、草稿自动保存、`<space>h`、`<space>v`

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/ui/store.go`
- Modify: `internal/ui/console.go`, `internal/ui/app.go`, `internal/ui/fakes_test.go`, `internal/ui/app_test.go`, `internal/ui/console_test.go`, `cmd/sqlvim/main.go`
- Test: `internal/ui/console_test.go`, `internal/ui/app_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7 `history.Entry`/`history.Store`；Task 1 `Settings.EditorCommand`；Task 14 `overlay.NewPicker`。
- Produces:
  - `type Store interface { AddHistory(e history.Entry) error; History(filter string, limit int) ([]history.Entry, error); SaveDraft(key, text string) error; Draft(key string) (string, error) }`
  - `func New(b Backend, st Store, settings config.Settings) *Model`（**签名变更**）
  - `func newConsoleTab(id int, b Backend, st Store, conn string, ns db.Namespace, s config.Settings, tm timings) *consoleTab`（**签名变更**）
  - `func (t *consoleTab) insertText(sql string) tea.Cmd`
  - 消息：`draftLoadedMsg`、`draftTickMsg`、`editFileMsg`、`editDoneMsg`、`editTextMsg`（tab 消息）；`historyLoadedMsg`、`historyPickedMsg`（根 Model）
  - `fakeStore`（测试替身）

**行为：**
- 每条执行过的语句（含失败、取消）写入历史：连接名、库、SQL、耗时、错误文本。被保护规则拒绝的语句没有执行，不写。
- 草稿键为控制台的 `Key()`。打开控制台时加载草稿（编辑器已有内容时，草稿放在前面、当前内容接在后面，两者都不丢）；内容变化后空闲 `timings.draft`（1 秒）保存；关闭 tab 或退出时同步保存。
- `<space>h`：读取最近 500 条历史，在选择器中按 `✓/✗ 时间 连接/库 SQL` 显示；回车把 SQL 追加到当前控制台（编辑器为空时直接替换），当前不在控制台时为该条历史的连接/库打开控制台再追加；连接已不存在时提示。
- `<space>v`：把编辑器内容写入临时文件 `sqlvim-*.sql`，用 `Settings.EditorCommand` 打开（`tea.ExecProcess` 挂起 TUI），编辑器正常退出后读回内容替换编辑器；编辑器以非零状态退出（如 vim 的 `:cq`）时保持原内容；临时文件总会删除。
- `main` 打开 `$XDG_STATE_HOME/sqlvim/state.db`，失败时记录警告并改用内存库。

`internal/ui/fakes_test.go` 追加：

```go
type fakeStore struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	entries []history.Entry
	drafts  map[string]string
}

func newFakeStore() *fakeStore { return &fakeStore{drafts: map[string]string{}} }

func (s *fakeStore) AddHistory(e history.Entry) error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.entries = append(s.entries, e)
	return nil
}

func (s *fakeStore) History(_ string, limit int) ([]history.Entry, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	var out []history.Entry
	for i := len(s.entries) - 1; i >= 0 && len(out) < limit; i-- {
		out = append(out, s.entries[i])
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *fakeStore) SaveDraft(key, text string) error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.drafts[key] = text
	return nil
}

func (s *fakeStore) Draft(key string) (string, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return s.drafts[key], nil
}

func (s *fakeStore) draft(key string) string {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return s.drafts[key]
}
```

追加到 `internal/ui/console_test.go`（import 增加 `"os"`）：

```go
func TestConsoleRecordsHistory(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	b.conn.setFail("select bad", errors.New("syntax error"))
	c, st := newConsoleWithStore(t, b, "local")
	c.editor.SetValue("select 1; select bad; select 3")
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunAll))
	if len(st.entries) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("entries = %+v (stops at the error)", st.entries)
	}
	ok, bad := st.entries[0], st.entries[1]
	if ok.SQL != "select 1" || ok.Conn != "local" || ok.Database != "app" || ok.Err != "" || ok.Time.IsZero() {
		t.Fatalf("ok entry = %+v", ok)
	}
	if bad.SQL != "select bad" || bad.Err != "syntax error" {
		t.Fatalf("bad entry = %+v", bad)
	}
}

func TestConsoleRejectedStatementsAreNotRecorded(t *testing.T) {
	c, st := newConsoleWithStore(t, newFakeBackend(), "prod")
	c.editor.SetValue("delete from t where id = 1")
	runTab(t, c, c.Handle(keymap.RunStatement))
	if len(st.entries) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("entries = %+v", st.entries)
	}
}

func TestConsoleDrafts(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	c, st := newConsoleWithStore(t, b, "local")
	st.drafts[c.Key()] = "select draft"
	runTab(t, c, c.Init())
	if c.editor.Value() != "select draft" {
		t.Fatalf("draft not restored: %q", c.editor.Value())
	}
	c.Handle(keymap.InsertMode)
	c.editor.Bottom()
	runTab(t, c, c.Input(uitest.Key("x")))
	if got := st.draft(c.Key()); got != "select draftx" {
		t.Fatalf("draft after typing = %q", got)
	}
	c.editor.SetValue("select final")
	c.shutdown()()
	if got := st.draft(c.Key()); got != "select final" {
		t.Fatalf("draft on close = %q", got)
	}
}

func TestDraftMergesWithTextTypedBeforeItLoaded(t *testing.T) {
	c, st := newConsoleWithStore(t, newFakeBackend(), "local")
	st.drafts[c.Key()] = "select old"
	c.editor.SetValue("select new")
	runTab(t, c, c.Init())
	if got := c.editor.Value(); got != "select old\nselect new" {
		t.Fatalf("value = %q", got)
	}
}

func TestExternalEdit(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsole(t, newFakeBackend(), "local")
	c.editor.SetValue("select 1")
	file, ok := run(c.Handle(keymap.ExternalEdit)).(editFileMsg)
	if !ok || file.err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("msg = %#v", file)
	}
	if data, _ := os.ReadFile(file.path); string(data) != "select 1" {
		t.Fatalf("temp file = %q", data)
	}
	if !strings.HasSuffix(file.path, ".sql") {
		t.Fatalf("path = %s", file.path)
	}
	if cmd := c.Update(file); cmd == nil {
		t.Fatal("the editor process is started")
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(file.path, []byte("select 2"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	runTab(t, c, c.Update(editDoneMsg{tabID: c.id, path: file.path}))
	if c.editor.Value() != "select 2" {
		t.Fatalf("value = %q", c.editor.Value())
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(file.path); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		t.Fatal("temp file removed")
	}

	file2 := run(c.Handle(keymap.ExternalEdit)).(editFileMsg)
	_ = os.WriteFile(file2.path, []byte("garbage"), 0o600)
	runTab(t, c, c.Update(editDoneMsg{tabID: c.id, path: file2.path, err: errors.New("exit status 1")}))
	if c.editor.Value() != "select 2" {
		t.Fatal("a failed editor keeps the text")
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(file2.path); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		t.Fatal("temp file removed after a failed edit too")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现**

`internal/ui/store.go`：

```go
package ui

import "github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/history"

// Store keeps SQL history and console drafts; *history.Store implements it.
type Store interface {
	AddHistory(e history.Entry) error
	History(filter string, limit int) ([]history.Entry, error)
	SaveDraft(key, text string) error
	Draft(key string) (string, error)
}
```

`internal/ui/console.go` 的修改：

1. `consoleTab` 增加字段 `store Store`、`editorCmd []string`、`draftGen int`；构造函数：

```go
func newConsoleTab(id int, b Backend, st Store, conn string, ns db.Namespace, s config.Settings, tm timings) *consoleTab {
	cfg, _ := b.Connection(conn)
	return &consoleTab{
		id: id, backend: b, store: st, conn: conn, ns: ns, dialect: sqlx.DialectOf(cfg.Driver),
		maxRows: s.MaxRows, timings: tm, editorCmd: s.EditorCommand(os.Getenv),
		editor: editor.New(), grid: grid.New(nil, s.MaxColumnWidth),
	}
}
```

2. 新增消息类型与草稿逻辑，并用下面的 `Init` **替换** Task 17 中返回 nil 的 `Init`：

```go
type (
	draftLoadedMsg struct {
		tabID int
		text  string
	}
	draftTickMsg struct{ tabID, gen int }
	editFileMsg  struct {
		tabID int
		path  string
		err   error
	}
	editDoneMsg struct {
		tabID int
		path  string
		err   error
	}
	editTextMsg struct {
		tabID int
		text  string
		err   error
	}
)

func (m draftLoadedMsg) TabID() int { return m.tabID }
func (m draftTickMsg) TabID() int   { return m.tabID }
func (m editFileMsg) TabID() int    { return m.tabID }
func (m editDoneMsg) TabID() int    { return m.tabID }
func (m editTextMsg) TabID() int    { return m.tabID }

func (t *consoleTab) Init() tea.Cmd {
	id, st, key := t.id, t.store, t.Key()
	return func() tea.Msg {
		text, err := st.Draft(key)
		if err != nil {
			slog.Warn("load draft", "err", err)
		}
		return draftLoadedMsg{tabID: id, text: text}
	}
}

func (t *consoleTab) scheduleDraft() tea.Cmd {
	t.draftGen++
	id, gen := t.id, t.draftGen
	return tea.Tick(t.timings.draft, func(time.Time) tea.Msg { return draftTickMsg{tabID: id, gen: gen} })
}

func (t *consoleTab) saveDraft() func() {
	st, key, text := t.store, t.Key(), t.editor.Value()
	return func() {
		if err := st.SaveDraft(key, text); err != nil {
			slog.Warn("save draft", "err", err)
		}
	}
}

// insertText appends sql to the editor (or fills an empty editor).
func (t *consoleTab) insertText(sql string) tea.Cmd {
	if t.editor.Value() == "" {
		t.editor.SetValue(sql)
	} else {
		t.editor.Bottom()
		t.editor.InsertString("\n" + sql)
	}
	t.onResults = false
	return t.scheduleDraft()
}

func (t *consoleTab) externalEdit() tea.Cmd {
	id, text := t.id, t.editor.Value()
	return func() tea.Msg {
		f, err := os.CreateTemp("", "sqlvim-*.sql")
		if err != nil {
			return editFileMsg{tabID: id, err: err}
		}
		_, err = f.WriteString(text)
		return editFileMsg{tabID: id, path: f.Name(), err: errors.Join(err, f.Close())}
	}
}
```

5. `Update` 增加分支，`runDoneMsg` 改为返回 `t.done(m)`：

```go
	case runDoneMsg:
		return t.done(m)
	case draftLoadedMsg:
		switch cur := t.editor.Value(); {
		case m.text == "" || m.text == cur:
		case cur == "":
			t.editor.SetValue(m.text)
		default:
			t.editor.SetValue(m.text + "\n" + cur)
		}
	case draftTickMsg:
		if m.gen == t.draftGen {
			save := t.saveDraft()
			return func() tea.Msg { save(); return nil }
		}
	case editFileMsg:
		if m.err != nil {
			t.setStatus("cannot create temp file: "+m.err.Error(), true)
			return nil
		}
		argv := append(slices.Clone(t.editorCmd), m.path)
		id, path := t.id, m.path
		return tea.ExecProcess(exec.Command(argv[0], argv[1:]...), func(err error) tea.Msg {
			return editDoneMsg{tabID: id, path: path, err: err}
		})
	case editDoneMsg:
		id, path, editErr := t.id, m.path, m.err
		return func() tea.Msg {
			defer func() { _ = os.Remove(path) }()
			if editErr != nil {
				return editTextMsg{tabID: id, err: editErr}
			}
			data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
			return editTextMsg{tabID: id, text: string(data), err: err}
		}
	case editTextMsg:
		if m.err != nil {
			t.setStatus("editor: "+m.err.Error()+" · text unchanged", true)
			return nil
		}
		t.editor.SetValue(strings.TrimRight(m.text, "\n"))
		t.setStatus("updated from "+t.editorCmd[0], false)
		return t.scheduleDraft()
```

6. `done` 改为返回 `tea.Cmd`：各个提前 `return` 改为 `return nil`，函数末尾 `return t.record(m.results)`；新增：

```go
func (t *consoleTab) record(results []runner.Result) tea.Cmd {
	if len(results) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	st, now := t.store, time.Now()
	entries := make([]history.Entry, len(results))
	for i, r := range results {
		entries[i] = history.Entry{Time: now, Conn: t.conn, Database: t.ns.Database, SQL: r.SQL, Duration: r.Duration}
		if r.Err != nil {
			entries[i].Err = errorText(r.Err)
		}
	}
	return func() tea.Msg {
		for _, e := range entries {
			if err := st.AddHistory(e); err != nil {
				slog.Warn("save history", "err", err)
				break
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
}
```

7. `shutdown` 同时保存草稿：

```go
func (t *consoleTab) shutdown() func() {
	s, cancel, save := t.session, t.cancel, t.saveDraft()
	t.session = nil
	return func() {
		if cancel != nil {
			cancel()
		}
		if s != nil {
			_ = s.Close()
		}
		save()
	}
}
```

`internal/ui/app.go`：`Model` 增加 `store Store`；`New(b Backend, st Store, settings config.Settings)` 保存它；`openConsole` 传入 `m.store`。新增：

```go
const historyLimit = 500

type historyLoadedMsg struct {
	entries []history.Entry
	err     error
}

type historyPickedMsg struct{ entry history.Entry }

func loadHistory(st Store) tea.Cmd {
	return func() tea.Msg {
		entries, err := st.History("", historyLimit)
		return historyLoadedMsg{entries: entries, err: err}
	}
}

func historyLabel(e history.Entry) string {
	loc := e.Conn
	if e.Database != "" {
		loc += "/" + e.Database
	}
	mark := "✓"
	if e.Err != "" {
		mark = "✗"
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s %s  %s  %s", mark, e.Time.Local().Format("01-02 15:04"), loc, strings.Join(strings.Fields(e.SQL), " "))
}

func (m *Model) historyLoaded(msg historyLoadedMsg) {
	switch {
	case msg.err != nil:
		m.setStatus("history: "+msg.err.Error(), true)
	case len(msg.entries) == 0:
		m.setStatus("no SQL history yet", false)
	default:
		labels := make([]string, len(msg.entries))
		for i, e := range msg.entries {
			labels[i] = historyLabel(e)
		}
		entries := msg.entries
		m.overlay = overlay.NewPicker("SQL history", labels, func(i int) tea.Msg { return historyPickedMsg{entry: entries[i]} })
	}
}

func (m *Model) useHistory(e history.Entry) tea.Cmd {
	if c, ok := m.activeTab().(*consoleTab); ok && m.focus == focusTabs {
		return c.insertText(e.SQL)
	}
	if _, ok := m.backend.Connection(e.Conn); !ok {
		m.setStatus("connection "+e.Conn+" no longer exists", true)
		return nil
	}
	open := m.openConsole(e.Conn, db.Namespace{Database: e.Database})
	c := m.activeTab().(*consoleTab)
	return tea.Batch(open, c.insertText(e.SQL))
}
```


```go
// openStore opens state.db, falling back to an in-memory store so a broken
// state file never stops sqlvim from starting.
func openStore(stateDir string) (*history.Store, error) {
	st, err := history.Open(filepath.Join(stateDir, "state.db"))
	if err == nil {
		return st, nil
	}
	slog.Warn("state database unavailable; history and drafts will not be kept", "err", err)
	return history.Open(":memory:")
}
```

### Task 20: 复制与导出接线

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/ui/clipboard/clipboard.go`, `internal/ui/transfer.go`
- Modify: `internal/ui/app.go`, `internal/ui/tabs.go`（抽出 `selectAll`）
- Test: `internal/ui/clipboard/clipboard_test.go`, `internal/ui/transfer_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 8/9 `export.*`；Task 10 `CopyFormat`/`ExportFormat`/`YankCell`/`YankRow`；Task 11 `grid.Row`/`Result`；Task 13 `NewChoice`/`NewPrompt`；Task 1 `Settings.ExportDirectory`。
- Produces:
  - `package clipboard`：`func Copy(text string) tea.Cmd`、`func usePbcopy(goos string, getenv func(string) string, lookPath func(string) (string, error)) bool`
  - `package ui`：`var copyToClipboard = clipboard.Copy`（测试替换）、`type exportSrc struct{…}`、`type exporter interface{ exportSource() exportSrc }`、`type gridder interface{ activeGrid() *grid.Model }`、`func selectAll(d db.Dialect, ref db.TableRef, orderBy []string) string`、`func tableParts(driver string, ref db.TableRef) []string`、`func expandHome(p, home string) string`、消息 `exportScopeMsg`/`exportPathMsg`/`exportDoneMsg`
  - `Model.home string`（`New` 中取 `os.UserHomeDir()`）

**行为：**
- `<space>y?`：把当前 tab 的结果集（数据 tab 为当前页；控制台为表格中的结果）编码后复制；状态栏 `copied N rows as CSV`。没有结果时 `nothing to copy`。
- `yl` 复制单元格文本（NULL 复制为 `NULL`），`yy` 复制当前行（TSV、无表头）。
- `<space>x?`：数据 tab 先选「Current page (N rows)」或「Whole table」；然后弹出路径输入框，默认 `<export_dir>/<表名或 query>-<时间戳>.<ext>`；回车后在 Cmd 中导出（自动创建目录，已存在的文件不覆盖），完成后状态栏 `exported N rows to PATH`。整表导出用 `Stream` 流式写出，与数据 tab 相同的 `ORDER BY` 主键顺序，不加 `LIMIT`。
- INSERT 的表名：数据 tab 为 `库.表`（MySQL）或 `schema.表`（PG）；控制台为最后结果语句的 `Target`，否则 `query`。
- 剪贴板：本机 macOS（无 `SSH_TTY`）且有 `pbcopy` 时用 `pbcopy`，否则（或 `pbcopy` 失败）用 OSC 52（`tea.SetClipboard`）。

`internal/ui/clipboard/clipboard_test.go`：

```go
package clipboard

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"
)

func TestUsePbcopy(t *testing.T) {
	found := func(string) (string, error) { return "/usr/bin/pbcopy", nil }
	missing := func(string) (string, error) { return "", errors.New("not found") }
	env := func(m map[string]string) func(string) string { return func(k string) string { return m[k] } }
	cases := []struct {
		goos string
		env  map[string]string
		look func(string) (string, error)
		want bool
	}{
		{"darwin", nil, found, true},
		{"darwin", map[string]string{"SSH_TTY": "/dev/ttys001"}, found, false},
		{"darwin", nil, missing, false},
		{"linux", nil, found, false},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		if got := usePbcopy(c.goos, env(c.env), c.look); got != c.want {
			t.Errorf("usePbcopy(%s, %v) = %v", c.goos, c.env, got)
		}
	}
}
```

```go
package ui

func captureClipboard(t *testing.T) *string {
	t.Helper()
	var got string
	old := copyToClipboard
	copyToClipboard = func(s string) tea.Cmd { got = s; return nil }
	t.Cleanup(func() { copyToClipboard = old })
	return &got
}

func TestCopyAsFromDataTab(t *testing.T) {

	openUsers(t, m)
	press(t, m, "space", "y", "c")
	if *clip != "id,name\n1,user1\n2,user2\n" {
		t.Fatalf("clipboard = %q", *clip)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(screen(m), "copied 2 rows as CSV") {
		t.Fatalf("screen:\n%s", screen(m))

func TestYankCellAndRow(t *testing.T) {

func exportedFile(t *testing.T, pattern string) string {
```

`internal/ui/clipboard/clipboard.go`：

```go
// Package clipboard copies text to the system clipboard.
package clipboard

import (
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"runtime"
	"strings"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
)

// Copy puts text on the clipboard: pbcopy in a local macOS session,
// otherwise the terminal's OSC 52 sequence (which also works over SSH).
func Copy(text string) tea.Cmd {
	return func() tea.Msg {
		if usePbcopy(runtime.GOOS, os.Getenv, exec.LookPath) {
			cmd := exec.Command("pbcopy")
			cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(text)
			if err := cmd.Run(); err == nil {
				return nil
			}
		}
		return tea.SetClipboard(text)()
	}
}

func usePbcopy(goos string, getenv func(string) string, lookPath func(string) (string, error)) bool {
	if goos != "darwin" || getenv("SSH_TTY") != "" {
		return false
	}
	_, err := lookPath("pbcopy")
	return err == nil
}
```

`internal/ui/tabs.go`：把 `fetchPage` 中拼 SQL 的部分抽成 `selectAll`：

```go
// selectAll is the table-data query in primary-key order.
func selectAll(d db.Dialect, ref db.TableRef, orderBy []string) string {
	q := "SELECT * FROM " + d.QualifiedName(ref)
	if len(orderBy) > 0 {
		quoted := make([]string, len(orderBy))
		for i, col := range orderBy {
			quoted[i] = d.QuoteIdent(col)
		}
		q += " ORDER BY " + strings.Join(quoted, ", ")
	}
	return q
}

func fetchPage(ctx context.Context, c db.Conn, ref db.TableRef, orderBy []string, page, size int) (*db.ResultSet, bool, error) {
	d := c.Dialect()
	rs, err := c.Query(ctx, d.Paginate(selectAll(d, ref, orderBy), size+1, page*size))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, false, err
	}
	hasMore := len(rs.Rows) > size
	if hasMore {
		rs.Rows = rs.Rows[:size]
	}
	return rs, hasMore, nil
}
```

`internal/ui/transfer.go`：

```go
package ui

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"time"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/export"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/sqlx"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/clipboard"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/keymap"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/overlay"
	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/ui/panes/grid"
)

// copyToClipboard is replaced in tests.
var copyToClipboard = clipboard.Copy

const exportTimeout = 10 * time.Minute

// exportSrc is what a tab offers for copy and export.
type exportSrc struct {
	rs      *db.ResultSet
	name    string // file name base
	opts    export.Options
	conn    string
	ref     db.TableRef
	orderBy []string
	whole   bool // the whole table can be streamed (data tabs)
}

type (
	exporter interface{ exportSource() exportSrc }
	gridder  interface{ activeGrid() *grid.Model }
)

type exportScopeMsg struct {
	tabID  int
	format export.Format
	whole  bool
}

type exportPathMsg struct {
	tabID  int
	format export.Format
	whole  bool
	path   string
}

type exportDoneMsg struct {
	path string
	rows int
	err  error
}

// tableParts is the INSERT target: database.table (MySQL) or schema.table (PostgreSQL).
func tableParts(driver string, ref db.TableRef) []string {
	ns := ref.Database
	if driver == "postgres" {
		ns = ref.Schema
	}
	if ns == "" {
		return []string{ref.Name}
	}
	return []string{ns, ref.Name}
}

func (t *dataTab) exportSource() exportSrc {
	cfg, _ := t.backend.Connection(t.conn)
	return exportSrc{
		rs: t.grid.Result(), name: t.ref.Name, conn: t.conn, ref: t.ref, orderBy: t.orderBy, whole: true,
		opts: export.Options{Driver: cfg.Driver, Table: tableParts(cfg.Driver, t.ref)},
	}
}

func (t *dataTab) activeGrid() *grid.Model { return t.grid }

func (t *consoleTab) exportSource() exportSrc {
	cfg, _ := t.backend.Connection(t.conn)
	src := exportSrc{rs: t.grid.Result(), name: "query", conn: t.conn, opts: export.Options{Driver: cfg.Driver}}
	if t.last != nil {
		if target := sqlx.Analyze(t.last.SQL, t.dialect).Target; target != "" {
			src.name, src.opts.Table = target, []string{target}
		}
	}
	return src
}

func (t *consoleTab) activeGrid() *grid.Model { return t.grid }

func expandHome(p, home string) string {
	switch {
	case p == "~":
		return home
	case strings.HasPrefix(p, "~/"):
		return filepath.Join(home, p[2:])
	}
	return p
}

func (m *Model) source() (exportSrc, bool) {
	e, ok := m.activeTab().(exporter)
	if !ok || m.focus != focusTabs {
		return exportSrc{}, false
	}
	src := e.exportSource()
	return src, src.rs != nil && len(src.rs.Columns) > 0
}

func (m *Model) copyAs(f export.Format) tea.Cmd {
	src, ok := m.source()
	if !ok {
		m.setStatus("nothing to copy", true)
		return nil
	}
	text, err := export.Encode(f, src.rs, src.opts)
	if err != nil {
		m.setStatus(err.Error(), true)
		return nil
	}
	m.setStatus(fmt.Sprintf("copied %d rows as %s", len(src.rs.Rows), f.Label()), false)
	return copyToClipboard(text)
}

func (m *Model) yank(a keymap.Action) tea.Cmd {
	g, ok := m.activeTab().(gridder)
	if !ok {
		return nil
	}
	gr := g.activeGrid()
	if a == keymap.YankCell {
		col, v, ok := gr.Cell()
		if !ok {
			m.setStatus("nothing to copy", true)
			return nil
		}
		text := v.Text
		if v.Null {
			text = "NULL"
		}
		m.setStatus("copied "+col.Name, false)
		return copyToClipboard(text)
	}
	row, ok := gr.Row()
	if !ok {
		m.setStatus("nothing to copy", true)
		return nil
	}
	one := &db.ResultSet{Columns: gr.Result().Columns, Rows: [][]db.Value{row}}
	text, err := export.Encode(export.TSV, one, export.Options{NoHeader: true})
	if err != nil {
		m.setStatus(err.Error(), true)
		return nil
	}
	m.setStatus("copied row", false)
	return copyToClipboard(text)
}

func (m *Model) exportAs(f export.Format) tea.Cmd {
	src, ok := m.source()
	if !ok {
		m.setStatus("nothing to export", true)
		return nil
	}
	id := m.activeTab().ID()
	if src.whole {
		m.overlay = overlay.NewChoice("Export "+f.Label(),
			[]string{fmt.Sprintf("Current page (%d rows)", len(src.rs.Rows)), "Whole table"},
			func(i int) tea.Msg { return exportScopeMsg{tabID: id, format: f, whole: i == 1} })
		return nil
	}
	m.askExportPath(id, f, false, src.name)
	return nil
}

func (m *Model) askExportPath(tabID int, f export.Format, whole bool, name string) {
	path := filepath.Join(m.settings.ExportDirectory(m.home), export.FileName(name, f, time.Now()))
	m.overlay = overlay.NewPrompt("Export "+f.Label(), "Save to:", path, func(p string) tea.Msg {
		return exportPathMsg{tabID: tabID, format: f, whole: whole, path: p}
	})
}

func (m *Model) exportScope(msg exportScopeMsg) {
	if e, ok := m.tabByID(msg.tabID).(exporter); ok {
		m.askExportPath(msg.tabID, msg.format, msg.whole, e.exportSource().name)
	}
}

func (m *Model) exportTo(msg exportPathMsg) tea.Cmd {
	e, ok := m.tabByID(msg.tabID).(exporter)
	if !ok {
		m.setStatus("the tab was closed", true)
		return nil
	}
	path := expandHome(strings.TrimSpace(msg.path), m.home)
	m.setStatus("exporting to "+path+"…", false)
	return exportCmd(m.backend, e.exportSource(), msg.format, msg.whole, path)
}

func exportCmd(b Backend, src exportSrc, f export.Format, whole bool, path string) tea.Cmd {
	return func() tea.Msg {
		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o755); err != nil {
			return exportDoneMsg{path: path, err: err}
		}
		if !whole {
			err := export.ToFile(path, f, src.opts, func(w export.Writer) error { return export.WriteRows(w, src.rs) })
			return exportDoneMsg{path: path, rows: len(src.rs.Rows), err: err}
		}
		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), exportTimeout)
		defer cancel()
		rows := 0
		c, err := b.Conn(ctx, src.conn, src.ref.Database)
		if err == nil {
			err = streamTable(ctx, c, src, f, path, &rows)
		}
		return exportDoneMsg{path: path, rows: rows, err: err}
	}
}

// streamTable writes the whole table to path without holding it in memory.
func streamTable(ctx context.Context, c db.Conn, src exportSrc, f export.Format, path string, rows *int) error {
	*rows = 0
	return export.ToFile(path, f, src.opts, func(w export.Writer) error {
		var headerErr error
		err := c.Stream(ctx, selectAll(c.Dialect(), src.ref, src.orderBy),
			func(cols []db.ColumnInfo) { headerErr = w.WriteHeader(cols) },
			func(row []db.Value) error {
				if headerErr != nil {
					return headerErr
				}
				*rows++
				return w.WriteRow(row)
			})
		return errors.Join(err, headerErr)
	})
}

func (m *Model) exportDone(msg exportDoneMsg) {
	if msg.err != nil {
		m.setStatus("export failed: "+msg.err.Error(), true)
		return
	}
	m.setStatus(fmt.Sprintf("exported %d rows to %s", msg.rows, msg.path), false)
}
```

### Task 21: 断线自动重连

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/ui/reconnect.go`
- Modify: `internal/ui/loaders.go`, `internal/ui/tabs.go`, `internal/ui/console.go`, `internal/ui/transfer.go`, `internal/ui/app.go`, `internal/ui/fakes_test.go`
- Test: `internal/ui/reconnect_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5 `db.IsConnLost`；Task 6 `Backend.Reset`；Task 12 `tree.SetBroken`/`Broken`。
- Produces:
  - `func withConn(ctx context.Context, b Backend, conn, database string, fn func(db.Conn) error) error`
  - `type connFailure interface{ failure() (conn string, err error) }`，由 `childrenLoadedMsg`、`pageLoadedMsg`、`structureLoadedMsg`、`runDoneMsg`、`exportDoneMsg` 实现（后四者新增 `conn string` 字段）
  - `func fetchChildren(ctx context.Context, c db.Conn, kind tree.Kind, connName string, ns db.Namespace) ([]*tree.Node, error)`（**签名变更**：接收已打开的连接）
  - `fakeBackend.lostConns int`：接下来这么多次 `Conn` 调用返回 `db.ErrConnLost`

**规则：**
- 树加载、表数据分页、结构加载、整表导出都走 `withConn`：断线时 `Reset` 该连接并**重试一次**；这些操作都是只读的，重试安全。
- 控制台不走 `withConn`（见 Task 17 的 `ensureSession`），语句从不自动重试。
- 根 Model 在处理完每条实现了 `connFailure` 的消息后：错误是断线 → 树中该连接 `SetBroken(true)`，状态栏 `connection X lost: select it in the tree and press enter to retry`；没有错误 → `SetBroken(false)`。
- 在已断开（`Broken`）的连接根节点上按 `l`/`enter`：先 `Reset` 后端连接、`ResetConn` 树节点，再重新加载。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

`internal/ui/fakes_test.go` 的 `fakeBackend` 增加字段 `lostConns int`，`Conn` 中在加锁之后、检查 `connErr` 之前加：

```go
	if f.lostConns > 0 {
		f.lostConns--
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: connection reset by peer", db.ErrConnLost)
	}
```

并加辅助方法：

```go
func (f *fakeBackend) setLost(n int) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.lostConns = n
}

func (f *fakeBackend) resetList() []string {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return slices.Clone(f.resets)
}
```

`internal/ui/reconnect_test.go`：

```go
package ui

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

func TestWithConnRetriesOnceAfterConnectionLoss(t *testing.T) {
	b := newFakeBackend()
	calls := 0
	err := withConn(context.Background(), b, "local", "app", func(db.Conn) error {
		calls++
		if calls == 1 {
			return fmt.Errorf("query: %w", db.ErrConnLost)
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil || calls != 2 || !slices.Equal(b.resetList(), []string{"local"}) {
		t.Fatalf("err=%v calls=%d resets=%v", err, calls, b.resetList())
	}

	calls = 0
	boom := errors.New("syntax error")
	err = withConn(context.Background(), b, "local", "app", func(db.Conn) error { calls++; return boom })
	if !errors.Is(err, boom) || calls != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("other errors are not retried: err=%v calls=%d", err, calls)
	}

	calls = 0
	err = withConn(context.Background(), b, "local", "app", func(db.Conn) error {
		calls++
		return db.ErrConnLost
	})
	if !db.IsConnLost(err) || calls != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("only one retry: err=%v calls=%d", err, calls)
	}
}

func TestTreeLoadReconnects(t *testing.T) {
	m, b := newTestModel(t)
	b.setLost(1)
	press(t, m, "l")
	if s := screen(m); !strings.Contains(s, "app") || strings.Contains(s, "disconnected") {
		t.Fatalf("one lost connection is retried:\n%s", s)
	}
	if !slices.Equal(b.resetList(), []string{"local"}) {
		t.Fatalf("resets = %v", b.resetList())
	}
}

func TestBrokenConnectionIsMarkedAndRetried(t *testing.T) {
	m, b := newTestModel(t)
	b.setLost(2)
	press(t, m, "l")
	s := screen(m)
	if !strings.Contains(s, "disconnected") || !strings.Contains(s, "connection local lost") {
		t.Fatalf("screen:\n%s", s)
	}
	press(t, m, "enter")
	if s := screen(m); strings.Contains(s, "disconnected") || !strings.Contains(s, "app") {
		t.Fatalf("enter retries:\n%s", s)
	}
}

	press(t, m, "space", "r")

func TestConsoleConnectionLossMarksTree(t *testing.T) {
	m, b := newTestModel(t)
	b.conn.setFail("select 1", fmt.Errorf("%w: broken pipe", db.ErrConnLost))
	press(t, m, "l", "j", "space", "c")

	press(t, m, "enter")
	if !strings.Contains(screen(m), "disconnected") {

	}
	b.conn.setFail("select 1", nil)
	press(t, m, "enter")
	if strings.Contains(screen(m), "disconnected") {
		t.Fatal("a successful run clears the mark")
	}
}
```

`internal/ui/reconnect.go`：

```go
package ui

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"

	"github.com/shepherd-yun/sqlvim/internal/db"
)

// withConn runs a read-only fn on the named connection. If the connection
// turns out to be dead, the pool is reset and fn is retried once.
func withConn(ctx context.Context, b Backend, conn, database string, fn func(db.Conn) error) error {
	c, err := b.Conn(ctx, conn, database)
	if err == nil {
		err = fn(c)
	}
	if !db.IsConnLost(err) {
		return err
	}
	slog.Info("connection lost, reconnecting", "conn", conn, "err", err)
	b.Reset(conn)
	if c, err = b.Conn(ctx, conn, database); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return fn(c)
}

// connFailure is implemented by results of work on a connection; the root
// model uses it to mark connections as disconnected (or healthy again).
type connFailure interface{ failure() (conn string, err error) }

func (m childrenLoadedMsg) failure() (string, error)  { return m.node.Conn, m.err }
func (m pageLoadedMsg) failure() (string, error)      { return m.conn, m.err }
func (m structureLoadedMsg) failure() (string, error) { return m.conn, m.err }
func (m exportDoneMsg) failure() (string, error)      { return m.conn, m.err }

func (m runDoneMsg) failure() (string, error) {
	if m.err != nil || len(m.results) == 0 {
		return m.conn, m.err
	}
	return m.conn, m.results[len(m.results)-1].Err
}
```

```go
func loadChildren(b Backend, n *tree.Node) tea.Cmd {
	kind, connName, ns := n.Kind, n.Conn, n.NS
	return func() tea.Msg {
		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), loadTimeout)
		defer cancel()
		var children []*tree.Node
		err := withConn(ctx, b, connName, ns.Database, func(c db.Conn) error {
			var err error
			children, err = fetchChildren(ctx, c, kind, connName, ns)
			return err
		})
		if err != nil {
			slog.Warn("load tree children", "conn", connName, "database", ns.Database, "schema", ns.Schema, "err", err)
		}
		return childrenLoadedMsg{node: n, children: children, err: err}
	}
}

	meta := c.Meta()
	switch kind {
	// …（各 case 与原来相同）
	}
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("tree node kind %d has no children", kind)
}
```

（只删除原函数开头的 `conn, err := b.Conn(…)` 与错误检查，把 `meta := conn.Meta()` 改为 `meta := c.Meta()`；switch 内容不变。）

`internal/ui/tabs.go`：`pageLoadedMsg`、`structureLoadedMsg` 增加 `conn string` 字段；`dataTab.load` 与 `structureTab.load` 改用 `withConn`：

```go
func (t *dataTab) load(page int) tea.Cmd {
	t.loading = true
	id, b, conn, ref, size := t.id, t.backend, t.conn, t.ref, t.pageSize
	orderBy, known := t.orderBy, t.orderKnown
	return func() tea.Msg {
		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), queryTimeout)
		defer cancel()
		msg := pageLoadedMsg{tabID: id, conn: conn, page: page, orderBy: orderBy}
		msg.err = withConn(ctx, b, conn, ref.Database, func(c db.Conn) error {
			if !known {
				cols, err := c.Meta().Columns(ctx, ref)
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}
				msg.orderBy = primaryKey(cols)
			}
			var err error
			msg.rs, msg.hasMore, err = fetchPage(ctx, c, ref, msg.orderBy, page, size)
			return err
		})
		return msg
	}
}

func (t *structureTab) load() tea.Cmd {
	t.loading = true
	id, b, conn, ref := t.id, t.backend, t.conn, t.ref
	return func() tea.Msg {
		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), loadTimeout)
		defer cancel()
		msg := structureLoadedMsg{tabID: id, conn: conn}
		msg.err = withConn(ctx, b, conn, ref.Database, func(c db.Conn) error {
			var err error
			msg.data, err = fetchStructure(ctx, c.Meta(), ref)
			return err
		})
		return msg
	}
}
```

```go
func (m *Model) noteConn(conn string, err error) {
	switch {
	case conn == "":
	case db.IsConnLost(err):
		m.tree.SetBroken(conn, true)
		m.setStatus("connection "+conn+" lost: select it in the tree and press enter to retry", true)
	case err == nil:
		m.tree.SetBroken(conn, false)
	}
}
```

```go
		}
```

`internal/ui/app.go`：

```go
type connAddedMsg struct {
	conn config.Connection
	err  error
}

func addConnection(b Backend, msg overlay.ConnFormMsg) tea.Cmd {
	return func() tea.Msg {
		err := b.AddConnection(msg.Conn, msg.Password, msg.Save)
		return connAddedMsg{conn: msg.Conn, err: err}
	}
}

func (m *Model) connAdded(msg connAddedMsg) {
	if msg.err != nil {
		m.setStatus("cannot add connection: "+msg.err.Error(), true)
		return
	}
	c := msg.conn
	m.tree.AddRoot(&tree.Node{Kind: tree.KindConn, Name: c.Name, Conn: c.Name, Prod: c.Env == config.EnvProd})
	m.setFocus(focusTree)
	m.setStatus("added connection "+c.Name+" to connections.toml", false)
}
```

```go
	case connAddedMsg:
```

`cmd/sqlvim/main.go`：删除 `len(cfg.Connections) == 0` 的错误返回；用 `newManager` 创建会话管理器：

```go

```

```go
// newManager builds the session manager; connections added in the UI are
// appended to dir/connections.toml.
func newManager(cfg *config.Config, dir string) *session.Manager {
	mgr := session.NewManager(cfg.Connections, secret.Keyring{}, mysql.Driver{}, postgres.Driver{})
	mgr.Persist = func(c config.Connection) error {
		_, err := config.AppendConnection(dir, c)
		return err
	}
	return mgr
}
```

```markdown
| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| `j` `k` `h` `l`, `gg` `G`, `Ctrl-d` `Ctrl-u` | Move |
| `l` / `Enter` | Expand node / open table (retry a disconnected connection) |
| `h` | Collapse / go to parent |
| `Tab`, `Ctrl-h` `Ctrl-l` | Switch pane |
| `1`–`9` | Go to tab |
| `/` `n` `N` | Search in the tree or a result grid |
| `[` `]` | Previous / next page (data), section (structure); `]` loads more console rows |
| `zh` `zl` | Scroll a grid sideways |
| `K` | Show full cell value |
| `yl` / `yy` | Copy cell / row |
| `p` | Re-enter connection password (tree) |
| `<Space>c` | SQL console for the selected connection / database |
| `i` `a` / `Esc` | Console: insert mode / back to normal mode |
| `Enter` / `R` | Console: run statement under cursor / run all (`Ctrl-Enter` or `Ctrl-j` in insert mode) |
| `Ctrl-c` | Cancel the running query (never quits) |
| `Ctrl-j` / `Ctrl-k` | Console: results / editor; `M` toggles the message log |
| `<Space>v` | Edit the console in `$EDITOR` |
| `<Space>h` | SQL history |
| `<Space>y t/c/j/m/i` | Copy result as TSV / CSV / JSON / Markdown / INSERT |
| `<Space>x c/j/s/x` | Export result as CSV / JSON / SQL / xlsx |
| `<Space>n` | New connection |
| `<Space>s` | Table structure |
| `<Space>b` | Toggle tree |
| `<Space>r` | Refresh |
| `?` | Help for the focused pane |
| `:q` / `:qa` | Close tab / quit |
```

在 Keys 表格后加一节：

```markdown
## Safety

- `readonly = true` (the default for `env = "prod"`) blocks INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE and DDL
  in the console, and the database session itself is read-only as a second line of defence.
- `UPDATE` / `DELETE` without `WHERE` asks you to type the table name before it runs.
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 示例与 CHANGELOG**

`docs/examples/config.toml`：

```toml
# Copy to ~/.config/sqlvim/config.toml. Every key is optional.
page_size        = 200            # rows per page in table data tabs
max_column_width = 40             # widest a grid column gets
max_rows         = 1000           # rows a console query fetches (] loads more)
editor           = "nvim"         # <space>v; default: $VISUAL, then $EDITOR, then nvim
export_dir       = "~/Downloads"  # default directory for <space>x exports
```

`docs/examples/ghostty.conf` 追加：

```
keybind = cmd+shift+f10=text:\x20c
keybind = cmd+shift+c=text:\x20yc
```

`CHANGELOG.md`：

```markdown
# Changelog

All notable changes to this project are documented here. The format follows
[Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/) and the project uses
[Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/).

## [0.2.0] - 2026-10-13

### Added
- SQL console (`<space>c`) with statement splitting, run statement under cursor or all,
  async execution and `Ctrl-c` cancellation on the server.
- Editing the console in `$EDITOR` (`<space>v`).
- Copy results as TSV/CSV/JSON/Markdown/INSERT and export them as CSV/JSON/SQL/xlsx,
  including streaming whole-table exports.
- SQL history (`<space>h`) and console drafts that survive restarts.
- Read-only connections enforced in the app and on the database session; confirmation
  for UPDATE/DELETE without WHERE.
- New connection form (`<space>n`); sqlvim starts with an empty config.
- Search (`/`, `n`, `N`) in the tree and grids; `zh`/`zl` sideways scrolling.
- Automatic reconnect for lost connections.

### Changed
- `Ctrl-c` cancels the running query instead of quitting; use `:qa` to quit.

## [0.1.0] - 2026-10-13

### Added
- Connection tree, paged table data, structure view, help and which-key hints.
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 手动冒烟测试**

```bash
orb start
docker run -d --name sqlvim-mysql -e MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD=secret -e MYSQL_DATABASE=app -p 3306:3306 mysql:8.4
docker run -d --name sqlvim-pg -e POSTGRES_PASSWORD=secret -e POSTGRES_DB=app -p 5432:5432 postgres:16-alpine
sleep 20
docker exec sqlvim-mysql mysql -uroot -psecret app -e "CREATE TABLE users (id INT PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(50), bio TEXT); INSERT INTO users VALUES (1,'alice','{\"a\":1}'),(2,'李雷',NULL);"
docker exec sqlvim-pg psql -U postgres -d app -c "CREATE TABLE users (id int PRIMARY KEY, name text); INSERT INTO users SELECT g, 'user' || g FROM generate_series(1, 5000) g;"
mkdir -p ~/.config/sqlvim && cp docs/examples/connections.toml ~/.config/sqlvim/
make run
```

在 Ghostty 中逐项确认（每项都要实际操作一遍）：

1. `local-mysql` → `app` 上按 `<Space>c`，`i` 输入 `select * from users; select 1;`，`Esc` 后光标放在第一条上按 `Enter`：结果只有 `users` 两行；`M` 打开消息日志看到一行 `✓`。
2. `R` 执行全部：表格显示 `select 1` 的结果，消息日志有两行。
3. 输入 `select sleep(30);` 执行，状态行转圈计时；`Ctrl-c` 在 1 秒内取消；随后 `select @@session.transaction_read_only;` 等会话语句仍可用（会话没断）。
4. `update users set name = 'x';` → 弹出确认框，必须输入 `users` 才执行；`Esc` 取消后数据不变。
5. 在 `local-pg` → `app` → `public` 打开控制台执行 `select * from users;`：显示 1000 行并提示 `truncated · ] more`；`Ctrl-j` 后按 `]`，加载到 2000 行。
6. 把 `local-pg` 在 `connections.toml` 中设为 `readonly = true`，重启：`delete from users where id = 1;` 被拒绝，状态行提示 read-only；`set default_transaction_read_only = off; delete from users where id = 1;` 也被应用层拒绝。
7. 在结果上 `<Space>yc`，粘贴到 Numbers 是表格；`<Space>yt` 粘贴到 Excel/Numbers 分列正确；`yy` 复制一行。
8. 在 `users` 数据 tab 上 `<Space>xx` → 选 `Whole table` → 回车，`~/Downloads/users-*.xlsx` 有 5000 行；再导出到同一路径时提示失败、不覆盖。
9. `<Space>v` 打开 nvim，改 SQL 后 `:wq` 回到 sqlvim，内容已更新；`:cq` 退出时内容不变。
10. 写一半的 SQL 不执行，`:qa` 退出再启动，打开同一控制台，草稿还在；`<Space>h` 能看到前面执行过的语句，回车插入到控制台。
11. `/` 在树中搜索 `users`、在表格中搜索 `李`，`n`/`N` 跳转；`zh`/`zl` 横向滚动。
12. `docker restart sqlvim-pg` 之后在 PG 数据 tab 上 `<Space>r`：自动重连并刷新；控制台再次执行时消息日志显示 `reconnected`。`docker stop sqlvim-pg` 后刷新：树中 `local-pg` 标红 `disconnected`，`docker start` 后在它上面按 `Enter` 恢复。
13. 临时把 `~/.config/sqlvim/connections.toml` 改名后启动：树中提示 `<space>n add one`；`<Space>n` 填表添加 `local-mysql`，文件被重新创建且权限为 `600`，密码进入 Keychain。
14. `Ctrl-c` 在没有查询运行时不退出；`:qa` 退出，终端恢复正常。

清理：

```bash
docker rm -f sqlvim-mysql sqlvim-pg
```
